Intshayelelo

'Bhavishya Malika' yingqokelela yamakhulu emibhalo eyabhalwa malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo yiPanchasakhas (abahlobo abahlanu/abancedisi) beNkosi uShri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Le mibhalo yahlala ifihliwe kangangeenkulungwane, kwaye ngelo xesha, abantu abaninzi babengayazi. Nangona kunjalo, inkanyamba enkulu eyabetha i-Odisha ngo-1999 yatsala ingqalelo yabantu abaninzi kwi-'Bhavishya Malika' kuba kwathiwa iziprofeto ezingale nkanyamba zazisele zenziwe kule mibhalo.

Nangona kunjalo, ngenxa yokuba ibhalwe ngolwimi lwesi-Odia, iBhavishya Malika yahlala ibekelwe umda kwi-Odisha ngelo xesha. Akukho mntu ngaphandle kwe-Odisha owayenolwazi ngale mibhalo. Kodwa ngokuthe ngcembe, amaxesha atshintsha. Ngonyaka ka-2020, ubhubhane we-COVID-19 waphinda watsala ingqalelo yabantu kwiBhavishya Malika. Kwakungemva kwalo bhubhane kuphela apho ukukhuthazwa nokusasazwa kwemibhalo yeMalika kwaqala ngaphandle kwe-Odisha, kwaye le nkqubo iqhubekile ukusukela ngoko.

Ngenxa yoku, abantu abaninzi abangenakubalwa baqalise ukufunda ngeBhavishya Malika kule minyaka imbalwa idlulileyo. Nangona kunjalo, njengoko kusoloko kusenzeka, kunye nolu papasho luhle, ukutolikwa okungachanekanga kunye nolwazi olungachanekanga malunga nezinye iivesi zeMalika nazo zaqala ukusasazeka. Ngokomzekelo, abanye abantu baqalisa ukusasaza amarhe okuba ixesha leKaliyuga yiminyaka eyi-10,000; ukuba igama lokumkani wokugqibela wasePuri liza kuba nguMukunda Dev; ukuba iziganeko eziphambili zeMalika kunye nemisebenzi engcwele yokutshabalalisa (Sanhaar-Leela) yeNkosi uKalki ziya kwenzeka ngexesha elilandelayo leMeena-Shani Yoga (ixesha eliphakathi kuka-2054-57); ukuba ummandla weKhandagiri awukho eBhubaneswar kodwa ukwindawo ethile; nokuba kusekho iminyaka emininzi eseleyo ngaphambi kokuqala kweSatyayuga, njl.njl.

Olu lwazi lungachanekanga lwaqala ukudala amathandabuzo amaninzi ezingqondweni zabazinikeleyo. Ngenxa yokungabikho kwesisombululo esifanelekileyo kula mathandabuzo, ukholo lwabo kwiMalika lwaqala ukuncipha. Injongo ephambili yeli nqaku ayikokubonelela abafundi abatsha ngolwazi oluchanekileyo nolunembileyo ngeBhavishya Malika kuphela kodwa nokusombulula amathandabuzo avelayo ezingqondweni zabazinikeleyo. Kukhangelelwe ukuba ngokufumana ulwazi oluchanekileyo malunga nezi zinto zisisiseko, abathembekileyo baya kukwazi ukuqinisa ngakumbi ukholo lwabo kwiMalika.

1 Bhavishya Malika – Intshayelelo Emfutshane

1.1 Intshayelelo

Phakathi kwenkulungwane ye-15 neye-16, iingcwele ezintlanu ezinkulu ezazizalelwe eOdisha (uShishu Ananta Das, uYashobanta Das, uAchyutananda Das, uBalarama Das, noJagannatha Das) zabhala amakhulu eencwadi ezinjalo apho kwabhalwa iziprofeto ezingenakubalwa. Ezi ncwadi zaziwa ngokuba yi-'Bhavishya Malika'. USri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, owazalwa ngo-1486 kwaye ethathwa njengokuzalwa kabini kukaBhagawan Sri Krishna noJaganmata Radharani, wayenobudlelwane obunzulu nezi ngcwele zintlanu. USri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu wazibiza ezi zintlanu ngokuba 'ngamaPanchasakha' (Abahlobo abaHlanu). Aba Panchasakha babhala iincwadi zeMalika ngomyalelo kaMahaprabhu ngokwakhe. Ngaphandle kwamaPanchasakha, ezinye iingcwele ezinkulu nazo zaqhubela phambili isiko lokubhala iMalika. Ngokomzekelo, umkhonzi uSalabega owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-17, uMhlekazi uArkhit Das noMhlekazi uHadidas abazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-18, uMhlekazi uBhima Bhoi wenkulungwane ye-19, uThakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa wenkulungwane ye-20, njl.njl.

Iziprofeto ezineenkcukacha zabhalwa kwezi ncwadi zeMalika kwimixholo efana neminyaka yeKaliyuga, ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki, ukutshatyalaliswa kwabangendawo, ukukhuselwa kwabakhonzi abanyanisekileyo, ukusekwa kweDharma, kunye nokufika kweSatyayuga.

1.2 Babengobani abaPanchasakha?

Omnye wamaPanchasakha, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das, wabhala kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Intsingiselo:
UBalarama Das, uYashobanta Das, uShishu Ananta Das, uAchyutananda Das, noJagannatha Das baba nodumo njengamaPanchasakha.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba imbali yamaPanchasakha indala kakhulu. Ekupheleni kwexesha ngalinye (Yuga), amaPanchasakha ehlela eMhlabeni, ezalwe kwinxalenye yeNkosi, ukunika igalelo lawo elixabisekileyo kumsebenzi weNkosi wokuseka kwakhona iDharma.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Udaya Kahani’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Intsingiselo:
Kule vesi, uMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das uthi: "Thina, abahlobo abahlanu, sizalwa kwimihlaba emihlanu eyahlukeneyo ngomyalelo weNkosi uKrishna. Kwixesha ngalinye, siyazalwa kwaye sicula izenzo zobuthixo (Leelas) zikaKrishna ngemilomo yethu."

1.3 Ukubonakala kwamaPanchasakha kuwo onke amaXesha amane

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Udaya Kahani’, uchaza iimo ezathathwa ziingcwele ezintlanu ezinkulu kwiiYuga nganye kwezine:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Intsingiselo:

Oko kukuthi, kumaxesha amandulo, ekupheleni kweAnanta Yuga xa iSatyayuga yaqala, iNkosi ePhakamileyo yazalwa ngohlobo lweBrahmin ukutshabalalisa iidemon. Ngelo xesha, iingcwele ezintlanu zabonakala emzimbeni weNkosi ngokwayo njengamaSage: uKripajala, uGarga (Gagarba), uMarkandeya, uSwayambhu, noNarada.

Emva kweSatyayuga, kwiTretayuga, xa iNkosi yenza izenzo zobuthixo ezingenakubalwa ekuzalweni kwayo njengoRama, aba bahlobo bahlanu bahla eMhlabeni ngeendlela zikaNala, uNila, uSushena, uJambavan (Jambeba), noHanuman.

Ekupheleni kweTretayuga, kwafika iDwaparyuga, kwaye iNkosi yazalwa ngohlobo lwenkwenkwe yomalusi, uShri Krishna. Ngeli xesha, uSubala, uSubahu, uSudama, uDama, noShribatsa baba ngamaPanchasakha kwaye bathatha inxaxheba kwizenzo zobuthixo kunye noShri Krishna.

Ngaphezu koko, kwiKaliyuga, xa iNkosi yabonakala ekuzalweni kwayo njengoChaitanya, aba bahlobo bahlanu bazalwa ukuze basasaze igama elingcwele (Hari-naam), beseka indlela elula nelula yosindiso kubo bonke. KwiKaliyuga, baba nodumo njengoShishu Ananta Das, uAchyutananda Das, uJagannatha Das, uYashobanta Das, noBalarama Das.

1.4 Umlando oneenkcukacha wamaPanchasakha kuwo onke amaXesha

Xa umfundi uRamadas wabuza ngamagama athile amaPanchasakha kuwo onke amaXesha amane, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wanika le ngxelo ineenkcukacha kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Udaya Kahani’.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Ukuguqulelwa kweZazisi:
  • UAchyutananda Das: WayenguSage Kripajala kwiSatyayuga, uNala kwiTretayuga, noSudama kwiDwaparayuga.
  • UYashobanta Das: WayenguSage Garga kwiSatyayuga, uJambavan kwiTretayuga, noSubahu kwiDwaparayuga.
  • UShishu Ananta Das: WayenguSage Swayambhu kwiSatyayuga, uSushena kwiTretayuga, noShribatsa kwiDwaparayuga.
  • UBalarama Das: WayenguSage Narada kwiSatyayuga, uNila kwiTretayuga, noDama kwiDwaparayuga.
  • UJagannatha Das: WayenguSage Markandeya kwiSatyayuga, uHanuman kwiTretayuga, noSubala kwiDwaparayuga.

1.5 Amandla Akhethekileyo noBuchule bamaPanchasakha

AmaPanchasakha ayengcwele anolwazi olupheleleyo, azifezekisileyo, kwaye aqonda uThixo. BabengooYogi abaphakamileyo, abafuni, nabakhonzi abanyanisekileyo, ngamnye egqwesa kwintsimi eyahlukileyo yesayensi yokomoya. UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uchaza oku kwiMalika yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Intsingiselo:
Phakathi kwamaPanchasakha azalelwe e-Odisha:
  • U-Yashobanta Das wayengumazi weemfihlelo ezinzulu zexesha elidlulileyo nelizayo (Agam-Nigam).
  • U-Shishu Ananta Das wayengumqeqeshi kwisayensi yemifanekiso engaqondakaliyo kunye noomatshini (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • U-Achyutananda Das wayengumboni wexesha elidlulileyo, langoku, nelizayo (Trikal-Darshi).
  • U-Balarama Das wayengumfundi onzulu wenyaniso yokugqibela kunye nefilosofi (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • U-Jagannatha Das wayengenakulinganiswa kwimvakalelo yokuzinikela okumsulwa (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Iziseko (ii-Ashram) zamaPanchasakha

AmaPanchasakha aseka iiPeetha zawo kunye neendawo zokuzihlukanisa kwiindawo ezintlanu ezahlukeneyo kulo lonke elase-Odisha. U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uchaza oku kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Intsingiselo:
  • U-Achyutananda Das waseka isihlalo sakhe eNemalo (isithili saseCuttack).
  • U-Yashobanta Das waseka iPeetha yakhe e-Adhanga (isithili saseJagatsinghpur).
  • U-Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) waseka indawo yakhe yokuhlala yoonongendi eBalipatna (isithili saseKhurda).
  • U-Jagannatha Das waseka i-Ashram yakhe eSata Lahadi.
  • U-Balarama Das (Balabanta) wenza iShrikhetra (Puri) indawo yakhe yokuhlala.

1.7 AmaPanchasakha – Ukuzalwa Kwakhona Okungcwele kweNkosi

AmaPanchasakha akhankanye izihlandlo ezininzi kwiincwadi zawo zeMalika ukuba angukuzalwa kwakhona okuyinxalenye (Anshavatar) kweNkosi Ephakamileyo ngokwayo. U-Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe ethi 'Chumbak Malika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Intsingiselo:
E-Kaliyuga, u-Achyutananda Das ngumhlobo uSudama wase-Dwaparayuga kwaye wazalwa esinqeni (Kati) seNkosi. U-Yashobanta Das wazalwa emlonyeni ongcwele (Shri-mukh) kaMahaprabhu. U-Shishu Ananta Das wabonakala entliziyweni (Hriday) yeNkosi, kwaye u-Balarama Das wazalwa ezingalweni (Bahu) zeNkosi. Ngaphezu koko, u-Jagannatha Das wehla ekuhlekeni (Haasya) kukaMama uRadharani.

Akunjalo nje u-Shishu Ananta Das ji, kodwa u-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das naye ubhale ngoku kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Chakada Madana'. U-Mahapurush ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kumzimba wokukhanya weNkosi Engenamzimba, thina baPanchasakha bahlanu sehla, kwaye sazala kulo mhlaba phantsi kwamagama ka-Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, noBalarama.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yeMalika, 'Barnatika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ukuzalwa kwamaPanchasakha kwenzeka kwiindawo ezintlanu zomzimba weNkosi uShri Krishna.

1.8 Iziprofeto ngeMyalelo kaThixo

Iivesi zeMalika zithathwa njengamazwi kaMahaprabhu ngokwakhe. Omnye wamaPanchasakha, u-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Intsingiselo:
U-Mahapurush uthi: "Nantoni na endiyibhalayo, la asingo amazwi am. Zonke ezi zinto zimiselwe yiNkosi kwasekuqaleni."

U-Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das naye waxelela umfundi wakhe ku ‘Patta Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Intsingiselo:
"O mfundi Baranga, mamela! Ezi asizizo ezam, kodwa ngamazwi eNkosi uShri Hari."

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhale kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Anakara Samhita':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Intsingiselo:
U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uthi iincwadi azibhalileyo ziquka iiSamhita ezingama-36, iiGita ezingama-78, iiBanshanucharitra ezingama-27, iiUpabanshanucharitra ezingama-24, iincwadi zeMalika ezili-100, kunye namakhulu amawaka eevasi (padas). U-Mahapurush ukwathe zonke ezi ncwadi zibhalwe bubukhazikhazi okanye ubabalo lweNkosi uShri Krishna.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhale kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Intsingiselo:
Kule sloka, u-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uthi ingqondo yakhe ayibambelele kuyo nayiphi na into ngaphandle kweenyawo zeNkosi Ephakamileyo. Ingqondo yakhe ihlala ifunxwe kule Nilachala Dhama, Puri, oko kukuthi, ekuzinikeleni okuthandayo kwiNkosi. Uphinda athi nantoni na ayibhalayo kwiincwadi zeMalika, onke ngamazwi athethwe yiNkosi; akabhalanga nto ngokuthanda kwakhe.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das waphinda wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Parardha Jataka':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kanye njengoko iNkosi Ephakamileyo yayalela kwaye yayalela, ndiyibhalile incwadi yeMalika ngaloo ndlela kanye.

U-Achyutanandaji ubhale kwi 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika' yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngomyalelo weNkosi Ephakamileyo, ngesikhokelo Sakhe, ndibhala iMalika. Nantoni na ephume kwiNkosi, ndiyichaza ezo sloka kwiMalika Purana.

1.9 IMalika yiNcwadi eyiMfihlelo kwaye Iziprofeto Zayo Zifihliwe

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Intsingiselo:
Nangona iiYogi ezinkulu kunye nabahlakaniphi abanakuyifumana imfihlelo yezi zinto zifihliweyo ngeYoga, ingasathethwa ke ngabanye abantu.

U-Achyutananda Das uphinda abhale malunga nobume obufihliweyo bencwadi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Intsingiselo:
Iziprofeto zibhalwe ngokusekelwe kwizibalo zamanani, kwaye la manani afihliwe ngokuguqula. Iziganeko ziya kwenzeka kodwa hayi ngokweminyaka yekhalenda eqhelekileyo; zilandela izibalo eziyimfihlelo ezingumfuziselo.

Ngokuphathelele ukuzalwa kukaKalki, ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Intsingiselo:
Ukuzalwa kweNkosi kuya kuba yimfihlelo. Amaxesha neminyaka ziya kuqhubeka ngendlela efihliweyo. Kuphela ngabazinikeleyo abafihliweyo abaya kukwazi oku kuba amanani afihliwe.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Chakada Madana':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, la ngamazwi angafihliyo nje, kodwa angafihliyo kakhulu awabhalwe nguAchyutananda Das ji ngokukhumbula iNkosi nangokucamngca ngeenyawo zaYo ezingathi lilotus.

Kwenye incwadi, uMahapurush uthi kumfundi wakhe uRamadasa:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalwa, ukubonakala kwaYo okungcwele kuya kwenzeka, kwaye thina maPanchasakhas siya kufumana ithuba elihle lokukhonza nokuhlala sizinikele ezinyaweni zaYo. Kodwa le midlalo ingcwele ayiyi kwenzeka ngokufihlakeleyo nje, kodwa ngendlela efihlakele kakhulu.

UMahapurush ubhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, O mfundi Ramachandra, amazwi eMalika angafihliyo nje, kodwa angafihliyo kakhulu. KwanabaPrajapati Brahma neNkosi uShankar abayi kukwazi ukuqonda iimfihlelo zawo, ingasathethwa ke ngeNkosi uIndra neNkosi uChandra.

USant Bhima Bhoi naye ubhale ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Stuti Chintamani':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, amadoda nabafazi abangenakubalwa ehlabathini abayi kukwazi ukufumana ulwazi malunga nokulandelelana kwamaxesha, ukuphela kweKali Yuga, iMalika, neziprofeto zayo. Kuphela abo iNkosi ibapha ubabalo lwaYo, ababengabazinikeleyo beNkosi kumaxesha adlulileyo, abaya kukwazi ukwazi ngezi ziprofeto zinzulu kakhulu.

Ngoku umbuzo uvela, ngoobani abo bazinikeleyo beNkosi abavela kumaxesha adlulileyo abaya kuthi iNkosi ibaphe ubabalo lwaYo kwaye baya kukholelwa kumazwi eMalika, bakwazi ukwazi ngayo, kwaye babe nolwazi oluchanekileyo malunga nokuphela kweKali Yuga kunye neKalki avatar!

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wanika impendulo koku kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. UMahapurush ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, umntu omnye kuphela kwizigidi ezilishumi uya kukwazi ukwazi ngezi ziprofeto, kwaye inani elipheleleyo labantu abanjalo kwihlabathi lonke liya kuba ngama-3,000 kuphela.

1.10 Iziprofeto zeMalika aziyi Kuphazamiseka

IBhavishya Malika iquka amazwi eNkosi ngokwaYo, ngoko akukho mbuzo wokuba angasilela. Kwincwadi yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Intsingiselo:
"Umhlaba ungahlukana kubini, ilanga eliphuma empumalanga lingaphuma entshona, ilotus lingadubula encotsheni yentaba—kodwa amazwi kaAchyutananda akanakuze abe bubuxoki. Afana nomgca okrolwe elityeni, kwaye akukho namnye onamandla okuwabonisa ukuba ayingo."

Kwi-‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Intsingiselo:
KwiKaliyuga, abantu bangade babize uMama uSita njengonganyanisekanga, kwaye ilotus lingadubula encotsheni yentaba, kodwa amazwi kaAchyutananda Das akasoze aphazamiseke, kuba afana nomgca okrolwe elityeni.

1.11 Kwi-‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, ecaphula amazwi eNkosi engenamzimba (Nirakar), ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Intsingiselo:
Kule vesi, iNkosi engenamzimba ngokwaYo ithi: "Ukuba amazwi eMalika akazaliseki, ngoko umlomo wam ongcwele uya kuboniswa ukuba bubuxoki." Kuba la ngamazwi avela emlonyeni weNkosi ngokwaYo, akanakuze asilele.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona kwi-'Chakada Madana':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ilanga lingaphuma entshona endaweni yempumalanga, ilotus lingadubula encotsheni yentaba, kodwa amazwi kaAchyutananda akanakuguqulwa. Amazwi kaAchyutananda akrolwe elityeni; ayinyaniso epheleleyo.

Ukuzalwa kwakhona kukaAchyutananda Das ji, uSant Hadi Das, owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-18 kufutshane neChatia kwisithili saseJajpur eOdisha, naye waqulunqa iincwadi ezininzi zeMalika.

Kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika 'Kali Chautisha', wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, izinto ezibhalwe kwiincwadi zeMalika aziyi kuguqulwa – ziya kwenzeka ngenye imini. Bonke abazinikeleyo balindile ngokholo ezi ziganeko.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Intsingiselo:
Ubhala ukuba ndichaza ngokuphindaphindiweyo kubazinikeleyo ngomonde. Abazinikeleyo akufuneki bathathe la mazwi njengobuxoki, kuba uAchyuta uziqulunqe ezi ncwadi ekwimeko yokucamngca. Izono neentsizi zeKali Yuga ziya kutshatyalaliswa, kwaye imidlalo engcwele yeNkosi uKalki iqinisekile. UMahapurush ubhala ngakumbi ukuba amazwi akhe akasoze aguqulwe.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwanento engenakwenzeka njengokulahlekelwa bubomi bomntu endaweni yokufumana ukungafi ngokusela incindi (Amrit) ingenzeka, kodwa amazwi kaAchyutananda Das akanakuze abe yimpazamo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwi-'Udaya Kahani' yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, amazwi kaAchyutananda Das akasoze abe yimpazamo. Nabani na obiza la mazwi bubuxoki uya kuba ngumthathi-nxaxheba weso siphoso.

UMahapurush ubhala kwi-'Chakada Madana':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, amazwi am akasoze abe ngenye indlela, nokuba ilotus lingadubula entabeni! La mazwi abhalwe ekwimeko yokucamngca phantsi komthi weNemal banyan (Nemal Vata).

Kwincwadi yeMalika 'Chakada Madana' ngokwaYo, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, umlilo oqhambuka emva kokuba umhlaba uhlukene ungazisa intshabalalo (Pralaya) kuwo wonke umhlaba, kodwa amazwi am akanakuze abe bubuxoki. Amazwi am awanakuchaswa kwaye awanakuguqulwa.

2 Injongo yeMalika – Usindiso lwabazinikeleyo

Rhoqo, abantu abaninzi bayoyika emva kokufunda iziprofeto zeMalika kwaye bazichithe njenge "zoyikiso." Nangona kunjalo, le ngcinga ayichanekanga ngokupheleleyo. Injongo ephambili kukukhuselwa ngokomoya nokukhokelwa kwabazinikeleyo ngexesha lotshintsho lwamaxesha.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Intsingiselo:
Ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, ukuvusa ingqondo elele yabazinikeleyo, uqulunqa ezi ncwadi zeMalika. Injongo kukuba kwiKaliyuga enzulu, xa ubumnyama bokungazi bukhona kuyo yonke indawo, abazinikeleyo beNkosi banokufikelela kuMahaprabhu Kalki ngomxholo weMalika, okuvumela ukumanyana komzinikeleyo noThixo. Ngesi sizathu, ubhala iziprofeto ezinkulu kwiincwadi zeMalika ukusuka kwiKaliyuga ukuya kwiSangam Yuga (Ixesha loTshintsho) nangakumbi kwiAnanta Yuga ezayo (Adi Satyayuga).

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Intsingiselo:
Injongo yeziprofeto ezibhalwe ndim kwiincwadi zeMalika kukulumkisa ihlabathi. Ucacisa ukuba ezi ziprofeto zibhalelwe intlalo-ntle yehlabathi.

UMahapurush ubhala kwakhona kwi-‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Intsingiselo:
Ngenxa ye-'vasana' edlulileyo (iimbonakalo), oko kukuthi, ukuzinikela kwiNkosi okuvela ekuzalweni kwangaphambili, ulwazi lwandivela kolu kuzalwa, kwaye emva koko ndaqulunqa iincwadi zeMalika ukuze kungenelwe kwaye kube nentlalo-ntle abazinikeleyo.

UMahapurush ubhala kwakhona kwi-‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Amazwi emibhalo yeMalika yenzelwe ukulumkisa; yazi ukuba akwimo yezilumkiso.

UMahapurush wabhala ngakumbi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Iivesi zeMalika zikwimo yezilumkiso kubantu. Zibhalelwe intlalo-ntle yabazinikeleyo ekupheleni kweKaliyuga. Ngoko ke, kufuneka kucace kuthi ukuba iMalika ayisasazi uloyiko, kodwa ivula indlela yosindiso. Injongo yodwa yeBhavishya Malika yintlalo-ntle yehlabathi.

2.1 IMalika ayiyiyo yabo bonke abantu

Kufuneka sazi kakuhle ukuba iMalika ayiyiyo yomntu wonke. IiPanchasakha Mahapurushas zabangela abafundi bazo kwimibhalo yeMalika ukuba bangasasazi la mazwi phambi kwezidenge. UMahapurush wathi ezi azizizo zabo bonke abantu; akukho bonke abantu abaya kukwazi ukuziqonda. Kuphela ngabazinikeleyo beNkosi abaya kukwazi ukukholelwa kuzo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Intsingiselo:
Ndiqulunqe imibhalo eyi-1,85,000 iyonke kwaye ngaphandle koko, ndabhala iziprofeto ezininzi ezizayo. Ndipapasha konke oku ukuze kungenelwe abazinikeleyo.

U-Achyutananda Das uze athi kumfundi wakhe uRamadas kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Intsingiselo:
Mamela, mfundi Ramadas, la ngamazwi ayimfihlo. Musa ukuwaxela phambi kwezidenge, kungenjalo uya kwenza isono. Le mibhalo ikwimo yezilumkiso kubantu eya kuba njengencindi kubazinikeleyo ekupheleni kweKaliyuga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Intsingiselo:
Ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, ngexesha lokusekwa kweDharma, kuya kubakho imfazwe eyoyikekayo, kwaye kuya kubakho isiphithiphithi eMhlabeni. UMhlaba uya kungcangcazela. Kuya kubakho ukuphazamiseka okuninzi emoyeni. Kodwa ngaphandle kwabazinikeleyo, akukho mntu uya kukwazi ukuqonda ukuba ezi zezona zenzo zokutshabalalisa zeNkosi.

UMahapurush uthi kumfundi uRamadas kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Intsingiselo:
O mfundi Ramadas, musa ukuwaxela amazwi ale ncwadi ‘Chakada Madana’ phambi kwezidenge. KwiKaliyuga enzulu, izidenge ziya kubahleka, nto leyo eya kukhokelela ekugxekeni kweNkosi.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Intsingiselo:
Kwizidenge, la mazwi eMalika anjengobutyhefu, ngelixa kubalumkileyo anjenge 'Piyush' okanye incindi. Ukuxelela la mazwi kubantu abangenalwazi kufana nokuxuba ubutyhefu nencindi.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona ku ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Intsingiselo:
Mamela mfundi Ramadas, la mazwi am eMalika anjenge 'Ganthidhana' okanye ubutyebi obuxabisekileyo kubazinikeleyo. INkosi iya kuseka iDharma kwaye abazinikeleyo baya kukhululwa. Ezi zenzo ziya kwenzeka ngendlela eyimfihlo kakhulu. Kuphela ngenxa yosindiso lwabazinikeleyo ndiqulunqa le mibhalo yeMalika. Ziya kuba luncedo kubo kwi ‘Barana Bela’ okanye ekupheleni kweKaliyuga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ngakumbi kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Intsingiselo:
Phantsi komthi weBanyan waseNemalo, ndibhala le ncwadi yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’. Iya kuba luncedo kubazinikeleyo ekupheleni kweKaliyuga. Yityhile kuphela phakathi kwabona bazinikeleyo; kungenjalo, kuya kubakho ukugxekwa kwala mazwi nakweNkosi.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona ku ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Intsingiselo:
IChakada Madana bubutyebi kubazinikeleyo. Ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, kuphela ngabazinikeleyo abaya kukwazi ukuqonda izinto zayo kunye neemfihlelo zayo. Musa ukuyityhila phambi kwezidenge.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush uqulunqe imiqulu eyi-318 yeziprofeto zeMalika kuphela ngenxa yentlalo-ntle yabazinikeleyo abavukileyo. Izidenge naboni abayi kukwazi ukwazi imfihlelo yale mibhalo.

Wabhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Intsingiselo:
Ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, iNkosi iya kuzalwa. Iya kutshabalalisa abangendawo naboni ukuze ikhusele uMhlaba kwaye iya kuseka kwakhona iSanatana Dharma. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ngelo xesha, ukukhokela abazinikeleyo nokubakhusela, uqulunqa imibhalo yeMalika. Kule vesi, uMahapurush wabhala ngokucacileyo ukuba injongo yokuqulunqwa kweMalika kukukhusela abazinikeleyo ngamaxesha entshabalalo nokuvula indlela yosindiso lwabo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ngakumbi kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Chakada Madana':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iziprofeto endizibhalileyo kwimibhalo yam yeMalika malunga nokuphela kwezono nokusekwa kweDharma ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, zonke zezabazinikeleyo kuba abazinikeleyo banjengabazalwana bam. Abazinikeleyo ngumoya wam wokuphila emihlanu (Pancha-prana), kwaye ndibhale yonke le mibhalo yeMalika kuphela ngenxa yabo.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, mfundi Rama, kukho ii-Parardhas ezingama-772 okanye izahluko kule ncwadi yeMalika egama lingu 'Chakada Madana'. Musa ukuzityhila phambi kwezidenge. Baninzi abantu abangendawo kwiKaliyuga eyoyikekayo; yigcine kude kubo bonke.

Ku 'Chakada Madana' ngokwayo, uMahapurush ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, O mfundi Ramadasa, musa ukuzityhila ezi ziprofeto zeMalika phambi kwezidenge. Baya kuthi la mazwi ngamanga, ukuba ezi ziganeko aziyi kwenzeka. Ngoko ke, baxelele kuphela abazinikeleyo; kuphela bona abaya kukwazi ukukholelwa koku.

3 Iiprofeto zeMalika Ezifezekileyo

IBhavishya Malika iquka amazwi kaMahaprabhu ngokwakhe, kwaye ngoko ke akumangalisi ukuba iziprofeto ezingenakubalwa kwiMalika zifezekile ukuza kuthi ga ngoku. Siza kuxoxa nawe ngezinye zeziprofeto ezinjalo ezibonakalise ukuba ziyinyani nge-100%.

3.1 Isiprofeto Ngokuphathelene no-Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwimibhalo yakhe yeMalika 'Kalikalpa Gita' kunye ne 'Achyuta Thara':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Intsingiselo:
Kwiinkulungwane ezidlulileyo, uMahapurush wabhala ngompolitiki obalulekileyo waseIndiya ezimeleyo. Wachaza ukuba xa iBrahmin ilawula iIndiya, utshintsho oluninzi luya kubonakala ehlabathini. Okokuqala, uya kulawula iintsuku ezili-13, kwaye kamva uya kubamba amandla iminyaka emihlanu. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba igama lalo mntu ulawula eDelhi liya kuba ngu "Atal Bihari Vajpayee". Njengoko imbali ibonisa, uShri Vajpayee wasebenza njengeNkulumbuso iintsuku ezili-13 ngo-1996 kwaye emva koko waba nexesha elipheleleyo leminyaka emi-5 ukusuka ngo-1999 ukuya ku-2004.

3.2 Isiprofeto Ngokuphathelene noMahatma Gandhi

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, kwiminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo kwincwadi yakhe edumileyo 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta', wenza isiprofeto esibanzi ngokuphathelene noMahatma Gandhi.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Intsingiselo:
"Uya kuzalelwa kwiphondo laseGujarat. Igama lakhe liya kuba ngu 'Mohan' kwaye uya kuhlala ecula igama likaRama. 'Ahimsa' (Ukungabikho kobundlobongela) kuya kuba sisixhobo sakhe, kwaye ngenxa yemizamo yakhe, amaBritane (amaFiringis) aya kunyanzelwa ukuba ashiye iIndiya."

Ingcwele Enkulu yabhala ngakumbi ukuba uMohan wayengayi kubamba amandla ngokwakhe, kwaye emva kokufa kwakhe, kuya kubakho ukuhla kweDharma. Wakhankanya ngokukodwa ukuba umntu ogama lingu "Godse" uya kumbulala nge "bullet" (Guli), kwaye ngokwenza oko, uya kuhlwayela imbewu yeKali esizweni. Uye waxela kwangaphambili ukuba abantu abaninzi abayi kuhluleka ukuqonda uGandhi ngokufanelekileyo kwaye baya kuthethelela isenzo sombulali ngenxa yokungazi. Njengoko sisazi, lonke igama lesi siprofeto ngokuphathelele uYise weSizwe lenzeka.

3.3 Isiprofeto seSichotho Esikhulu sika-1999 kunye nokuqhekeka kweKalpavata

Kumbhalo wakhe 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', iNgcwele Enkulu uAchyutananda Das waxela kwangaphambili isichotho sika-1999 kwiminyaka engama-600 ngaphambili.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Intsingiselo:
"Ulwandle luya kunyuka kwaye kuya kubakho umoya onamandla. Isebe leKalpavata engcwele (umkhiwane wakudala ngaphakathi kwendawo yeTempile yaseJagannath) liya kuqhekeka." Ingcwele Enkulu yathi esi siganeko siya kwenzeka ngonyaka "Anesata" (99).

Ngo-Oktobha 29, 1999, iSichotho Esikhulu satsho eOdisha ngemimoya engama-250 km/h. Sabangela intshabalalo enkulu, sathatha ubomi babantu abangaphezu kwe-10,000. Ngokwenene kwisiprofeto, isebe elikhulu lomthi wakudala weKalpavata ngaphakathi kwetempile yasePuri laqhekeka ngexesha lesi siphango.

3.4 Isiprofeto seNkululeko yaseIndiya (1947)

Kumbhalo 'Garuda Kalpa', uNgcwele uAchyutananda waxela kwangaphambili ukuba iIndiya iya kukhululeka kulawulo lwaseBritane ngo-1947 esebenzisa ikhowudi yamanani.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Ukucazululwa kwamanani:
Ingcwele Enkulu yasebenzisa amagama afuziselayo amanani:
  • Brahma: Imelwe ngu-1 (Njengaku "Ekam Brahma").
  • Bhakti: Imelwe ngu-9 (Iifom ezilithoba zeNavadha Bhakti).
  • Veda: Imelwe ngu-4 (IiVeda ezine).
  • Baar: Imelwe ngu-7 (Iintsuku ezisixhenxe zeveki).

Xa zibekwe kunye (1-9-4-7), zenza u-1947. Isiprofeto sithi ngalo nyaka, abaphambukeli (amaBritane) babeya kubaleka kulo mhlaba.

3.5 Isiprofeto Ngokuphathelele Ukubulawa Kweentsana Zazintombi Esibelekweni

Kwi 'Jaiphula Malika', iNgcwele Enkulu yaxela kwangaphambili ububi boluntu besiphelo seKaliyuga:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Intsingiselo:
"Abantu baya kutshabalalisa intombazana (Kanya Ratna) esibelekweni (Garbha) ngokuphindaphindiweyo ngaphandle kwemvakalelo yesono ezintliziyweni zabo." Oku kubonisa ngokuchanekileyo ixesha elimnyama lokubulawa kweentsana zazintombi esibelekweni elihlukumeze uluntu kumashumi eminyaka akutshanje.

3.6 Isiprofeto soBhubhane luka-2020 (COVID-19)

Ingcwele Enkulu uHadidas, ukuzalwa ngokutsha kwePanchasakhas owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-18, wabhala kwi 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Intsingiselo:
"Xa kufika unyaka 'Dui-Binsha' (Amashumi Amabini / 2020), kuya kubakho ukuphazamiseka okukhulu. Abantu baya kufa sisifo esingaziwayo (Ajana Vyadhi) kwaye kuya kubakho izikhalo zokuphelelwa lithemba (Haahakaar) kuyo yonke indawo." Oku yinkcazo echanekileyo ngokumangalisayo yobhubhane we-COVID-19 owabamba ihlabathi ngo-2020.

3.7 Isiprofeto seDama laseHirakud

Kumbhalo 'Chakada Madana' kunye ne 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', Abangcwele uAchyutananda noArkhit Das baxela kwangaphambili ukwakhiwa kwedama elikhulu eOdisha.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
UAchyutananda Das ji kumbhalo wakhe weMalika 'Chakada Madana' kunye nongcwele uBrahma Avadhut uArakhita Das, owazalwa kwisiqingatha sesibini senkulungwane yeshumi elinesibhozo kwaye wenza isadhana egqibeleleyo emqolombeni waseOlashuni, kumbhalo wakhe weMalika 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' wabhala ukuba kuya kwakhiwa idama elikhulu kwiphondo laseOdisha eliya kuthiywa igama elithi "Hirakud". Uye wathi ngexesha lesiphelo seKaliyuga, ngohlaselo lweentshaba, elo dama liya kuqhekeka, apho izithili ezithandathu zephondo laseOdisha ziya kuthi zintywiliselwe, ezothutho ziya kuma, kwaye kuya kubakho ilahleko enkulu yobutyebi nabantu. IiMahapurushas zathi ngelo xesha iGanga iya kubonisa imo yakhe ekhohlakeleyo kwaye nemilambo iBaitarani, iBrahmani, njl. nayo iya kubangela intshabalalo eyoyikekayo.

Njengoko kusaziwa, ngonyaka ka-1948 kwiphondo laseOdisha eIndiya, umsebenzi wokwakha idama elibizwa ngokuba yiHirakud waqala, elagqitywa ngonyaka ka-1953. Ngenyanga kaJanuwari yonyaka ka-1957, yiNkulumbuso yangelo xesha uPandit Jawaharlal Nehru, kwenziwa uvulo olusemthethweni lweli dama. Ngobude obupheleleyo be-26 km, elakhiwe phezu komlambo iMahanadi kwisithili saseSambalpur eOdisha, eli dama namhlanje lelona dama lide elenziwe ngabantu ehlabathini. Ngale ndlela, ukuchaneka kwenxalenye yokuqala yesi siprofeto kwangqinwa.

Kungekudala ihlabathi liya kubona inxalenye yesibini yesi siprofeto isenzeka xa, ngexesha lemfazwe phakathi kweIndiya neTshayina, ngohlaselo lwemijukujelwa lwaseTshayina, eli dama liya kuqhekeka kwaye iphondo laseOdisha liya kubandezeleka sisikhukula esoyikekayo samanzi.

3.8 Isiprofeto sokuZalwa kwePakistan Kwiinkulungwane Ngaphambili

Ingcwele Enkulu uAchyutananda Das nabanye bakhankanya ukudalwa negama lePakistan kwiminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo kwimibhalo efana ne 'Gupta Kheda Malika' kunye ne 'Bhavishyat Malika'. Ngokufanayo, ungcwele wenkulungwane ye-18 uArkhit Das wayikhankanya kwi 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Intsingiselo:
"Ilizwe elibizwa ngokuba yiPakistan liya kubakho ngesiquphe. Ngamaxesha esiphelo, iIndiya iya kujongana nemfazwe ekhohlakeleyo nePakistan. Amagunya aseMpuma Ephakathi naseNtshona aya kuhlanganisa; izizwe zamaSilamsi, amazwe aseNtshona, iTshayina, neMelika ziya kuxhasa iPakistan, ngelixa iRashiya, iJamani, neJapan ziya kunceda iIndiya. IPakistan iya kutshatyalaliswa ngokupheleleyo (Chara-khara) kule mfazwe, kwaye phakathi kokuphelelwa lithemba kwindalo yonke, ekugqibeleni iya kuqonda iimpazamo zayo."

Kufanelekile ukuqaphela ukuba igama elithi 'Pakistan' laqanjwa kuphela ngo-1933, kodwa aba bangcwele balithiya igama kwinkulungwane ezininzi ngaphambili.

3.9 Iziprofeto Ngokuphathelele iSixeko saseBhubaneswar

Isiseko seBhubaneswar yanamhlanje sabekwa ngo-1948 yiNkulumbuso uNehru. Nangona kunjalo, ukukhankanywa kwayo njengekomkhulu kubonakala kwimibhalo yeMalika efana ne 'Chakada Madana' kunye ne 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Abangcwele baxela kwangaphambili ukuba ngoLwesibini ngenyanga kaChaitra, isandi esikhohlakeleyo (ukugquma kukaBhairavi) siya kuviwa eBhubaneswar. Ngexesha leMfazwe Yehlabathi Yesithathu, imikhosi yeentshaba iya kufika eBhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). Imibhalo yakhankanya ngokucacileyo ukuba iBhubaneswar iya kuba likomkhulu kwaye ibe yindawo ekujoliswe kuyo ziintshaba zangaphandle.

3.10 Isiprofeto Ngokuphathelele uMfundisi Wokomoya Odumileyo uShri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

Ingcwele Enkulu uAchyutananda Das, kwimibhalo yakhe yeMalika eyabhalwa malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo, waxela kwangaphambili ukuzalwa kwe "Yugapurusha" (Indoda yeXesha) owayeya kwenza imidlalo emininzi engcwele kwaye udumo lwakhe luya kusasazeka kwihlabathi lonke.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Intsingiselo:
Ingcwele Enkulu yabala ukuba ungcwele ogama lingu "Anukul" uya kuvela, kwaye abazinikeleyo abavela eIndiya nakwamanye amazwe baya kuza kufuna indawo yokusabela kwi "Satsanga" yakhe. Igama elingcwele (Satnaam) liya kuculwa, kwaye kuya kubakho intlanganiso enkulu yabazinikeleyo. UAchyutananda Das uye wakhankanya ukuba yena ngokwakhe, ehlala kwindawo efihlakeleyo/engenanto (Shunya Maarga), uya kungqina umdlalo wobuthixo wale Yugapurusha.

Ukuzalisekisa esi siprofeto, UAnukulchandra Chakravarty wazalwa nge-14 kaSeptemba, 1888, kwisithili sasePabna saseBengal engahlukananga (ngoku iBangladesh). Waziwa kwihlabathi jikelele njengoShri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, waseka i 'Satsang Ashram' eDeoghar, eJharkhand, ngo-1946. Izigidi zabalandeli kwihlabathi jikelele zazimanyana nomsebenzi wakhe, zihlala zimbiza ngokuthi 'Yuga Purushottam.'

3.11 Ukukhankanywa kweBhomu yeAtom kwimibhalo yeMalika

Ngelixa ibhomu yeatom yaphuhliswa phakathi kwenkulungwane yama-20, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das (kwi 'Gupta Kheda Malika' naku 'Bhavishyat Malika') noBalarama Das (kwi 'Kali Tandaba Gita') bayikhankanya kwiinkulungwane ezidlulileyo.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Abangcwele babhala ukuba ngexesha leMfazwe yeHlabathi yesiThathu, iintshaba zaseIndiya ziza kuceba ukutshabalalisa imida yaseIndiya zisebenzisa iiBhomu zeAtom (Paramanu Bomba). Nangona kunjalo, isicwangciso sabo siya kusilela ngenxa yokuba iNkosi uKalki iya kufunxa amandla atshisayo/atshabalalisayo (Dahika Shakti) ezo bhombu. Izixhobo kanye ezithi imikhosi yasentshona neyotshaba iziqhayise ngazo ziza kuba "ngumkhenkce" (zingasebenzi) kwaye zingabi nampembelelo kumhlaba waseIndiya. Ukubona lo msebenzi ungenakwenzeka, iintshaba zangaphandle ziza kubaleka zoyika. Imibhalo ilumkisa ukuba imfazwe eyoyikekayo yenyukliya (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) iya kwenzeka ekugqibeleni, yenza uMhlaba ungahlaleki phantse, ekuyakubonwa ngabazinikeleyo.

3.12 Ukukhankanywa kukaMadan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, noMotilal Nehru

Wazalwa ngo-1850, ingcwele enkulu uBhima Bhoi (umlandeli wesithethe samaPanchasakhas) wanika iinkcukacha ngeenkokeli zentshukumo yenkululeko yaseIndiya kwincwadi yakhe ethi 'Padmakalpa'.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Intsingiselo:
Umbhalo waxela kwangaphambili ukuzalwa komlandeli ogama lingu "Madan Mohan Malaviya" owathi, kunye no "Mahatma Gandhi," uza kukhokela intshukumo esekelwe kwinkolo yokungabi nabundlobongela (Ahimsa). Ukongeza, yakhankanya ukuzalwa komlandeli onempembelelo enkulu nonamandla "Motilal Nehru." Bonke abathathu baba ziinkokeli eziphambili zeIndian National Congress kunye nomzabalazo wenkululeko.

3.13 Isiprofeto sokuBulawa kukaMahatma Gandhi nguNathuram Godse

Kwi 'Jaiphula Malika', uMahapurush Achyutananda Das waxela kwangaphambili ukufa kukaMahatma.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Intsingiselo:
"Ukufa kukaMohan Gandhi kuya kwenzeka ezandleni zikaNathuram." Njengoko imbali ibhala, ngomhla wama-30 kuJanuwari, 1948, uNathuram Vinayak Godse wambulala uMahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Inkululeko yaseIndiya Phantsi kobuNkokeli bukaMahatma Gandhi

Kwi 'Kali Bhagabata', uMahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (ingcwele yenkulungwane yama-20 kwisithethe seMalika) wachaza utshintsho kwezopolitiko.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Intsingiselo:
Ingcwele yaxela kwangaphambili ukuba ngokuhlanganisana kwamaSatyagrahi, amaYurophu aya kunyanzelwa ukuba ahambe. Wachonga u "Mohan" (Gandhi) njengomphathi/inkokeli yalo mzabalazo mkhulu wokuqala wenkululeko. Wachaza ngakumbi uMahatma Gandhi njengomphefumlo omkhulu (Mahatma) kunye nokuzalwa ngokutsha kukaNarada, amagama akhe aya kuhlonitshwa ngabo bonke. Oku kwazaliseka njengoko ubunkokeli bukaGandhi baba ngamandla aphambili akhokelela amaBritane ukuba ahambe kumhlaba waseIndiya.

4 Ixesha leKaliyuga

Ngokwezibhalo zeSanatan, ubudala bonke beKaliyuga yiminyaka engama-432,000. IBhavishya Malika nayo iqinisekisa obu budala bonke, kodwa yongeza isiprofeto esibalulekileyo: ngenxa yezono ezigqithisileyo zoluntu, iKaliyuga ayiyi kugqiba ixesha layo elipheleleyo.

4.1 Umyalelo weZibhalo weminyaka engama-432,000

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwi 'Adi Samhita':

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Intsingiselo:
"KwiKaliyuga, iilakh ezine na mashumi amathathu anesibini amawaka (432,000) eminyaka zimiselwe njengobude bayo bobomi."

Emva koko uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe, 'Dashapatala Malika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ubude bobomi obumiselweyo beKaliyuga ziilakh ezine, amawaka angamashumi amathathu anesibini eminyaka.

Ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Parardha Jataka':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ubude bobomi beKaliyuga ziilakh ezine, amawaka angamashumi amathathu anesibini eminyaka.

Nangona kunjalo, iiMahapurusha azizange ziyeke emva kokuthetha oku nje; bakwathi kwimibhalo yabo yeMalika ukuba ngenxa yezono ezahlukeneyo ezenziwe ngabantu, iKaliyuga ayiyi kukwazi ukugqiba ubude bayo obupheleleyo.

4.2 Kutheni iKaliyuga iza kuphela kwangethuba

AbaPanchasakhas bacacisa ukuba ngelixa ubudala bethiyori buyiminyaka engama-432,000, i "Bhoga" (ubude obuchanekileyo obufunyenweyo) buya kuba bufutshane kakhulu ngenxa yobunzima bezono. Kwi 'Dashapatala Malika', kubhaliwe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Intsingiselo:
"O Nkosi Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Sixelele ukuba iKaliyuga ayiyi kukwazi njani ukufumana ixesha layo elipheleleyo leminyaka engama-432,000."

UMahapurush ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngenxa yomthwalo ogqithisileyo wezono kwiKaliyuga, iKaliyuga ayiyi kukwazi ukufumana ubude bayo bobomi beminyaka engama-4,32,000. Nangaphambi kweli xesha, iNkosi iya kuzalwa ngokutsha, aboni baya kutshatyalaliswa, kwaye iDharma iya kusekwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona kwesinye sezibhalo zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iKaliyuga ayiyi kukwazi ukufumana ixesha layo elimiselweyo leminyaka engama-4,32,000. Ngenxa yezono, ubude bayo bobomi buya kubola, oko kuthetha ukuba, ayiyi kukwazi ukufunyanwa ngokupheleleyo.

4.3 Ukuba ayizizo iminyaka engama-4,32,000, yintoni ke ubude bobomi obufunyenweyo beKaliyuga?

Namhlanje, abanye abantu bazibandakanya ekusasazeni, bekhankanya iMalika, ukuba ubude bobomi obufunyenweyo beKaliyuga yiminyaka eyi-10,000. Makwaziwe ukuba oku akuchanekanga ngokupheleleyo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iKaliyuga iya kuphela kwiminyaka eyi-10,000 – oku yingxelo engahambelaniyo okanye yobuxoki.

IiPanchasakha Mahapurusha zibhale kwiindawo ezininzi kwimibhalo yazo yeMalika ukuba ubude bobomi obufunyenweyo beKaliyuga buya kuba yiminyaka eyi-5,000, emva koko iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalwa ngokutsha kwaye iSatya-Kali okanye iAnanta Yuga okanye iAdya Satya Yuga iya kufika.

Yizani, masifumane ulwazi olungakumbi ngesi sihloko ngasinye ngasinye ngeesloka zeMalika.

Okokuqala, masazi ukuba iNkosi uShri Krishna Yena ngokwakhe wathini ngokuphathelene nobude bobomi beKaliyuga.

4.4 Ubude Obuchanekileyo: 5,000 Iminyaka

Ngokuchasene nolwazi olungachananga olusasazwa ngabanye lokuba iKaliyuga ihlala iminyaka eyi-10,000, imibhalo yeMalika ihlala isithi ulawulo lokwenyani lukaKali luphela emva kweminyaka eyi-5,000, kulandele ixesha lotshintsho.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ucaphula amazwi eNkosi uKrishna kuGaruda kwi-'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Intsingiselo:
"O Garuda onobuqhawe! Mamela, iKaliyuga ayizukuhlala iminyaka eyi-432,000. Ngenxa yesono, ubude bayo buya kuncipha. Iyakuphela kufutshane neminyaka eyi-5,050.

4.5 Umzekeliso kaRavana

IsiOdia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

Kwi-'Kalpatika', u-Achyutananda Das ucacisa le nto esebenzisa umzekelo kaRavana. URavana wayenze intlungu enkulu waza wafumana ubomi beminyaka "engama-56 Ganda" (ixesha elikhulu lendalo yonke). Nangona kunjalo, ngenxa yokuba waqweqwedisa uMama uSita, wathuka iingelosi zasezulwini, waza wenza izono ezimbi njengeBrahman-hatya, wanceda kuphela inxalenye enye yesipho sakhe, ngelixa ezinye iinxalenye ezintathu zatshatyalaliswa zizono zakhe.

Ngokufanayo, ubude beKaliyuga beminyaka eyi-432,000 "ibhangiswa" kukwanda okukhawulezayo kwesono sehlabathi, okukhokelela ekupheleni kwayo kwangethuba emva kweminyaka eyi-5,000 yexesha eliqinisekisiweyo, ivula indlela ye-Ananta Yuga okanye iSatya Yuga.

4.6 Ubudala beKaliyuga: Uhlalutyo Olunzulu

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwenye yemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, ubudala beKaliyuga yiminyaka engamakhulu amane anamashumi amathathu anambini amawaka (432,000), kodwa ngenxa yempembelelo yezono, obu budala buya kuncitshiswa buya kuphela kwiminyaka nje emihlanu amawaka (5,000).

Umfanekiso kaMahapurush Achyutananda Das, owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-18 kwisithili saseJajpur e-Odisha, uMhlekazi uMahapurush Hadi Das waxoxa ngobudala beKaliyuga kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esibizwa ngokuba yi-'Kali Chautisha'. Wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, kwi-"Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (iminyaka eyi-5,000), iKaliyuga iya kuphela, kwaye emva koko, i-Adya Satya Yuga iya kuqala ngexesha elifanelekileyo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwenye imibhalo yeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush ubhala apha kwakhona ukuba emva kweminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga, kuya kubakho umoya woloyiko olukhulu; iintlekele ziya kuwela kuMlawuli waseDelhi okanye kwiNkulumbuso yelizwe. Umkhosi waseGandhara (amaTaliban) ngokuqinisekileyo uya kulwa neIndiya. UMahapurush ubhala ngakumbi ukuba konke oku kuya kwenzeka emva kokuphela kweKaliyuga, oko kukuthi, emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,050 yeKaliyuga.

U-Achyutananda Das ji wabhala kwakhona ivesi kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika apho engazange achaze ubudala beKaliyuga ngokuthe ngqo njenge-5,000 okanye i-5,050, kodwa wayikhankanya ngokungangqalanga ngeengcebiso:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, kwiminyaka 'yama-20'—ukusuka kunyaka wamawaka amabini (2000) ukuya kumawaka amabini anesithoba anesithoba (2099)—ngexesha elithile ngeli xesha, iKaliyuga iya kuphela. INkosi u-Ananta iya kutshabalalisa iKaliyuga eMhlabeni.

Omnye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, wachaza ubudala beKaliyuga ngokweenkcukacha kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Intsingiselo:
Umfundi uBaranga Das uthi kuMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"O Guru Dev, ndixelele ngekamva. Ndifuna ukuva emlonyeni wakho ukuba iya kuvela nini iNkosi ngendlela yaYo yeKalki."

Akuva oku, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das uxelela umfundi wakhe omkhulu uBaranga ukuba xa kuvela i-'Yuga-Sandhi' (utshintsho phakathi kweminyaka), kuphela ngelo xesha iNkosi iya kuthatha umfanekiso waYo weKalki. Echaza ngakumbi, uthi le Yuga-Sandhi iya kwenzeka xa iKaliyuga ibe ihlale iminyaka eyi-5,000. Kanye njengokuba kusiza intsasa emva kobusuku, omnye ubudala buza emva kobunye ubudala. Inxalenye ephakathi phakathi kobudala obubini ibizwa ngokuba yiYuga-Sandhi. Kwaye kule Yuga-Sandhi, uNarayana uya kuthatha indlela yaKhe yeKalki. Le Yuga-Sandhi iya kuza emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga.

U-Achyutananda ji, ngelixa echaza ubudala beKaliyuga kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, uthi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, yongeza enye kwinani eline (4+1=5) kwaye ubeke oonothi abathathu ecaleni kwesiphumo; inani elifunyenweyo libonisa ixesha emva koko ixesha leKaliyuga eyoyikekayo liya kuphela. Kule vesi ingentla, uMahapurush uxelela ubudala beKaliyuga ngokomfuziselo. Uthi 4+1=5, kwaye emva koko ukubeka oonothi abathathu ecaleni kwe-5 kuthetha ukuba emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga, obu budala buya kuphela.

Kwenye imibhalo yeMalika ethi ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, uthi kumfundi wakhe uRamachandra:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, O mfundi Ramachandra, umfanekiso weNkosi uKalki uya kwenzeka e-Amarapur (ummandla ongafi—okho nangaphambi kweSatya Yuga, oko kukuthi, iJajpur), kwaye oku kuya kwenzeka emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga.

Emva koko, kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Kali Bayalisha’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das uxelela ngobudala beKaliyuga ngeempawu. UMahapurush uthi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, bhala inani lesi-3 phezu kwenani loonyana bakaKumkani uDasharatha (oko kukuthi, 4^3 = 64). UMahapurush emva koko uthi makuguqulwe kwaye kukhutshwe i-11 kwelo nani (Ukuguqula i-64 kunika i-46, kwaye i-46 - 11 = 35). UMahapurush uthi makudibaniswe idijithi ekunene kweli nani lifunyenweyo nge-'Gandiva' (oko kukuthi, i-1000) kwaye ubhala ukuba iKaliyuga iya kuphela kwinani elifunyenweyo okanye iminyaka eyi-5,000.
Qaphela: Apha, nge-'Gandiva', uMahapurush ubhekisa kumgama wayo wokubetha, owawuyi-1,000 Yojanas.

U-Achyutananda Das ji ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Adi Sanhita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, ubudala beminyaka eyi-432,000 yeKaliyuga buya kuncipha bube yiminyaka nje eyi-5,000 ngenxa yezono.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, amava eKaliyuga aya kuba yiminyaka eyi-5,000. Emva koko, i-'Satyakali'—ukukhanya kwe-Adya Satya Yuga ngaphakathi kweKaliyuga—kuya kuvela.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga, uThixokazi uGanga uya kudlula eCuttack; oko kuthetha ukuthi, yonke into ukusuka eKolkata ukuya eCuttack iya kutshona; kuya kubakho isikhukula esoyikekayo.

UMahapurush wabhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga, iKaliyuga iya kuphela kwaye iNyaniso (Satya) iya kufika. Emva koko, ukukhanya kweNkosi uKalki kuya kuvela kuzo zonke iindawo—Mpuma, Ntshona, Mntla, kunye noMzantsi.

UMhlekazi u-Achyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, iminyaka eyi-427,000 iya kutshatyalaliswa kubudala beminyaka eyi-432,000 yeKaliyuga; kuphela iminyaka eyi-5,000 iya kuqhubeka, emva koko iSatya Yuga iya kuqala.

Hayi kuphela uMahapurush Achyutananda Das, kodwa omnye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, naye waxoxa ngobudala beKaliyuga kwimibhalo yakhe eyahlukeneyo yeMalika. Ngelixa echazela umfundi wakhe uBaranga kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Chumbaka Malika’, wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, mamela ngononophelo mfundi Baranga, bamba inani lesi-5. Xa kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000, kuphela ngelo xesha iya kuvela izenzo ezipheleleyo zobuthixo (Leelas) zeNkosi.

UMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala kwakhona kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Chumbaka Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga, umfanekiso weNkosi uVishnu uya kwenzeka.

Echaza ukuba iKaliyuga iya kuphela emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka emihlanu amawaka kwaye imidlalo yeNkosi iya kwenzeka, uMahapurush Balarama Das wabhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika esithi ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kuthetha ukuthi, umhlaba owawuyiHastinapur Cuttack yidolophu yaseDelhi yangoku. Mamela ibali elidlulileyo—uDevaapi wenza intlungu. Wenza intlungu kwilali yaseKalapa kummandla wase-Uttarakhand eIndiya, eya kugqitywa emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka eyi-5,000 yeKaliyuga. Emva kokuba intlungu igqityiwe, uya kudibana neNkosi. INkosi iya kumbeka isithsaba ngezandla zaYo ezingcwele kwaye imenze uKumkani waseHastinapur okanye eDelhi.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, emva kweminyaka engama-5,000 yeKaliyuga idlulile, iNkosi uJagannatha iya kushiya 'iDaaru-rupa' yakhe okanye imo kaJagannatha ize ithathe imo kaKalki.

Kuzo zonke iivesi ezingentla, kucacile ukuba ubudala obufunyenweyo beKaliyuga yiminyaka engama-5,000 kuphela. Ayiyiyo iminyaka engama-432,000 okanye iminyaka eli-10,000.
