Kodwa umbuzo obalulekileyo uvela apha— Njengoko sisazi, iminyaka engama-5,000 yeKaliyuga sele idlulile kwaye okwangoku kuqhuba unyaka wama-5,127. Ngokwale ngqikelelo, iKaliyuga ibifanele ukuba iphelile ngoku kwaye 'iAdya Satya Yuga' ibifanele ukuba iqhuba! Kodwa impembelelo yeKaliyuga isabonakala kuyo yonke indawo. Isono nokungalunganga kuxhaphakile kuyo yonke indawo. Kutheni le nto kukho olu phikisano?

Impendulo yile— Okokuqala, iminyaka engama-5,000 yingxelo jikelele, ayichanekanga ngokungqongqo. Iminyaka engama-5,000 ithetha malunga neminyaka engama-5,000. Esi sisizathu sokuba kwiindawo ezininzi, kubhalwe neminyaka engama-5,050.

Okwesibini, ngokweMalika, ubude beKaliyuga busele buphelile kwaye okwangoku, i-'Sandhikala' (ixesha lotshintsho) leKaliyuga neSatya Yuga liyaqhubeka. NgokweMalika, kule Sandhikala apho iziganeko zokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki kunye nokusekwa kweDharma nguye—okt, ukutshatyalaliswa kwabangendawo kunye nosindiso lwabazinikeleyo—ziya kwenzeka. Yiyo le nto isono nokungalunganga zikwinqanaba eliphezulu nangona ubude beKaliyuga buphelile. Kufuneka kwaziwe ukuba xa ukusekwa kweDharma kugqityiwe, impembelelo yeKaliyuga iya kuphela ngokupheleleyo kwaye iNyaniso, iDharma, kunye noBulungisa ziya kusekwa kwakhona.

UAchyutananda Das ukwabhalile kwiincwadi zakhe zeMalika ngokuba oku kusekwa kweDharma kuya kwenzeka nini phantsi kobunkokeli beNkosi uKalki.

4.7 Ukudibana kweMeena-Shani

UMahapurush wabhala kwincwadi yakhe ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, ukuba ubeka oonothi abathathu kunye nenani lesi-5 phambi kwabo (5,000), kuthetha ukuba emva kweminyaka engama-5,000 yeKaliyuga idlulile, kuya kwenzeka ukudibana kweMeena-Shani (iSaturn kwiPisces), okuya kuba kubaluleke kakhulu.

5 I-Meena-Shani Yoga; Ngokwe-Malika

5.1 Yintoni iMeena-Shani?

Kwisayensi yeenkwenkwezi, iSaturn (Shani) ithathwa njengeplanethi ehamba kancinci, ihlala kumqondiso omnye wezodiac malunga neminyaka engama-2.5. Kukho imiqondiso ye-zodiac eyi-12 iyonke kumjikelezo we-zodiac, kwaye iSaturn ihlala kwiqela ngalinye malunga neminyaka engama-2.5. Oku kuthetha ukuba iSaturn ithatha malunga neminyaka eyi-12 × 2.5 = 30 ukuhamba kuwo wonke umjikelezo we-zodiac.

Masithi iSaturn ingena kumqondiso we-zodiac namhlanje; ibuyela kuloo mqondiso emva kweminyaka engama-30 emva kokugqiba uhambo olupheleleyo. IMeena-Shani ithetha ukuhamba kweSaturn kumqondiso we-zodiac wePisces (Meena).

Okokugqibela iSaturn yahamba ngePisces kwakungo-1995. Ekubeni kuthatha malunga neminyaka engama-30 ukuba iSaturn ibuyele kumqondiso ofanayo, 1995 + 30 = 2025. Ngoko ke, ngonyaka ka-2025, iSaturn iya kuhamba iye kwiPisces kwakhona. Emva kuka-2025, uhambo olulandelayo luya kuba ngu-2025 + 30, oko kukuthi 2054–2055.

Iipanchasakha Mahapurushas zachaza ngokucacileyo kwiincwadi zazo zeMalika ukuba ukutshatyalaliswa kwabangendawo, ukukhuselwa kwabazinikeleyo, kunye nokusekwa kweDharma yiNkosi uKalki kuya kwenzeka ikakhulu ngexesha leMeena-Shani.

Ukuze siyiqonde ngcono le nto, masijonge ezinye iivesi ezikhankanywe kwiMalika kunye neengcaciso zazo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Prachi Kalpa’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho isikhalo emhlabeni; kuya kubakho iziphithiphithi kuyo yonke indawo. Akukho mntu ngaphandle kwabazinikeleyo oya kukwazi ukuziqonda ezi zinto. Kwezi ziganeko, abantu abangendawo abangenakubalwa naboni baya kutshatyalaliswa. UMahapurush wathi ezi ziganeko ziya kwenzeka ngexesha leminyaka engama-2.5 yeMeena-Shani Yoga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ukuba xa iSaturn ikwiPisces, ngelo xesha, iimvavanyo zabo bonke abahanahanisi nabangabazinikeleyo ziya kwenzeka eJobra naseKaliaboda eCuttack. OoMama abaNgcwele (ooThixokazi) baya kubatshabalalisa bonke abo bangabazinikeleyo. Ngelo xesha, kuya kubakho nobumnyama iintsuku ezisixhenxe nobusuku obusixhenxe. Ingqondo yomntu wonke iya kuyeka ukusebenza. Ngelo xesha, aboni abayi kukwazi nokufumana ukutya.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa iSaturn ikwiPisces, kuya kubakho iziphithiphithi eIndiya. Ngelo xesha, iNkosi ePhakamileyo iya kushiya imo yayo eqhelekileyo yobuntu ize ithathe imo kaKalki enamandla kakhulu.

Kwenye incwadi yeMalika, uMahapurush wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha lokudibana kweMeena-Shani, kuya kubakho kwakhona ukutshatyalaliswa okoyikekayo (iPralaya) eMhlabeni.

UAchyutananda Das ji ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha apho iSaturn ihamba ngePisces, iMfazwe yeHlabathi yesiThathu iya kuqala eMhlabeni, kuquka neIndiya. Kangangeminyaka emibini enesiqingatha, kuya kubonakala intshabalalo eyoyikekayo eMhlabeni. Ihlabathi lonke liya kutshatyalaliswa kwaye lithathe imo yendawo yokutshisa izidumbu. Ngelo xesha, abantu abangenakubalwa baya kufa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwenye incwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Bhabishyata Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, O bantu, lumkani. IMeena-Shani Yoga iyafika. Ngelo xesha, kuya kuza iintlekele ezininzi, kwaye intshabalalo iya kuchaphazela phantse wonke umntu; akukho mntu uya kusindiswa. Ingqondo yabantu iya kuyeka ukusebenza. Isiphithiphithi siya kuxhaphaka kuyo yonke indawo.

Wazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-19, imbongi engcwele uBhima Bhoi wabhala ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Padmakalpa’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, emva kwenxalenye ephakathi yeMeena-Shani Yoga, imilambo nemifudlana iya kuqala ukoma ngomzuzwana. Imikhosi yamazwe otshaba iya kungena eOdisha ize ilwe. Phakathi kwaloo Meena-Shani Yoga, xa iMars ikwiLeo, kuya kwenzeka neenyikima zomhlaba. INkosi iya kubagcina abazinikeleyo bayo bekhuselekile ngononophelo emiqolombeni yeentaba. UChandi noYoginis baya kubatshabalalisa abangendawo naboni.

Imbongi engumlandeli uNarada Das ukwakhankanye iziphithiphithi ezenzeka ngexesha leMeena-Shani Yoga kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika. Wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iMeena-Shani Yoga iya kuhlala iminyaka emibini enesiqingatha iyonke kwaye ngeli xesha, abantu abangenakubalwa baya kufa. Kuya kubakho isikhalo eMhlabeni. OoYoginis baya kwenza i-leela yentshabalalo. Ngeli xesha, izidumbu ziya kusasazeka emhlabeni. UMhlaba uya kungcangcazela ngesandi sikaBhairavi.

Ecaphula incoko phakathi kweNkosi uKrishna noMama uRukmini ngexesha leDvapara Yuga, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Rukmini Gita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha apho iSaturn ihamba kwiPisces, kuya kubakho imfazwe ekrakra eIndiya.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa iMeena-Shani Yoga isenzeka, iziphithiphithi ezoyikekayo ziya kubonakala kulo Mhlaba.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yeMalika egama lingu ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, ngexesha lokuseka iDharma, kuya kwenzeka isiqingatha senyanga (iPaksha) seentsuku ezilishumi elinesithathu. Kuya kubakho iMeena-Shani Yoga eya kuhlala iminyaka emibini enesiqingatha. Ngelo xesha, iinkwenkwezi ziya kubonakala emini. Ngelo xesha, abazinikeleyo baya kujamelana nobunzima obuninzi. Kuya kubakho imfazwe eChoudwar yaseCuttack; izixhobo zesayensi neze-elektroniki ziya kuyeka ukusebenza. Kuya kwenzeka imfazwe ekrakra. ImijukuJelwa yanamhlanje nemipu yamazwe otshaba iya kuthintelwa ngamandla okuzila (iTapa) abangcwele neengcwele zaseIndiya ezigqibeleleyo. INkosi uKalki iya kuvela; abazinikeleyo baya kufumana umbono (iDarshan) weNkosi. Ixesha eliNguna (iAnanta Yuga) liya kufika. Umnqweno kaAchyutananda uya kuzaliseka.

Wabhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iinkwenkwezi ziya kuwa esibhakabhakeni emini; abantu abangenakubalwa baya kufa ngenxa yempembelelo yezifo kunye nezifo ezosulelayo. Ngelo xesha, iMeena-Shani Yoga iya kuba iqhubeka.

UMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa iSaturn ingena kwiPisces, ixesha lokutshabalala koluntu liya kuqala. Inkqubo yedemokhrasi iya kuwa, kwaye iIndiya iya kuphulukana nobukhosi bayo—isenokude ibe phantsi kolawulo lomnye umntu. Isiphithiphithi siya kuthatha indawo yenkqubo yolawulo, kwaye ilizwe liya kutshona kwisiphithiphithi. Abazinikeleyo baya kothuka bakubona ezi ziganeko. Umlilo wokutshabalala awuyi kuginya iOdisha neIndiya kuphela, kodwa ihlabathi lonke.

UAchyutananda ji ubhala kwiKalikalpa Gita yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ukukhulula uMhlaba esonweni, iNkosi uMahavishnu iya kuthatha ukuzalwa komntu eMhlabeni. Ngexesha leMeena-Shani Yoga, ethatha imo yeKalki, iNkosi ePhakamileyo iya kuhamba kwilizwe kwaye ngokutshabalalisa abangendawo naboni, Iya kuhlangula uMama uMhlaba.

UMhlekazi ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kwenzeka. Imfazwe ekrakra iya kwenzeka eVirat Gadi okanye eChoudwar yaseCuttack, kwaye ikrele elineengalo ezilishumi elinambini liya kubulala aboni. Iinyikima zomhlaba ezinkulu ziya kwenzeka eziza kutshabalalisa abangendawo. Uvavanyo lwabo bonke abahanahanisi luya kwenzeka eJobra Ghat yaseCuttack, kwaye uMama oNgcwele uya kusela igazi labo. Ngeli xesha, abazinikeleyo baya kukhuselwa. UMhlekazi wabhala ukuba ezi ziganeko ziya kwenzeka kwiMeena-Shani Yoga.

UAchyutananda Das ji ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Tattvabodhini’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, emva kokungena kweSaturn kwiPisces, ilizwe laseOdisha liya konakala.

UMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iMeena-Shani Yoga iya kwenzeka, kwaye kuya kubakho iiveki ezimbini zeentsuku ezilishumi elinesithathu. Ngelo xesha linye, abantu baya kufa ngokufihlakeleyo, kwaye iDharma iya kusekwa.

Ngaphaya koko kwi‘Kalikalpa Gita’, ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha leMeena-Shani Yoga, iKaliyuga iya kuphela kwaye iDharma iya kusekwa.

UMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, eli hlabathi liya "konakala" okanye litshatyalaliswe ngokupheleleyo. UMama uBhairavi uya kukhupha isikhalo esikhulu; aboni abaninzi baya kujamelana nokufa okungafanelekanga, kwaye abaninzi baya kufa ngokufihlakeleyo. Zonke ezi zenzo zokutshabalalisa ziya kwenzeka ngexesha leMeena-Shani Yoga, oko kuthetha ukuba xa iSaturn ikhona kumqondiso wezodiac wePisces.

Kwimibongo yeMalika enikwe ngasentla, kucacile ukuba ngexesha leMeena-Shani Yoga, izenzo zokutshabalalisa zeNkosi uKalki ziya kuba nezona ziphumo zibalaseleyo. Ngeli xesha, uMhlaba wonke uya kubona iziganeko ezinkulu nezingazange zibonwe ngaphambili ezibuhlungu. Kuphela kukuphakama, imfazwe, iziphithiphithi, ukuphazamiseka, kunye neentlekele zendalo eziza kubonwa kuyo yonke indawo. Ngeli xesha, abangendawo nabangemalungisa baya kutshatyalaliswa kuMhlaba wonke.

5.2 Kweyiphina iMeena-Shani eziya kwenzeka kuyo iLeelas zeNkosi uKalki?

Emva kokwazi kwimibongo engasentla ukuba iileelas zokutshabalalisa zeKalki ziya kwenzeka ngexesha leMeena-Shani, umbuzo omkhulu uvela: ngeliphi ixesha elithile leMeena-Shani ezi ziganeko ziya kwenzeka?

IMeena-Shani Yoga yenzeka kanye emva kweminyaka engama-30. Ngoko ke, kubalulekile ukwazi ukuba yeyiphi iMeena-Shani, ngokweMalika, eya kubona iziganeko zokusekwa kweDharma.

Xa sijonga ngokusondeleyo, sifumanisa ukuba iSaturn yayikwiPisces ngo-1995; okwangoku, ngo-2025, iSaturn iphinde yangena kwiPisces; emva koko ngo-2054, kwaye malunga neminyaka engama-30 emva koko, ngo-2084, ukuhamba kweSaturn kwiPisces kuya kwenzeka kwakhona.

Yeyiphi iMeena-Shani?

Kubhalwe ngokucacileyo kwiBhavishya Malika ukuba xa ezi ziganeko zenzeka ngaphakathi kweminyaka emibini enesiqingatha yeMeena-Shani, igama loKumkani wasePuri, eOdisha, ngelo xesha liya kuba nguDivyasingha Deva.

UMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chandra Kalpa’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa iSaturn ikwiPisces, ngelo xesha, ubumnyama obunzulu buya kugubungela uMhlaba iintsuku ezi-7 nobusuku obu-7. Ukuchithwa okubangelwa zizinto ezintlanu (uMhlaba, Amanzi, Umlilo, uMoya, kunye neEther) kuya kuginya uMhlaba wonke. UMhlekazi wabhala ukuba ngelo xesha, igama loKumkani wasePuri liya kuba ngu‘Divyasingha’.

Ngoku kukho amanqaku afanele ukuqwalaselwa apha. UKumkani wasePuri wangoku, uShri Divyasingha Deva (IV), okwangoku uneminyaka engama-72 ubudala. KwiMeena-Shani elandelayo—oko kukuthi, kwiminyaka engama-30 ukusuka ngoku—ubudala bakhe buya kuba yi-100+ kwaye kukho amathuba angaphantsi kweqanda okuba uya kube esahlala esihlalweni sasePuri ngelo xesha!

Ngoku abanye abantu, abathi ixesha leziganeko yiMeena-Shani elandelayo (oko kukuthi, unyaka ka-2054–2057), bacebisa ukuba ezi ziganeko zisenokwenzeka ngexesha lolawulo lukakumkani othile ogama linguDivyasingha! Kodwa mandikuxelele ukuba oku akunakwenzeka. UKumkani wasePuri wangoku nguKumkani we-19, kwaye ngokweMalika, uKumkani we-19 uya kuba nguKumkani wokugqibela weKaliyuga. Oko kukuthi, ngokweMalika, emva koKumkani wangoku uShri Divyasingha Deva, akuyi kubakho baKumkani basePuri.

Ingxoxo engasentla ingqina ukuba zonke iziganeko ezibuhlungu ziya kwenzeka ngexesha leMeena-Shani Yoga phakathi kuka-2025–2028, ngexesha lolawulo lukaShri Divyasingha Deva IV.

UMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwisigaba sokugqibela seKaliyuga, kuya kubakho ukungabulungisa okukhulu kunye nomthwalo ogqithisileyo wesono. Ngelo xesha, amanzi olwandle aya kungena kwiJagannath Temple. Imfazwe iya kuqhambuka ngaphakathi etempileni. Umlambo wegazi uya kuhamba ngeSango leNgonyama (Singhadwara). Ngelo xesha, ubumnyama buya kugubungela uMhlaba wonke iintsuku ezisixhenxe nobusuku obusixhenxe. Bonke abo bangabazinikeleyo abahanahanisi, abafumana ubomi babo ngezenzo ezimbi, baya kutshatyalaliswa ngoMama abangcwele. Abazinikeleyo baya kuchitha ixesha labo elonwabileyo. Ngeli xesha, uKumkani wasePuri uya kuba nguShri Divyasingha Deva, oya kubanjwa okanye athinjwe ngabacinezeli.

Kucacile ngokupheleleyo kwindinyana engasentla ukuba xa iziganeko zokusekwa kweDharma zenzeka, kwaye xa kukho ubumnyama iintsuku ezisixhenxe nobusuku obusixhenxe eMhlabeni, uKumkani wasePuri ngelo xesha uya kuba nguShri Divyasingha Deva.

Omnye uMhlekazi waseOdisha owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-20 wabhala kwenye yeencwadi zakhe ukuba emva konyaka ka-2033, ukukhanya kwexesha elitsha (iSatyayuga) kuya kuvela; oko kuthetha ukuba, ngokweMhlekazi, zonke iileelas zokutshintsha kwamaxesha ziya kugqitywa ngaphambi konyaka ka-2033. Siya kunikezela imibongo enxulumene noku phambi kwakho kwiSahluko 12. Ingongoma kukuba zonke iziganeko ezibuhlungu ziya kwenzeka ngaphambi kuka-2033.

UMhlekazi uBhima Bhoi (imbongi eyimfama ezinikeleyo eyazalelwa eSambalpur, eOdisha ngenkulungwane ye-19) wabhala ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Padma Kalpa’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa iSaturn ingena kwiPisces, iileelas zokutshabalalisa zeNkosi uKalki ziya kwenzeka. Eli xesha liya kuhlala iminyaka emibini enesiqingatha. Ngeli xesha, iimfazwe ezingenakubalwa ziya kulwa phakathi kwamazwe ahlukeneyo. INkosi uKalki iya kukhusela abazinikeleyo, kwaye uMama uDurga uya kutshabalalisa abangendawo naboni.

Kwindinyana engasentla, uMhlekazi wachaza ngokucacileyo ukuba yeyiphi iMeena-Shani eziya kwenzeka kuyo iileelas zokutshabalalisa zeKalki. Echaza ubuni baloo Meena-Shani, wathi ngosuku iSaturn engena ngalo kwiPisces, iiplanethi ezi-5 ziya kube sele zikhona kwiPisces. Oko kukuthi, ngaloo mini, zizonke iiplanethi ezi-6 ziya kuba zikhona kwiPisces—iiplanethi ezi-5 sele zikhona kunye neSaturn enye, zizonke iiplanethi ezi-6.

Ukuba sijonga iMeena-Shani ka-2025, sifumanisa ukuba intetho yeMhlekazi ibonakala ichaneke ngokupheleleyo. Ngomhla wama-29 kuMatshi 2025, usuku iSaturn ehamba ngalo ingena kwiPisces, iiplanethi ezintlanu (uRahu, iVenus, iMercury, iLanga, kunye neNyanga) zaziyele zikhona kwiPisces.

Ngoku, ukuba sijonga iMeena-Shani ka-2054, sifumanisa ukuba ngomhla we-14 kuMeyi 2054, usuku iSaturn ehamba ngalo ingena kwiPisces, iiplanethi ezi-2 kuphela (iVenus kunye neMars) ziya kuba zikhona kwiPisces ngaphambili, kwaye kunye neSaturn, zenza iiplanethi ezi-3. Ngelixa ngokukaSaint Bhima Bhoi, kufanele kubekho iiplanethi ezi-6 zizonke kwiPisces, hayi ezi-3.

Ukuba sijonga iMeena-Shani Yoga yonyaka ka-2084, sifumanisa ukuba ngomhla wama-20 kuMatshi 2084, usuku iSaturn ehamba ngalo ingena kwiPisces, iiplanethi ezi-2 kuphela (iMercury kunye neLanga) ziya kuba zikhona kwiPisces ngaphambili. Nokuba kubandakanywa iSaturn, ziiplanethi ezi-3. Oku kuthetha ukuba ukuhamba kweeplanethi ezi-6 akwenzeki apha nako.

Kucacile ukuba uMhlekazi akathethi ngeMeena-Shani Yoga ka-2054 okanye ka-2084. IMeena-Shani athetha ngayo kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Padma Kalpa’ yeyona Meena-Shani ka-2025.

UMhlekazi uAchyutananda Das wachaza kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ukuba ngubani oya kuba yiMahant (Intloko) yePadmaban Peeth yakhe eNemalo, eCuttack, xa ezi leelas zokutshabalalisa zenzeka. Wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, O mfundi uRamadas, xa iKaliyuga iphela, ngelo xesha umntu ogama linguGaganānanda uya kuba yiNtloko yePeeth yam.

UMahapurush wabhalela kwenye incwadi yeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iileela ziya kwenzeka ngexesha likaMahant Gaganānanda. Ngeli xesha, kuya kubakho isikhalo kwiphondo laseOdisha.

5.3 UMahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba kwisizukulwana se-14 sikaMahapurush Achyutananda Das, inzala yakhe kwaye ngoku oneminyaka engama-85–90 ubudala uShri Gaganānanda Goswamiji yiNtloko yesihlalo sokomoya sikaMahapurush, iPadmaban, Nemalo. Oku kwenza kucace ukuba uMahapurush uthetha ngexesha langoku.

Kumanqaku amane angentla, kucacile ukuba ngokweMalika, iMeena-Shani apho kwenzeka khona iileela zokutshabalalisa zikaKalki iya kuza ngaphambi konyaka ka-2033, kwaye ngelo xesha leMeena-Shani, uShri Divyasingha Deva uya kuba yiKumkani yasePuri kwaye uShri Gaganānanda uya kuba nguMahant wePadmaban Peeth, Nemalo. Ayipheleli nje apha, kodwa ngosuku uSaturn adlulela ngalo kwiPisces, iShad-graha (iiplanethi ezi-6) Yoga iya kuba isenza kwiPisces.

Kucacile ukuba uMahapurush ukhomba kwiMeena-Shani efika phakathi kuka-2025–2028 kuba onke amanqaku amane angentla asebenza kuphela kule Meena-Shani Yoga kwaye akukho enye.

Akukho sidingo sokutsho ukuba ngokweMalika, iziganeko ezinkulu zentlekele eziya kwenziwa yiNkosi uKalki ziya kwenzeka phakathi kuka-2025–2028.

Qaphela: Kule mihla abanye abantu bathi iMeena-Shani ekhankanywe kwiMalika ayiyo Meena-Shani yangoku, kodwa yile elandelayo efika ngo-2054! Kuya kufaneleka ukuba bonke abazinikeleyo abanokholo kwiMalika bangabhidaniswa yoku, kuba ezo ngxelo azichanekanga ngokupheleleyo.

6 Ukumkani wasePuri Ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga

Kwisahluko esidlulileyo, sibone ukuba zonke iziganeko eziphambili zokusekwa kweDharma ziya kwenzeka ngexesha lolawulo lweKumkani yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva. Kwesi sahluko, siya kukuqonda oku ngezinye iivesi zeMalika.

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Jagannath Das, wabhalela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kali Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, ukuqala ngeKumkani uPurushottama Deva emva kweKumkani uIndradyumna, iikumkani ezili-19 zizonke ziya kuba ziiKumkani zaseJagannath Puri ngamaxesha ahlukeneyo. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba iKumkani ye-19 iya kuba ngaphandle konyana kwaye uya kuba yikumkani yokugqibela; oko kukuthi, akukho mntu wumbi uya kuba yikumkani emva kwakhe.

Ukuba sijonga imeko yangoku, sifumanisa ukuba iKumkani yasePuri yangoku yiKumkani ye-19 kwaye nayo ayinanyana. Uphawu olucacileyo lukaMahapurush kukuba zonke iintshukumo zikaKalki zokusekwa kweDharma ziya kwenzeka ngexesha lolawulo lwekumkani yangoku.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Adi Samhita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, uShri Divyasingha uya kuba yiKumkani yasePuri.

Kwenye incwadi yeMalika, wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, xa ulawulo lweKumkani egama linguShri Divyasingha Deva luqhubeka ePuri, ngelo xesha zonke iintshukumo zobuthixo zeNkosi uKalki ziya kwenzeka. Ngeli xesha, iNkosi iya kushiya iTempile yayo yaseJagannath kwaye izalwe emzimbeni womntu njengeKalki Avatar.

Emva koko kwincwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, uAchyutanandaji ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, u“Baalak-Briddha” uya kuba yiKumkani yasePuri; oko kukuthi, umntu uya kuba yikumkani enyuka esihlalweni ebuntwaneni kwaye ahlale kweso sikhundla de abe mdala. Ngexesha lolawulo lwakhe, iziganeko ezifana neentlekele zamanzi neentlekele zomoya ziya kwenzeka.

Kuyaziwa ukuba phakathi kookumkani abenziweyo ukuza kuthi ga ngoku, akukho namnye ngaphandle kukaShri Divyasingha Deva oye waba yikumkani ukusuka ebuntwaneni ukuya kubudala. IKumkani yangoku uShri Divyasingha Deva wanyuka esihlalweni nge-7 kaJulayi 1970, eneminyaka eli-17 kuphela. Ngelixa ekulesi sikhundla, ungene kubudala kwaye ngoku uneminyaka engama-72 ubudala. Ngalo vesi, ngolwimi olungumfuziselo, uMahapurush watyhila ukuba uDivyasingha Deva uya kuba yikumkani ekupheleni kweKaliyuga.

UAchyutananda Dasji wabhala ngakumbi kwi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, xa uShri Divyasingha Deva eyiKumkani yasePuri, ngelo xesha ikometi iya kubonakala kwicala lasempumalanga. Kuya kubakho imvula yeenkwenkwezi emhlabeni wonke. Izilwanyana ezitya inyama ezifana nezinja neempungutye ziya kuphuma emahlathini zisingise kwiindawo zokuhlala. UMama uKali kunye nabahlobo bakhe abangooYoginis baya kutshabalalisa aboni emhlabeni, okukhokelela ekufeni kwabantu abaninzi.

UMahapurush wazisa ngokufutshane ngovesi olungentla ukuba iziganeko zokusekwa kweDharma ziya kwenzeka ngexesha lolawulo lweKumkani yasePuri egama linguShri Divyasingha Deva ekupheleni kweKaliyuga.

UMahapurush wathi kumfundi wakhe uRamadas kwenye incwadi yeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, O mfundi Ramadas, mamela ukuba leliphi ixesha endithetha ngalo—ngexesha ezi ziganeko zenzeka, ulawulo lweKumkani egama linguDivyasingha luya kuba luqhubeka, ongayi kuba nanyana; iintombi kuphela.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalela kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, kuya kubakho ulawulo lweKumkani egama linguDivyasingha engayi kuba nanyana.

Esi siprofeto sikaMahapurush sikwafaneleka kwiKumkani yasePuri yangoku, uShri Divyasingha Deva, ngokugqibeleleyo. IKumkani yangoku ayinanyana; unentombi ezine kuphela.

Kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika, i ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala amazwi athethwa yiNkosi uKrishna kuArjuna eDwaparayuga, apho iNkosi uKrishna yathi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, iKumkani yasePuri ayiyi kwenza inkonzo yam yemihla ngemihla (ngendlela kaJagannath).

UAchyutananda Dasji emva koko wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, iKumkani yasePuri ayiyi kuhlala eShrikshetra okanye ePuri, kodwa iya kuhlala kwenye indawo. Akazukutshata inkosazana yosapho lwasebukhosini lwaseOdisha kodwa uya kutshata inkosazana evela kwelinye ilizwe.

Ingcwele enkulu yaseOdisha eyazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-18, uMahapurush Arakshita Das, wabhalela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush ubhala ukuba ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, uDivyasingha Deva uya kuba yiKumkani yasePuri kwaye akayi kuhlala eShrikshetra okanye ePuri, kodwa uya kuhlala kwindawo yaseKhandagiri okanye eBhubaneswar. UMahapurush ubhala ngakumbi ukuba yena ngokwakhe wayenokuyazi njani imfihlelo yezi zinto; uMama wokuqala (uAdi Mata) wamxelela ezi zinto.

Mandazise abafundi ukuba zonke iikumkani ngaphambi kweKumkani yangoku zazihlala ePuri, eOdisha, kwaye zenza inkonzo yemihla ngemihla yeNkosi uJagannath, kodwa iKumkani yangoku uShri Divyasingha Deva wenza inkonzo yeNkosi uJagannath kuphela ngezihlandlo ezithile. Akahlali ePuri kodwa uhlala eShahid Nagar, eBhubaneswar. Ukongeza, uShri Divyasingha Deva akazange atshate inkosazana yaseOdisha kodwa watshata intombi kaLieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak waseJammu.

Ezi ziprofeto zintathu zingentla zifaneleka kwiKumkani yasePuri yangoku, uShri Divyasingha Deva, ngokugqibeleleyo. Oku kukwangubungqina bokuba iKaliyuga iya kuphela ngexesha lolawulo lweKumkani uShri Divyasingha Deva.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalela kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika ethi ‘Tera Janma Sharana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, xa uDivyasingha Deva eyiKumkani yasePuri, ke ulawulo lukaAnanta Keshari, oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki, luya kwenzeka. Kuya kuba lusuku lweMagha Saptami; IBhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) iya kuba yindawo.

Lo vesi ukwangqina ukuba iKumkani yasePuri ngexesha leentshukumo zobuthixo zikaKalki ayiyi kuba ngomnye ngaphandle kukaShri Divyasingha Deva.

Kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Mohakalpa’, uAchyutanandaji wanika esinye isalathiso esicacisa ukuba uShri Divyasingha Deva uya kuba yikumkani yokugqibela yasePuri. UMahapurush ubhala oku:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, iKumkani yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva, uya kulishiya iVaishnava Dharma lakhe aze amkele umsebenzi weKshatriya aze alwe iimfazwe ezininzi. Akubona iNkosi uKalki, uya kuwa ezinyaweni zeNkosi, afumane iintsikelelo zeNkosi, aze atshabalalise ama-lakh asithoba abangakholwayo. Kwindawo yaseJagannath, ePuri, bonke abaBrahmins abakhoyo baya kuvavanywa ngeGayatri Mantra. Esoyika isono sokubulala abaBrahmins, uDivyasingha Deva akayi kubabulala abo Brahmins abasilela kuvavanyo. UMahapurush ubhala ukuba bonke baya kutshatyalaliswa yiSudarshana Chakra yeNkosi ngokwayo. INkosi iya kubeka iSudarshana Chakra yayo esibhakabhakeni esingenanto ize inqumle iintloko zabo Brahmins ngale chakra inye abangayiculi iGayatri Mantra.

Apha, uMahapurush unika ubungqina bokuba iKumkani yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva, uya kudibana neNkosi uKalki aze negalelo ekusekweni kweDharma. Kucacile ukuba imidlalo engcwele etshabalalisayo yeNkosi uKalki iya kwenzeka ngexesha lolawulo lukaShri Divyasingha Deva.

Emva koko uMahapurush ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Kalpa Tika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, xa iKumkani yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva, ithinjwa yimikhosi yangaphandle, emva kweentsuku ezimbini, iNkosi uKalki iya kufikelela kwikumkani ebusuku, imkhulule ekubanjweni, ize imfihle ezintabeni zaseKhandagiri.

Ngaphaya koko, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Padmakalpa Tika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, xa intlekele enkulu yamanzi isenzeka, abazinikeleyo abaxhomekeke kwiNkosi uKalki baya kuhlala bekhuselekile emqolombeni waseAnanta okwintaba yaseKhandagiri. Ngelo xesha, urhulumente uya kubonelela ngokutya nezinto zokunceda amaxhoba. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ngelo xesha, uAnanta Madhava, oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki, uya kuba seKhandagiri, eOdisha. IKumkani uDivyasingha Deva nayo iya kuba Naye.

UMahapurush waphinda wabhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Kalpa Tika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, xa iNkosi uAnanta Keshari, oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki, ikwiKhandagiri, ngelo xesha uDivyasingha Deva naye uya kuba seKhandagiri. EKhandagiri, uDivyasingha Deva uya kuba nombono (Darshan) weNkosi.

Kuzo zonke iivesi ezintathu ezinikwe ngasentla, kuyaziwa ukuba ngexesha lemidlalo engcwele yeNkosi uKalki, iKumkani yasePuri iya kuba nguDivyasingha Deva.

6.1 Intlanganiso yeNkosi uKalki noBaladeva noDivyasingha Deva kunye neeKumkani ezininzi

IBhavishya Malika izele ziziganeko ezininzi eziyimfihlo neziprofeto. Phakathi kwezi ziprofeto kukho isiprofeto sokudibana kookumkani abaninzi neNkosi.

UMahapurush ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ukuba kwixesha elizayo, kuya kufika ixesha apho amajoni angaphandle aya kuhlala ukusuka eChhatia, eJajpur ukuya eNilagiri okanye eNilachal, ePuri. Nomkhosi omkhulu wotshaba uya kungena ngaphakathi kwiTempile yaseJagannath. Ebona konke oku kusizi olukhulu, iNkosi, ezintywiliselwe ekuzileni, iya kukhumbula uSahadeva, uNakula, uAshwatthama, noShalya. Ngokukhawuleza xa iNkosi ibakhumbula, ukucamngca nokuzila kwaba majoni mane anesibindi, iingcali zokutola, kuya kuphazamiseka, kwaye bonke abane baya kufika eChhatia baze bawe ezinyaweni zeNkosi, becela umyalelo Wayo. INkosi iya kubayalela bonke abane ukuba batshabalalise umkhosi omkhulu wamaMughal baze bakhusele abazinikeleyo. Ngokomyalelo weNkosi, bonke abane baya kubatshabalalisa amajoni amaMughal baze babuyele eNkosini. Ngelo xesha, abazinikeleyo abaninzi baya kuba neNkosi eChhatia, kwaye kangangeentsuku ezingama-27, iChhatia iya kuba yindawo yokudibana yabazinikeleyo. Ngeli xesha, ookumkani baseRashiya, eNepal, eManipur, eAli, eKujang, eKanika, njl., baya kudibana neNkosi, bafumane iDarshan Yayo, baze bazinikele ezinyaweni Zayo. Ngelo xesha, iNkosi iya kubathatha bonke baze bafike eSealdah ukuze bacele iNkosi uBaladeva ukuba atshabalalise umkhosi wasemzini oseleyo. Ngale ndlela, bonke ookumkani abaneNkosi baya kuya eSealdah baze badibane noBaladeva, apho uArjuna noBhima baya kuba sele bekhona.

Apha uMahapurush ubhala ukuba ngeli xesha, phakathi kookumkani abaya kudibana neNkosi uAnanta Keshari kunye neNkosi uBaladeva, iKumkani yasePuri, iShrikshetra, nayo iya kuba khona. Etyhila ukuba uya kuba ngubani, uMahapurush ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, phakathi kookumkani abafumana iDarshan yeNkosi kuya kuba yiKumkani yaseShrikshetra Puri, uDivyasingha Deva.

Kwiingxoxo ezingentla, kucacile ukuba iKumkani yasePuri ngexesha lemidlalo engcwele kaKalki iya kuba nguShri Divyasingha Deva.

Kwiivesi ezili-17 ezingentla, kubonakaliswe ngokucacileyo ukuba ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, xa iNkosi uKalki sele izelwe kwaye xa imidlalo Yayo etshabalalisayo isenzeka kwihlabathi lonke, xa intshabalalo yabangendawo, usindiso lwabazinikeleyo, kunye nomsebenzi wokuseka iDharma zenzeka, ngelo xesha ikumkani egama linguDivyasingha Deva iya kuhlala etroneni yaseJagannath Puri, kwaye uya kuba yikumkani yokugqibela ukuhlala kwesi sikhundla.

UShri Divyasingha Deva uya kuba yikumkani yokugqibela—oku kwabhalwa nguMahapurush Jagannath Das, omnye weePanchasakhas, kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika, esikhankanywe kwivesi yokuqala yale sahluko.

Kodwa into ephawulekayo kukuba akazange abhale uJagannath Das kuphela, kodwa omnye uMahapurush phakathi kwePanchasakhas, uShishu Ananta Das, naye wabhala ngalo mbandela kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Patta Madana’. UMahapurush ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Intsingiselo:
Intsingiselo, iKumkani uDivyasingha Deva iya kulawula kwaye emva kwakhe, akuyi kubakho lawulo lwayo nayiphi na enye ikumkani. UDivyasingha Deva uya kuba yikumkani yokugqibela. Ngamanye amazwi, iKaliyuga iya kuphela ngexesha lakhe, kwaye imidlalo engcwele etshabalalisayo yeNkosi uKalki iya kwenzeka.

6.2 UShri Divyasingha Deva

Inyaniso efanele ukuphawulwa apha kukuba uMahapurush akazange ayeke nje ngokuthi igama leKumkani yasePuri ekupheleni kweKaliyuga liya kuba nguDivyasingha Deva; endaweni yoko, waxela izinto ezininzi ngayo, njengokuba uya kuba yikumkani ye-19, uya kuba nguDivyasingha Deva wesine, uya kuba engenanyana, uya kuba neentombi kuphela, akayi kuhlala ePuri kodwa eBhubaneswar, akayi kukwazi ukwenza iinkonzo zemihla ngemihla zeNkosi uJagannath, uya kuthatha ubukumkani ebuntwaneni aze ahlale eyikumkani de abe mdala, njl.

Zonke ezi zinto zihambelana ngokugqibeleleyo neKumkani yangoku uShri Divyasingha Deva.

7 Iimpawu zokuphela kweKaliyuga kwiTempile yaseJagannath

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das kunye nabanye abakwaPanchasakhas babhale kwiindawo ezininzi kwizicatshulwa zabo zeMalika ukuba abazinikeleyo baya kufumana iimpawu ezininzi kwiTempile yaseJagannath apho baya kukwazi ukwazi ukuba iKali Yuga iphelile kwaye iNkosi uKalki izelwe. Ezi mpawu ziya kubonwa kungekuphela nje kwiTempile yaseJagannath kodwa nakwezinye iimonastri kunye neetempile eIndiya, kodwa ngokuyintloko ziya kubonakala kwiTempile yaseJagannath ekwiPuri.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ukuba ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, iintlobo ezahlukeneyo zeziganeko ezimbi (iimpawu ezimbi) ziya kubonwa ngokuphindaphindiweyo kwiShrimandir (iTempile yaseJagannath) ekwiPuri. Wongezelela wathi eli lizwi lam liya kungqineka liyinyaniso ngokupheleleyo.

7.1 Ingxelo yeempawu ezimbi kwiTempile yaseJagannath

Namhlanje sifumanisa ukuba kule minyaka ili-15-20 idlulileyo, kuye kwabonwa iimpawu ezininzi ezimbi kwiTempile yaseJagannath, njengokuba ilitye elikhulu liwe kwi-"Amla Bedha" yetempile ngonyaka we-1990; amatye awa emhlabeni ukusuka kwizakhiwo eziphezulu zetempile, kuquka nendawo engcwele kakhulu (sanctum sanctorum), izihlandlo ezininzi kule minyaka yakutshanje; iintaka ezimbi (ezitya izidumbu) zihleli kwipali yeflegi yetempile nakwiAruna Stambha phambi kweSinghadwara (Isango leNgonyama); ukhozi olungenakubalwa lujikeleza phezu kwetempile; umthunzi ohonjiswe ngamatye anqabileyo (Ratnachandua) obekwe phezu kwezithixo ubamba umlilo; i-'Patitapabana Bana' (iflegi) ephakanyiswe phezu kwetempile ibamba umlilo; iNeelachakra enobunzima be-2.2-ton, eyenziwe ngeAshtadhatu (iintsiba ezisibhozo), igobile yimimoya enesantya esiphezulu; i-'Patitapabana Bana' iphephethwa yawa elwandle ngexesha lesaqhwithi esikhulu; isebe leKalpavata elikhulu (umthi we-banyan) elikwindawo yetempile liqhawuka; iPatitapabana Bana itsha ngenxa yokubethwa yimbane; malunga nama-40 ezitovu zodongwe zonakaliswa ngabantu abangendawo ekhitshini elingcwele letempile; kuqhekeka okufunyenwe kwiAruna Stambha yetempile, umqadi ngaphakathi kweNatamandapa, ihholo eliphambi kwendawo engcwele kakhulu (Jagamohana), nasecaleni kweSinghadwara; ilitye le-'Padma Pakhuda' (iqabunga lelotus) lanyamalala kwiRatnasinghasana (itroni ehonjiswe ngamatye anqabileyo); ukubethwa yimbane ekhitshini letempile; ubusela obenzeka etempileni ebusuku; ukwanda kwamagundane etempileni ngenxa yokuncipha kwenani labazinikeleyo ngexesha lobhubhane weCorona, okubangela ukuphazamiseka nokuphazamisa amasiko etempile; amachaphaza egazi afunyenwe kwiindawo ezininzi kuquka neRatnasinghasana yetempile, kulandelwa ukuhlamba okukhulu kwesiko (Mahasnana) kweChaturdha Murti (oothixo abane); isibhakabhaka sijika saba bomvu macala onke etempile; ukunyamalala ngequbuliso kwamalulwane awayebonwa ngobuninzi etempileni; ukusetyenziswa kweefowuni eziphathwayo nangona zivaliwe etempileni; ukurekhodwa kwevidiyo okukhohlisayo kwindawo yetempile kusetyenziswa iikhamera zobuntlola ezifihlwe kwiiglasi; ukungena kwabangengomaHindu nabangengomaSanatani etempileni nangona kuvaliwe; ukuzibulala okanye ukufa ngenxa yezinye izizathu okwenzeka ngaphakathi etempileni; ukufa kwiBaisi Pahacha, oko kukuthi, izinyuko ezingama-22 zetempile; kuqhekeka okufunyenwe kwiGumuta (idome) yeHasti Dwara (Isango laseMantla) yetempile, njl.

Iziprofeto ezahlukileyo malunga nezinye zezi ziganeko zifumaneka kwizicatshulwa ezahlukeneyo zeMalika, apho kucaciswe ngokucacileyo ukuba ezi ziganeko ziya kwenzeka kuphela ekupheleni kweKali Yuga kwaye ziya kunika abazinikeleyo umqondiso wokuhla kweNkosi uKalki emhlabeni. Ngoko ke, abo bavakalisa amathandabuzo nokungakholwa kwabo namhlanje malunga nokuphela kweKali Yuga kunye nokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki kufuneka baphinde bacinge ngeembono zabo.




UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwi-'Jaiphula Malika' yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ngexesha elikhankanyiweyo, iziganeko zokuwa kwamatye ngokuphindaphindiweyo kwiTempile yaseJagannath ekwiPuri ziya kuvela, eziya kubonwa ngokucacileyo ngabazinikeleyo ngamehlo abo.

Ukuba siqwalasela inyaniso yale profeto, siya kufumanisa ukuba emva kokuba ilitye elinobunzima be-5-6 ton liwe kwi-"Amla Bedha" yetempile nge-14 kaJuni, 1990, iziganeko ezinjalo zenzeke izihlandlo ezininzi. Nge-13 kaAgasti, 1992, amatye amakhulu amabini awa ngqo kwindawo engcwele kakhulu yetempile. Ngokufanayo, nge-26 kaOkthobha, 1998, ilitye elinobunzima be-15 kg laqengqeleka ukusuka etempileni ephambili laza lawa kufutshane netempile yaseBimala ngaphakathi kwendawo yetempile ephambili. Nge-1 kaNovemba, 2011, ilitye elinobunzima obumalunga ne-1 ton lawa ukusuka kufutshane neDadhinauti yesango lasentshona leTempile yaseJagannath.

Kwakhona ngonyaka ka-2015, nge-2 kaFebruwari, ilitye elinobunzima obuyi-30-40 kg lawela phantsi lisuka kwiJalakrida Mandapa eyakhiwe kufutshane neBimala Temple kwicala elingasentshona leJagannath Temple. Sithetha ngamaxesha akutshanje, nge-3 ka-Agasti, 2022, isiqwenga seplasta esinobunzima obumalunga ne-1.5 kg sawa eluphahleni lwendawo engcwele kakhulu yale tempile yakhiwe ngenkulungwane yeshumi elinesibini. Nge-23 ka-Okthobha, 2022, ilitye lawa kwikona esemazantsi-mpuma etempile kufutshane neMajana Mandapa neCharcha Ghara, kwaye kwisithuba seveki emva koko, nge-28 ka-Okthobha, 2022, ilitye eliyi-2-3 kg lawa kumaleko wesithathu ngaphezu komfanekiso weSakhi kufutshane nendawo edibeneyo yeNatamandapa neJagamohana. Nangonyaka ka-2024, nge-9 kaMatshi, ilitye elinobunzima obumalunga neekhilogram ezine lawa eluphahleni lweBaraha Temple ebekwe phambi kweBimala Temple kwisakhiwo seJagannath Temple.

Ngale ndlela, isiprofeto esenziwa kwiminyaka engamakhulu eyadlulayo nguMahapurush Achyutananda Das malunga namatye awa "tharaku thara" (ngokuphindaphindiweyo) kwiJagannath Temple naso singqineke siyinyani kule minyaka imbalwa idlulileyo.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uye waxoxa ngokweenkcukacha ngemiqondiso emininzi emibi yesiphelo seKali Yuga efumaneka kwiJagannath Temple ePuri kwizibhalo ezininzi zeMalika kuquka 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', kunye ne'Chakada Madana'. Kulo mongo, ubhala ukuba xa ilitye liwa kwi-"Amla Bedha" yeJagannath Temple, abazinikeleyo kufuneka baqonde ukuba isiphelo seKali Yuga sisondele kakhulu kwaye iintlekele nezibetho ezininzi ziza kuhlela umhlaba kungekudala. Uthi ukuwa kweli litye kuya kuba ngumqondiso obalulekileyo noyimfihlo wesiphelo seKali Yuga, kwaye emva kweminyaka engama-23 emva kokuwa kwalo, iKali Yuga iya kuphela. Uphinda athi ngelo xesha, intaka embi iya kuhlala phezu kweJagannath Temple kunye neAruna Stambha ebekwe phambi kweSinghadwara yetempile. Akwaneli oko, iNeelachakra enobunzima obuyi-2.2-thoni eyenziwe ngeAshtadhatu encotsheni yetempile iya kugoba ngenxa yesantya esiphezulu somoya.

Abafundi baya kumangaliswa kukwazi ukuba zonke iziprofeto ezikhankanywe ngasentla ezabhalwa kwiminyaka engamakhulu eyadlulayo ngoku zizalisekile kwiJagannath Temple. Phakathi kwezimvula ezinkulu, okokuqala, nge-14 kaJuni, 1990, ilitye elinobunzima obuyi-5-6 iithoni lawa kwi-"Amla Bedha" yetempile. Emva koko kwafunyanwa nentaka embi ihleli kwiAruna Stambha.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba akukho ntaka yakha yabona ibhabha phezu kwetempile yaseJagannath Puri, kwaye oosonzululwazi nabaphandi nabo basilele ukuhlalutya ukuba kwenzeke njani oku. Kodwa ngeli xesha lesiphelo seKali Yuga, kungqina iziprofeto zeMalika ziyinyani, iintaka ezimbi ezifana neengqatyana/amaxhalanga ziye zabonwa zihleli kwiPatitapabana Bana (isibonda seflegi) phezu kwetempile izihlandlo ezininzi. Kwiminyaka yakutshanje, kwenzeka okufanayo nge-11 ka-Agasti, 2018, nge-26 kaDisemba, 2018, nange-23 kaJulayi, 2020, xa kwabonwa iintaka ezimbi zihleli kwisibonda seflegi setempile. Nge-7 kaDisemba, 2021, iintaka ezingenakubalwa ezinjalo zabonwa zijikeleza ngomhlambi phezu kweJagannath Temple. Isiganeko esifanayo sabonwa kwakhona nge-17 ka-Agasti, 2022. Olu landelelwano aluzange luphele apha; kwiinyanga zikaMeyi, Juni, noSeptemba zonyaka ka-2023, abahlali basePuri baphinda bangqina esi siganeko singathandekiyo xa kwabonwa iintaka ezifanayo zihleli kwisibonda seflegi esifakwe phezu kwetempile.

Kutshanje ngonyaka ka-2025, intaka yabonwa phezu kweAruna Stambha nge-4 kaSeptemba, ngelixa iingqatyana zabonwa zihleli phezu kwetempile nge-2 kaSeptemba, nge-16 kaSeptemba, nange-14 kaOkthobha. Nge-9 kaDisemba, iintaka ezingenakubalwa zafunyanwa zijikeleza iShrimandir.

Ukongeza kuko konke oku, isiphango esinamandla kakhulu, 'Fani', esabetha unxweme lwasePuri nge-3 kaMeyi, 2019, sagoba iNeelachakra phezu kwetempile, ngaloo ndlela sizalisekisa esinye isiprofeto esabhalwa kwiminyaka engamakhulu eyadlulayo kolu landelelwano.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba ezi ziprofeto zabhalwa ngenkulungwane ye-16 azingobungqina nje besiphelo seKali Yuga, kodwa zikwangimiqondiso enikwe yiPanchasakhas yokufika kweKalki Avatar, kuba iKalki Avatar iya kufika kuphela ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, njengoko kuchazwe kuzo zonke izibhalo ezingcwele kuquka neBhavishya Malika.





IsiOdia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ukhankanye omnye umqondiso wesiphelo seKali Yuga kunye nokuzalwa kweNkosi Kalki kwizibhalo zakhe zeMalika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' kunye ne'Kalikalpa Gita'. Ubhale ukuba kuya kufika ixesha apho iPatitapabana Bana encanyathiselwe phezu kweNeelachakra yeShrimandir iya kuphaphazela isuke endaweni yayo. UMahapurush uphinde wabhala ukuba iflegi iya kuphaphazela iwele elwandle.

Esi siprofeto esenziwa nguMahapurush Achyutananda Das naso singqineke sichanekile. Ntsuku-nye ngaphambi kokuba isiphango esinamandla kakhulu 'Fani' sibethe unxweme lwasePuri, nge-2 kaMeyi, 2019, ngenxa yemimoya enamandla, iPatitapabana Bana yaphaphazela yawa elwandle. Kwenzeka kanye isiganeko esifanayo kwakhona nge-18 kaMeyi, 2020, kwiintsuku ezimbini ngaphambi kokuba iCyclone 'Amphan' ibethe unxweme lwaseOdisha.



IsiOdia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Intsingiselo:
Ecaphula amazwi eNkosi, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwisibhalo sakhe seMalika 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' ukuba xa iNkosi Jagannath ishiya iNilachala, oko kukuthi, iShrimandir, ukuze izalwe emzimbeni womntu, ngelo xesha umthunzi ohonjiswe ngamatye anqabileyo obekwe phezu kobuthixo obuthathu obungcwele kwiJagannath Temple uya kubamba umlilo.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba isiganeko seRatnachandua esibambe umlilo senzeka ngonyaka ka-2004-05, kwaye ngaloo ndlela esi siprofeto sikaMahapurush naso singqineke siyinyani.

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwisibhalo sakhe seMalika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' kwiminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo ukuba kuya kufika ixesha apho isebe leKalpavata elibekwe kwisakhiwo seJagannath Temple liya kuqhekeka. Uphinde wabhala kwesinye sezibhalo zakhe ukuba xa kukho 'isithoba' ecaleni 'kwesinye', oko kukuthi, inombolo 19, kuphela ngelo xesha kuya kwenzeka esi siganeko.

Ngokuchanekileyo ngokwesitatimende sikaMahapurush, esi siprofeto saziseka ngonyaka ka-2019 xa isiphango esinamandla kakhulu 'Fani', esabetha unxweme lwasePuri nge-3 kaMeyi, sonakalise iKalpavata enkulu, endala kakhulu ebekwe kwisakhiwo setempile.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Intsingiselo:
Phakathi kwemiqondiso emininzi ekhankanywe nguMahapurush ukuba ifunyanwe kwiJagannath Temple ePuri ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, enye yayikukubona amachaphaza egazi kwiRatnasinghasana yezithixo zobuthixo.

Esi siprofeto esabhalwa nguMahapurush naso singqineke siyinyani. Kule minyaka imbalwa idlulileyo, amachaphaza egazi awele kwiindawo ezininzi etempileni, kuquka neRatnasinghasana, emva koko, ngokwesiko letempile, iChaturdha Murti zahlambululwa ngesiko leMahasnana (lokuhlamba okukhulu). Ngokomzekelo, nge-6 kaMatshi, 2021, igazi labonwa kufutshane ne-'Bhitar Katha' ebekwe kude kancinci kwindawo engcwele kakhulu yetempile. Nge-18 ka-Epreli, 2021, ngenxa yengxabano phakathi kwamapolisa amabini abekwe kukhuseleko lwethempile, amachaphaza egazi awa kwisakhiwo setempile. Nge-7 kaMatshi, 2022, ngexesha le-'Abakasha Niti' (isiko), amachaphaza egazi afunyanwa kufutshane ne-'Bhitar Katha' ngaphakathi kwiJagamohana yetempile (iholo eliphambi kwendawo engcwele kakhulu). Emva koko nge-11 ka-Epreli, 2022, amachaphaza egazi abonwa kufutshane neDhukudi Dwara yetempile. Nge-3 kaNovemba, 2022, emva kwe-'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', kunye ne-'Tadapa Lagi' amasiko, amachaphaza egazi abonwa kufutshane ne-'Bhitar Katha'. Isiganeko esinjalo sabonwa kwakhona kwiveki yesithathu kaJanuwari ngonyaka ka-2023. Emva kokuphela kwe-'Abakasha' kunye ne-'Besa Lagi' amasiko eNkosi Jagannath, amabala egazi abonwa kwiShrimandir, ngenxa yoko umnikelo (Bhoga) kuGopalaballabha awuzange ugqitywe. Emva koko nge-4 kaJuni, 2023, emva kokuphela kokuhlamba kobuthixo (Devasnana), amachaphaza egazi abonwa kwiSnana Mandapa. Nangoku kutshanje, nge-13 kaOkthobha, 2023, ngenxa yengxabano enobundlobongela phakathi kwabalungiseleli ababini malunga nokusasazwa kwePrasad ngoko nangoko emva kwe-'Madhyahna Dhupa' kwiShrimandir, amachaphaza egazi awa kwindawo engcwele kakhulu yetempile. Ngonyaka ka-2024, ekuseni nge-23 ka-Epreli, emva kokuba iSinghadwara ivuliwe, umfazi osuka kusapho lwabalungiseleli wabeka isandla sakhe emnyango wetempile xa umnwe wakhe wacinezelwa kuwo. Ngenxa yoko, igazi laphuma emnweni wakhe kufutshane neDhukudi Dwara ekufutshane neBedha yangaphakathi. Kwimeko enjalo, iMahasnana yeNkosi ePhakamileyo yenziwa nangaphambi kweMangala Alati. Ngokufanayo, iziganeko zokopha kwisakhiwo setempile zaqhubeka nangonyaka ka-2025.

7.2 Ukuwa kwesithixo seNkosi Balabhadra ngexesha leRath Yatra 2024.

Ngethuba leRath Yatra yeentsuku ezili-11 eShri Jagannath Puri, amasiko e-'Adapa Mandapa Bije' aqala ngo-7 PM ngorhatya lwe-9 kaJulayi, 2024. Ngeli xesha, ngelixa kuthathwa isithixo seNkosi Balabhadra kwinqwelo ukuya kwiAdapa Mandapa yeGundicha Temple, Yawa ngobuso phantsi kwiCharamala, ngenxa yoko abalungiseleli abali-12 nabo bonzakala. Isiganeko esinjalo esingelulo senzeka okokuqala kwimbali. Ukuwa kweNkosi Balabhadra ngale ndlela ngokuqinisekileyo ngumqondiso weentlekele ezizayo, ukukhankanywa kwazo kufumaneka ngolu hlobo kwisibhalo 'Niladri' esinxulumene nemithetho nemigaqo yeShrikhetra, njengoko yalelwe yiNkosi Brahma neNkosi Vishnu.

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, "O Kumkani, ukuba ngokungaqondanga uJagannath, uBalabhadra, okanye uSubhadra bawe, ngoko intlekele enkulu iya kuhlela ukumkani nelizwe. Indlala iya kwenzeka, kwaye izifo ezosulelayo ziya kubetha ngokuphindaphindiweyo."

Umlilo kwindawo engcwele kakhulu yeMahakal Temple, Ujjain ngonyaka ka-2024

Ngentsasa yoMvulo, nge-25 kaMatshi, 2024, ngexesha lomnyhadala weHoli, kwabakho umlilo kwindawo engcwele kakhulu yeMahakaleshwar Temple eUjjain ngenxa yokuphoswa kwe-'gulal' (umgubo onemibala) ngexesha leBhasma Aarti. Abantu abamalunga ne-14, kuquka umbingeleli oyintloko nabanye abalungiseleli, bafumana ukwenzakala kokutsha kwesi siganeko.

7.3 Imiqondiso emikhulu emibi efumaneka kwiJagannath Temple ngonyaka ka-2025

Imiqondiso emibi efumaneka kwiJagannath Temple yaqhubeka nangonyaka ka-2025. Ngemiqondiso emininzi, iNkosi Jagannath yanika abazinikeleyo bayo isilumkiso sangaphambili seentlekele ezizayo, eminye yemiqondiso ephambili esiyibeka phambi kwenu.

(a) Ukuqhekeka kweAkshaya Danda nge-14 kaFebruwari

Njengosuku ngalunye, emva kwemini nge-14 kaFebruwari, 2025 (ngoLwesihlanu), xa umChunara servitor wafika kwiNeelachakra phezu kwetempile ukuze atshintshe iPatitapabana Bana okanye iflegi ephambili, wafumana iAkshaya Danda (isibonda soqalo esibambe iPatitapabana Bana) iqhekekile. Emva koko, abalungiseleli ngandlela thile babopha iflegi kwiNeelachakra ngokwayo.

Ukuqhekeka kweAkshaya Danda ngale ndlela kwenzakalisa iimvakalelo zabazinikeleyo abangenakubalwa, kwaye bavakalisa usizi olukhulu ngayo. Kunye noku, abazinikeleyo abaninzi bakubiza oku njengomqondiso weentlekele ezizayo kungekudala.

(b) Amachaphaza egazi afunyenwe etempileni nge-16 kaFebruwari; iMahasnana yezithixo zenziwa

NgoFebruwari 16 (iCawa), emva kokugqitywa kwezithethe zasekuseni ezifana ne-'Dwara Phita Niti' kunye ne-'Mangala Alati', ngokukhawuleza xa iDarshan yeNkosi uJagannath yaqala, emva kwexesha elifutshane, abalungiseleli bafumana amabala egazi kufutshane nendawo ye-'Bhitar Katha'. Ngokukhawuleza emva kwesi siganeko, kwenziwa amalungiselelo e-'Bada Mahasnana Niti' yezithixo, kwaye iDarshan nayo yanqunyanyiswa okwethutyana.

(c) Ukhozi lubhabha luhamba nePatitapabana Bana ngoAprili 13

NgoAprili 13, 2025, abazinikeleyo beNkosi uJagannath abafumaneka kwiPuri Shrimandir babona umqondiso ongazange ubonwe ngaphambili. Malunga ne-5 PM, kwabonwa ukhozi lujikeleza itempile luthwele iPatitapabana Bana ngomlomo walo. Ngokweengxelo, ukhozi lwajikeleza itempile malunga ne-4-5 izihlandlo lwaza lwabhabha lusingise elwandle ngaphezu kwesango lasentshona.

Ividiyo yesi siganeko yasasazeka kakhulu kwimidiya yoluntu. Abaninzi abazinikeleyo bavakalisa usizi kunye neenkxalabo zabo ngoku. Abaninzi abazinikeleyo bakudibanisa neentlekele ezizayo, kwaye abambalwa bakubiza umqondiso ombi ongazange ubonwe ngaphambili onikwe yiNkosi uJagannath.

(d) Amabala egazi afunyenwe etempileni ngoAprili 21 kunye neMahasnana yezithixo eyenziweyo

NgoAprili 21 (uMvulo) nako, kwabonwa amabala egazi kufutshane neBhitar Katha eShrimandir, emva koko iMahasnana yezithixo zeNkosi yenziwa ngokufanelekileyo ngokwesiko. Okwangoku, iDarshan yeNkosi ePhakamileyo yabazinikeleyo yanqunyanyiswa malunga neyure eli-1. Ngokweengxelo, amabala egazi afunyenwe ngaphakathi etempileni ngenxa yamaqhekeza eglasi agqobhoza unyawo lwephoyisa.

(e) Ukhozi luhleli phezu kwePatitapabana Bana ngoSeptemba 2

Ukhozi oluhleli kwiPatitapabana Bana phezu kweShrimandir lubonwe izihlandlo ezininzi kule minyaka imbalwa idlulileyo. Ngalo lonke ixesha kubonwa isiganeko esinjalo, siboniswa phantse kuzo zonke iitshaneli zeendaba zasekhaya e-Odisha. NgoSeptemba 2, kwenzeka kanye into efanayo kwakhona xa ukhozi lwabonwa lubhabha kufutshane neNeelachakra kwaye emva kwexesha kwafunyanwa luhleli kwiPatitapabana Bana encanyathiselwe phezu kweNeelachakra.

(f) Ukhozi luhleli kwiAruna Stambha kwakhona ngoSeptemba 4

Ayisiyiyo nje iTempile yaseJagannath, iBhavishya Malika ikwachaza ukuhlala kokhozi okanye nayiphi na enye intaka embi kwiAruna Stambha efumaneka phambi kweSinghadwara yetempile njengomqondiso ombi. NgoSeptemba 4, oko kukuthi, kwiintsuku ezimbini emva kokuba ukhozi lubonwe phezu kweShrimandir ngoSeptemba 2, kwabonwa nako ukhozi luhleli kwiAruna Stambha. Abantu bakubona oku njengomqondiso ombi. Ngesi siganeko, abaninzi bachaza isiprofeto seMalika esabhalwa kwiminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo njengesizalisekayo.

(g) Ukhozi luhleli phezu kwetempile kwakhona ngoSeptemba 16

Kwiintsuku nje ezili-15 emva koSeptemba 2, kusasa ngoSeptemba 16, kwabonwa kwakhona ukhozi luhleli phezu kwePatitapabana Bana yetempile.

(h) Ukhozi lufunyenwe luhleli kwiPatitapabana Bana yeShrimandir kwakhona ngoOktobha 14

Ukhozi lwafunyanwa luhleli phezu kwetempile kabini ngenyanga kaSeptemba. Ulandelelwano aluphelelanga apha; abahlali basePuri babona ukuphindaphindwa kwesi siganeko kwakhona ngoOktobha 14. Ukuhlala okuphindaphindiweyo kokhozi phezu kwetempile kwachazwa ngabazinikeleyo abaninzi njengomqondiso weentlekele ezizayo.

(i) Amaxhama angenakubalwa ajikeleza phezu kweShrimandir ngoDisemba 9

Kanye njengangoDisemba 7, 2021, nangoAgasti 17, 2022, umqondiso ombi kakhulu wabonwa nangoDisemba 9, 2025, xa kwafunyanwa amaxhama angenakubalwa ejikeleza phezu kweTempile yaseJagannath. Esi siganeko satsala ingqalelo hayi nje yabazinikeleyo basePuri okanye e-Odisha, kodwa nabo bahlala ngaphandle kwe-Odisha. Abaninzi abazinikeleyo basibiza esi siganeko umqondiso wesiganeko esikhulu esingalindelekanga esiza kwenzeka kungekudala.

7.4 Imiqondiso emibi efumaneke kwezinye iimonastri ezibalulekileyo kunye neetempile ngonyaka ka-2025

Kuqhambuka umlilo kwisakhiwo seTempile yaseMahakaleshwar e-Ujjain

NgoMeyi 5, 2025, nge-12 PM, kuqhambuke umlilo kwigumbi lokulawula lebhodi yolawulo longcoliseko elikufutshane neShankh Dwara kwisakhiwo seTempile yaseBaba Mahakaleshwar edume kwihlabathi e-Ujjain. Emva kwesi siganeko, kwabakho uloyiko kwisakhiwo setempile. Emva kokuqhambuka komlilo, ukungena kwabazinikeleyo etempileni kwanqunyanyiswa okwethutyana.

Kuqhambuka umlilo kwisakhiwo sePeetha sikaMahapurush Achyutananda Das

Ngosuku lweDevasnana Purnima, ngoJuni 11, 2025, umlilo oqhambuke kwisakhiwo seNemal Padmabana Peetha sikaMahapurush Achyutananda Das esiseNischintakoili, Cuttack wabonwa njengomqondiso ombi kakhulu ngabazinikeleyo beMalika. Umoya woloyiko waba khona phakathi kwesihlwele samawaka abazinikeleyo xa isibane esigcinwe kwisakhiwo sakhawuleza sabamba umlilo, kwaye ngaphakathi kwemizuzwana, kwaqala ukuqhambuka amadangatye avuthayo.

Umthi weNemal banyan unqunyulwe lulawulo

Ekuqaleni kukaAgasti, uninzi lweziqu zomthi we-banyan omdala kakhulu phambi kwetempile efumaneka kwiSadhana Peetha yombhali oyintloko wemibhalo yeMalika, ingcwele enkulu uShri Achyutananda Das, zanqanyulwa. Kuthiwa, eli nyathelo lathathwa lulawulo ngenxa yomsebenzi wophuhliso lweAchyutananda Das Peetha. Kwakukho inkcaso ebanzi koku ngabantu balapho kunye nababingeleli, kwaye emva kokuchasa kwabo, kwashiyeka amasebe ambalwa nje omthi we-banyan engacuthwanga. Kufuneka kuqatshelwe ukuba uMahapurush Achyutananda Das waqulunqa imibhalo yeMalika ngelixa ehleli phantsi kwe-'Nemal Banyan Tree' ekwimeko yokucamngca. Ukucuthwa komthi weNemal banyan kwabonwa njengomqondiso ombi kakhulu ngabazinikeleyo beMalika.

Iflegi yegolide efakwe encotsheni yeTempile yaseMahakaleshwar iyawa

NgoJuni 1, 2025, umqondiso ombi wabonwa kwakhona kwiTempile yaseMahakaleshwar edume kwihlabathi efumaneka e-Ujjain. Ngapha koko, ngenxa yemimoya enamandla, iflegi yegolide efakwe encotsheni yeTempile yaseMahakaleshwar yawa ngesiquphe. Umbhisholo wetempile wasichaza esi siganeko njengento ekhathazayo ngokwenkolo.

Isigqubuthelo sobuso beNkosi uShiva esenziwe kwiShivalinga kwiTempile yaseMahakaleshwar siyaqhekeza siwe

Isiganeko esingaqhelekanga senzeka ngoAgasti 18 kwiTempile yaseMahakal efumaneka e-Ujjain. Apha, njengosuku ngalunye nge-8:00 PM ngexesha lokuhombisa (iShringara) yeShivalinga, kwenziwa isigqubuthelo sobuso beNkosi uShiva ngeBhang (intlama yecannabis). Ubungakanani obukhulu beBhang bafakwa ngeli xesha. Ngokukhawuleza emva koku, iAarti yayiza kuqala xa iBhang Shringara yaqhekezeka yawa. Impumlo, imilebe, kunye neliso elinye zaqhekezeka kwisigqubuthelo sobuso beNkosi uShiva zawa.

Umbono ongaphaya kwendalo obonwe kwiTempile yaseMaa Samaleswari

KwiTempile edumileyo yaseMaa Samaleswari efumaneka eLakhanpur, Jharsuguda, Odisha, ngobusuku boLwesine, ngoAgasti 21, 2025, abazinikeleyo bafumana umqondiso woThixokazi eshiya isithixo Sakhe ephuma. Xa umbhisholo wavula iingcango zetempile kusasa ngexesha le-'Mangala Alati', kwabonwa iinyawo ezisinga ngaphandle, ezipeyintwe ngeAlta (idayi ebomvu), ezizezoThixokazi, kwaye kunye noku, ikrele Lakhe (iKhanda) kunye ne-trident (iTrishul), ezazinamathele kwisithixo soThixokazi, zafunyanwa ziwele ecaleni.

Ngokwe-'Bhavishya Malika', kwisigaba sokugqibela seKali Yuga, phambi kokusekwa kweDharma, bonke oThixokazi baya kushiya iindawo zabo baphume ukuze benze umsebenzi wokutshabalalisa abangendawo naboni. Esi siganeko bubungqina bokuba elo xesha lifikile kwaye eso siprofeto siyazaliseka.

Kuqhume umlilo kwizithixo ezingcwele kwiTempile yaseBrahmagiri Jagannath

Ngo-Agasti 28, 2025, izithixo zeNkosi uJagannath, umkhuluwa uBalabhadra, kunye nomama uSubhadra zabamba umlilo kwiTempile yaseBrahmagiri Jagannath ekwilali yaseKandagoda phantsi kwesithili sasePuri e-Odisha. Ngokwenene, emva kokugqitywa konqulo lwangokuhlwa, ababingeleli babuyela emakhaya abo. Ngo-8:30 PM, ngenxa yomsi owawuphuma etempileni, xa kwaphulwa umnyango wetempile, kwabonakala ukuba izithixo ngaphakathi zazivutha umlilo. Emva kokubonisana neMuktimandapa, iShaligrama yaqala ukunqulwa ukusukela ngaloo mini. Emva kokuqokelelwa kweDaru (inkuni engcwele) kwiNabakalebara ezayo, kwafakwa izithixo ezintsha.

Uphawu lwentshabalalo ezayo olufunyenwe kwiTempile yaseLingaraj, isibonda seflegi setempile siyaphuka

NgoMvulo, Septemba 15, 2025, ngenxa yomsindo weemvula ezinkulu nomoya, isibonda seflegi seTempile yaseLingaraj eyakhiwe ngenkulungwane ye-11, ekwiBhubaneswar, saphuka. NgoLwesibini kusasa, emva kokuba sibonwe ngabakhonzi, kwanyuswa iflegi kwisibonda esitsha.

Iflegi esincotsheni yeTempile yaseLingaraj ekwiBhubaneswar ikrazuka ngenxa yemvula enkulu nesiphango. Indawo engcwele kakhulu ichaphazeleka kukubethwa yimbane; ubumnyama bugubungela itempile.

Phakathi kweemvula ezinkulu nesiphango esinzima ngoLwesithathu, Oktobha 8, 2025, iTempile yaseLingaraj ekwikomkhulu lase-Odisha, iBhubaneswar, yaba lixhoba lokubethwa yimbane. Nalo yayiluphawu lwesiganeko esingalunganga. Iflegi yetempile yakrazuka ngenxa yesiphango, ngelixa iikhamera zeCCTV zasetempileni kunye nenkqubo yokukhanyisa zonakaliswa kukubethwa yimbane. Kwaxelwa ukuba abanye abazinikeleyo nabakhonzi ababekhona etempileni nabo bafumana ukothuka kombane.

(j) Okokuqala kwimbali yayo yeminyaka engama-500, iBal Bhog ayinikelwanga kwiTempile yaseBanke Bihari eVrindavan

UThakur Banke Bihari, owabonakala malunga neminyaka engamakhulu amahlanu eyadlulayo, unikwa iBhog (umnikelo wokutya) ngamaxesha amane osuku. Phambi kokuba kubekwe isihlalo seDarshan yasekuseni, kunikelwa iBal Bhog. Phambi kokuba itempile ivalwe emva kwemini, kunikelwa iRaj Bhog, kwaye xa iminyango ivulwa ngokuhlwa, kunikelwa iUtthapan Bhog. Xa iminyango ivalwa, kunikelwa iShayan Bhog. Isivumelwano sokulungisa iBhog sinikwa umenzi weeswiti (Halwai). Umenzi weeswiti ulungisa iBhog ayinike abakhonzi, kwaye bona banikezela iBhog.

Kodwa ngoDisemba 15 ngo-8:45 AM, iminyango yetempile yavulwa, kodwa umenzi weeswiti akazange afike neBal Bhog. Xa kuqhagamshelwene naye, kwafunyaniswa ukuba umenzi weeswiti akazange afike ngenxa yokungahlawulwa kwemali yenyanga. Okokuqala kwimbali yayo yeminyaka engama-500, into enjalo yenzeka xa iBal Bhog ingazange inikelwe kuBanke Bihari Ji ngaloo mini.

7.5 Ukuququzelela kweRath Yatra ngexesha elingafanelekanga

Njengoko sonke sisazi, iRath Yatra iququzelelwa minyaka le ngeDwitiya Tithi yeShukla Paksha yenyanga yeAshadha. Esi siko besisoloko silandelwa kwezinye iitempile zaseJagannath eIndiya nakwamanye amazwe, kuquka iTempile yaseJagannath e-Puri. Kodwa kule minyaka imbalwa idlulileyo, kuye kwakho amaxesha amaninzi apho i-ISKCON ibonwe isophula esi siko.

Kulo mba, emva kokuba kuphakanyiswe izichaso yiNtloko yoMlawuli woLawulo lweTempile yaseShree Jagannath kunye noSihlalo weKomiti yoLawulo lweTempile yaseShree Jagannath, uGajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva, kwaye kwenziwa isikhalazo esibhaliweyo kwikomkhulu le-ISKCON eliseMayapur, ngokuqinisekileyo kwenzeka uphuculo oluthile, kodwa i-ISKCON isabonwa iququzelela iiRath Yatra ezingekho ngexesha eIndiya kwaye ngakumbi kwamanye amazwe.

Kolu luhlu, i-ISKCON kutshanje iququzelele iRath Yatra eTaiwan ngoNovemba 23, 2025, eyagxekwa luluntu lwase-Odia kwihlabathi jikelele kunye nabazinikeleyo abaninzi bakaJagannath base-Odisha. Abazinikeleyo bayithatha njengokungaphuli nje kwemithetho yesiko kodwa bayibona njengokungahoywa komyalelo weNkosi uJagannath ngokwakhe.


7.6 Impazamo kwikhalenda yesiNgesi ka-2026 eprintwe yiShrimandir Administration

Kutshanje, impazamo enkulu iye yabonakala kwikhalenda yesiNgesi ka-2026 ekhutshwe yiJagannath Temple Administration. Ngokwenene, ifoto yeRatnabedi iprintwe kwikhalenda apho ulandelelwano lweTrinity lungachanekanga. Ngokwembono yombukeli, kwifoto yekhalenda, iNkosi uJagannath ibekwe ngasekhohlo kakhulu, emva koko uThixokazi uSubhadra, emva koko umkhuluwa uBalabhadra, ngelixa kulandelelwano oluchanekileyo, iNkosi uBalabhadra kufuneka ibe ngasekhohlo kakhulu, emva koko uThixokazi uSubhadra, emva koko uShri Jagannath. Oko kukuthi, impazamo kukuba kwikhalenda, ifoto yomkhuluwa uBalabhadra iprintwe endaweni yeNkosi uJagannath, kwaye ifoto yeNkosi uJagannath ikwindawo yeNkosi uBalabhadra. Ukongeza koku, uChakraraj Sudarshan akazange anikwe indawo kwifoto yeRatnasinghasana kwikhalenda. Ayipheleli apho, ifoto yeRath Yatra nayo iprintwe kwikhalenda, ebonisa inqwelo kaMama uSubhadra kuqala, ilandelwe yinqwelo yeNkosi ePhakamileyo uJagannath, kwaye ekugqibeleni inqwelo yeNkosi uBalabhadra.

Ngenxa yezi mpazamo, kukho ukunganeliseki kunye nenzondo ezingqondweni zabazinikeleyo. Kukwakho nengqumbo phakathi kwabakhonzi beTempile yaseJagannath malunga nesi siganeko. Ngokwabanye abazinikeleyo, olu luphawu olunikezwe yiNkosi uJagannath ngokwakhe lweentsizi ezizayo kunye notshintsho kulungelelwano lwehlabathi kunyaka omtsha.

8 Ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki – Nini kwaye Phi?

NgokweBhavishya Malika, iNkosi uKalki sele izelwe. Ayisiyiyo nje iNkosi uKalki, kodwa bonke amajoni angamagorha exesha leMahabharata afana noYudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, njl., bonke bazalwe ngokutsha kulo mhlaba. NgokweMalika, ngeli xesha onke la majoni aya kulwa kunye kwicala leDharma ngokuchasene neAdharma.

Nangona kunjalo, asiyi kuxoxa ngamanye amajoni apha kwaye siya kunciphisa amazwi ethu kuphela kuParahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Nkosi uKalki Ram.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa iPatitapabana Bana (iflegi) yeTempile yasePuri Jagannath iphaphazela isuka kwindawo yayo iye kwenye indawo, ngelo xesha linye, uMahaprabhu Kalki uya kuzalwa eJajanagar okanye eJajpur yase-Odisha endlwini yeBrahmin eculela iNkosi uVishnu.
