Ndifanele ndicacisele abafundi ukuba esi siganeko sibonwe amaxesha amaninzi kwiTempile yaseJagannath, umzekelo ngonyaka ka-1990-91, ngonyaka ka-2005, emva koko ngexesha leSikhukula iFani kunye neSikhukula iAmphan ngeminyaka ka-2019 no-2020 ngokulandelelanayo, njl.

UMahapurush wabhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ukuba ukuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu Kalki kuya kwenzeka kuphela emva kwesi siganeko. Ekubeni esi siganeko senzekile, kungatshiwo ngokuqinisekileyo ukuba iNkosi uKalki izelwe. Kodwa ekubeni esi siganeko senzeke kwiminyaka emininzi eyahlukeneyo, akubonakali kunokwenzeka ukufikelela kunyaka othile wokuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu kwesi siganeko kuphela.

Ukuze sazi ukuba uMahaprabhu wazalwa ngaluphi unyaka, kuya kufuneka sisebenzise ezinye iivesi zeMalika.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Intsingiselo:
Ngeevesi ezingentla, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das uxelela umfundi wakhe uRamadas ukuba xa ukumkani ogama linguShri Dibyasingha Deb elawula e-Puri, ngelo xesha uAnanta Keshari, oko kukuthi, uMahaprabhu Kalki, uya kuba nolawulo lwakhe e-Ekamra Nagar okanye eBhubaneswar. Kuya kuba lusuku lwesixhenxe (Saptami Tithi) lwenyanga kaMagha. UMahapurush uqhubeka esithi ngelo xesha, nam ndiya kuhlala neNkosi ndikhule. Oku kuya kwenzeka xa ukumkani waseVisakhapatnam (oko kukuthi, iNkulumbuso yase-Andhra Pradesh) esifa.

Ukuba siqwalasela ngokusondeleyo, siya kufumanisa ukuba ngophawu, uMahapurush uthetha ngokufa kweNkulumbuso yase-Andhra Pradesh, uY.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, okwenzeka kwingozi yehelikopta ngonyaka ka-2009. Kwiivesi ezingentla, uMahapurush akaxeli unyaka wokuzalwa kweNkosi, kodwa ngokuqinisekileyo uthi malunga nonyaka ka-2009, izenzo (Leelas) zikaKalki iya kuqhubeka.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwi‘Jaiphula Malika’ yakhe –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi efanayo eyayifike ngaphambili njengoShri Krishna noShri Chaitanya, iya kuza kwihlabathi labantu ngendlela kaKalki kwinani lama-47.

Kuyaziwa kakuhle ukuba eli nani lama-47 lelo Kumkani wasePuri, uShri Dibyasingha Deb, eliwela phakathi kweminyaka ka-2005 no-2008.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwisicatshulwa sakhe seMalika ‘Kalpatika’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, uMahaprabhu ngokuqinisekileyo uya kuthatha ukuzalwa emhlabeni emva kokushiya indawo yakhe yaseJagannath. Ngelixa echaza unyaka wokuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu, uMahapurush uthi xa udibanisa amashumi amabini (20) kwinani leRevati Nakshatra (27), ke inani eliphuma kufuneka liqondwe njengonyaka wokuzalwa kweNkosi. Oko kukuthi, 20+27 = 47.

Kuyaziwa kakuhle ukuba eli nani ngunyaka wolawulo (Anka) woKumkani wasePuri, uDibyasingha Deb, eliqondisa kwenye yeminyaka phakathi kuka-2005 no-2008.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona kwesinye sezicatshulwa sakhe seMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa kungunyaka wama-45 kaMaharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wasePuri, ke iNkosi uShri Hari iya kuthatha ukuzalwa kukaKalki ihle emhlabeni.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba unyaka wama-45 kaMaharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wasePuri ukhomba kunyaka ophakathi kuka-2005 no-2008.

U-Achyutananda Das Ji wabhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe iMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, kuya kubakho ukungabikho kobulungisa okukhulu ehlabathini. Akukho mntu uya kuba neengcinga zentsapho okanye alandele iDharma. Kuya kubakho isiphithiphithi kuyo yonke indawo; akukho mntu uya kuchitha iintsuku zakhe ngoxolo nolonwabo. Ngelo xesha, kunyaka wama-45 wokumkani ogama linguDibyasingha Deb (phakathi kuka-2005-2008), iNkosi uShri Hari iya kuhla kulo mhlaba ofayo ngohlobo lukaKalki. Uya kususa umthwalo wesono emhlabeni, atshabalalise abangendawo, kwaye atshabalalise 'iiMlechhas'.

IiMahapurushas zikhankanye ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki kwiindawo ezininzi kwiincwadi ezininzi.

Kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, uMlambo iBaitarani uqukuqela kufutshane neTempile yaseBiraja ebekwe kwisithili saseJajpur eOdisha. Kufutshane neDashashwamedha Ghat yalo Mlambo iBaitarani kukho itempile yeSaptamatruka, apho oothixokazi abasixhenxe abangoomama—okanye uChamunda, uVarahi, uIndrani, uVaishnavi, uBrahmani, uKaumari, uMaheshwari, noNarasimhi—bakho.

UMahapurush ubhala ukuba xa igazi liphuma emilebeni, oko kukuthi, emilonyeni, yooMama beSaptamatruka, kuya kuba luphawu ekufuneka abazinikeleyo beqonda ngalo ukuba iNkosi uKalki izelwe okanye ukuzalwa kwayo kuseduze kakhulu.

Kuyaziwa kakuhle ukuba esi siganeko sele senzekile kwiTempile yaseSaptamatruka, singqina isiprofeto sikaMahapurush Achyutananda Das siyinyani.

Ngokutsho kwabefundisi beTempile yaseBiraja, eJajpur, neTempile yaseSaptamatruka, kunye nabantu balapho, ukuphuma kwegazi emilonyeni yooMama beSaptamatruka sele kwenzekile ngonyaka ka-2005.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Intsingiselo:
Apha, uMahapurush uchaza ixesha lokuzalwa kweMahaprabhu ngolwimi olungumfuziselo. Ubhala ukuba ngosuku uMaa Biraja eshiya isihlalo sakhe, ngelo xesha umntwana (iNkosi uKalki) uya kungena esibelekweni sikanina, oya kuthi kamva aye eBhubaneswar.

Kuyaziwa kakuhle ukuba uMaa Biraja wasishiya isihlalo sakhe ngonyaka ka-2004. Ngenyanga kaSeptemba 2004, abanye abantu bezopolitiko baqulunqa icebo baza beba umfanekiso kaMaa Biraja (ofakwe kwiTempile yaseBiraja, eJajpur). Emva kweenyanga ezi-6, ngoMatshi 2005, umfanekiso kaMama wabuyela etempileni.

Kwiindinyana ezintandathu ezingentla, kubonakala ngokucacileyo ukuba uMahaprabhu Kalki uzelwe, kwaye ixesha lakhe lokuvela liphakathi kweminyaka ka-2005-2008. Ngoko ke, bonke abafundi mabacace ukuba iKalki avatar ikhona ngoku eMhlabeni.

8.1 Uzalelwe phi uMahaprabhu?

Ngokutsho kweePuranas, indawo yokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki yidolophana yaseSambhal. IBhavishya Malika ikwathi iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa kwidolophana yaseSambhal. Wonke umntu uyavumelana ngale ngongoma.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ebona intlungu yabazinikeleyo kwincopho yexesha leKali, iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa eSambal Nagar. Igama likayise weMahaprabhu liya kuba nguVishnusharma kwaye igama likanina liya kuba nguSumati.

UMahapurush wabhala ngakumbi ku‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, uMahaprabhu Kalki uya kuzalelwa ngokufihlakeleyo eSambal Nagar. Umzabalazo okanye imfazwe yasemini nobusuku iya kuqhambuka eChhatia Bata.

U-Achyutananda Das Ji wabhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa ukufa, indlala, kunye neerhafu zingena kuyo yonke imizi kwaye isiphithiphithi sisasazeka kuyo yonke indawo, iNkosi uKalki iya kuvela eSambal Nagar ngelo xesha lokugqibela leKaliyuga.

UMahapurush wabhala ngakumbi ku‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Intsingiselo:
"Xa ixesha leKaliyuga lifikelela esiphelweni, uKalki uya kuzalelwa endlwini kaVishnuyasha kwidolophana yaseSambal ukuze atshabalalise iiMlechhas."

Nangona kunjalo, apho i-'Sambal' ikhona sisihloko sokungavisisani kwiingqondo zabantu abaninzi nanamhlanje. Kweli nqaku, siya kusombulula oku.

8.2 Ithini ngokwenene iBhavishya Malika ngalo mbandela?

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngelo xesha xa isono sisesona siphezulu, iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa kwiphondo laseOdisha. Inkohliso yeNkosi iya kuba namandla kangangokuba akukho mntu uya kukwazi ukuyibona.

UMahapurush uphinda abhale kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Intsingiselo:
"Ukuzalwa kukaKalki kuya kuba kumhlaba waseUtkala (Odisha), kwidolophana egama linguRajahansa. Emva kokuzalwa, uKalki uya kubonakalisa imidlalo yakhe engcwele."

Ngoku, malunga nokuba uya kuzalelwa phi eOdisha iNkosi uKalki, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ngokwakhe wabhala kwenye incwadi yeMalika, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, indawo yokuzalwa kweMahaprabhu iya kuba kufutshane nonxweme lweGupta Ganga (uMlambo iBaitarani) eSambal Nagar, eOdisha. Kweso sithuba, endlwini yeBrahmin olungileyo ocula iindumiso zeNkosi uVishnu, uMahaprabhu Kalki uya kuzalwa.

Kwiindinyana zombini ezingentla, kucacile ngokupheleleyo ukuba ngokutsho kweBhavishya Malika, iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa eSambal, ekwiphondo laseIndiya laseOdisha.

8.3 Iphi iSambal eOdisha?

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Jagannath Das, wabhala kwincwadi iMalika ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Intsingiselo:
Kwindinyana engentla, uMahapurush ubhala – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Oko kukuthi, “ESambal Nagar yaseJajpur”. Kucacile ukuba iSambal Nagar ikhona eJajpur.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, emazantsi omlambo iKhira nasemantla eMahanadi kukho indawo yetempile yaseBiraja. Kufutshane nalo mmandla waseBiraja kukho idolophana eyaziwa ngokuba yiKamalapur. Yile nto ibizwa ngokufihlakeleyo ngokuba yidolophana yaseSambal.

Ngale ndinyana, uMahapurush utyhile ukuba iSambal ikhona kummandla waseBiraja okanye kwisithili saseJajpur eOdisha.

Masiqonde ukuba iSambal ikuphi e-Odisha ngendlela yeevesi ezithile.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, uChakradhara (iNkosi uVishnu) uya kuzalelwa endlwini yomzingeli ohlala eSambal Nagar, eGaya, kwaye emva koko uya kwenza intlungu.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Intsingiselo:
"INkosi iya kuzalelwa endlwini kaVishnusharma, kwingingqi yaseHarihara yaseSambal Nagar, eGaya."

Ngokwezi vesi zombini zingasentla, ukuzalwa kweNkosi kuya kwenzeka eSambal Nagar yaseGaya. Ngoku, ukuba yeyiphi iGaya leyo kwaye ikuphi e-Odisha kwaxelelwa yiNkosi uKrishna ngokwakhe kumaGopas akhe alishumi elinambini, nto leyo uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wayicaphula kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. UMahapurush wabhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Intsingiselo:
"Yivani, nyana abalishumi elinambini, eli lilizwi eliqinisekileyo elivela kuAchyuta: ilali iSambalpur ikwingingqi yaseHarihara kwindawo engcwele yaseNabhigaya."

Oko kukuthi, ingingqi yaseHarihara ikwindawo engcwele yaseNabhigaya, ekuthiwa yiSambal.

Kwiingxoxo ezingasentla, sibone kwiindawo ezininzi ukuba uMahapurush wayichaza indawo yokuzalwa kaMahaprabhu njengengingqi yaseBiraja okanye indawo engcwele yaseGaya (Nabhigaya). Ngoku siza kwazi ukuba ezi ngingqi zikuphi kanye, ziziphi iinkolelo zasentsomini ezinxulumene nazo, kwaye yintoni intsingiselo yazo.

8.3.1 Ingingqi yaseBiraja

I-'Biraja Temple' yitempile enkulu ekwimida yoMlambo iBaitarani eJajpur, e-Odisha, apho i-Adi Shakti inqulwa ngendlela ye-'Biraja'. Ngokwenene, kumaxesha amandulo, uBrahma Ji ngokwakhe wenza iYajna (umbingelelo) apha eBrahmakunda, emva koko uMama uParvati waphuma emlilweni wombingelelo waza wayalela uBrahma Ji ukuba amise igama lakhe elithi 'Biraja'. Le ngingqi ikwabizwa ngokuba yingingqi yaseBiraja emva kwegama likaThixokazi uBiraja.

Kwakule ndawo apho inkaba (Nabhi) kaThixokazi uSati yawela khona, nto leyo eyenza ukuba ibe yenye yeeShakti Peethas ezingama-51. Le yitempile ekuphela kwayo apho uThixokazi anqulwa ngendlela kaMahishasura Mardini enengalo ezimbini.

Le ndawo ikwaziwa njengeNabhigaya ngePinda Daan (iminikelo yookhokho). Ngokwenene, yiyo kuphela iShakti Peetha apho kwenziwa khona iPinda Daan. Kufuphi neBrahmakunda, kukho iqula elinzulu elibizwa ngokuba yiNabhikunda, ngamanzi alo abantu benza iPinda Daan kubokhokho babo. Le ndawo ikwakhankanywe kwiSkanda Purana.

8.3.2 INabhigaya

Kukhankanywe kwiiPuranas ukuba kwiSatya Yuga, kwakukho idemon egama linguGayasura. Nangona wazalelwa kusapho lweedemon, intliziyo yakhe yaphefumlelwa yiDharma kunye nentlungu. Wayenqula kakhulu, enamandla kakhulu, kwaye enomzimba omkhulu. Wayesoloko esebenzela intlalo-ntle yehlabathi kunye nenzuzo yabanye. Kukhankanywe kwiiPuranas ukuba idemon yenza intlungu enzulu, apho iNkosi uVishnu yavuya khona, kwaye iNkosi yamcela ukuba afune isipho. UGayasura wacela isipho esingaqhelekanga. Wathi, “Izidalwa mazifikelele ezulwini ngokuchukumisa kwam nje.” INkosi uVishnu yasipha esi sipho ibona intlungu yakhe enzulu. Kodwa ngenxa yoko, iYamaloka (ihlabathi labafi) yaqala ukuba yinkangala, kuba nabani na uGayasura amchukumisayo waya ngqo ezulwini. Oku kwaba yingxaki enkulu koothixo nakuYamraj. Bonke oothixo basondela kwiTrinity (uBrahma, uVishnu, uMahesh) baza bacenga ukuba kufumaneke isisombululo kule ngxaki. ITrinity yagqiba ekubeni imise uGayasura, owayebangela izithintelo kwimithetho yendalo nangona elilungisa. Bobathathu bathatha uhlobo lwamaBrahmin amathathu, baya kuGayasura, baza bathi bafuna ukwenza iYajna ngokuqhubekayo iintsuku ezisixhenxe kwaye ngenxa yoku, babefuna indawo enkulu nengcwele. Bacela uGayasura ukuba anike umzimba wakhe kwiYajna, kodwa wayengafanele ashukume kwezi ntsuku zisixhenxe. UGayasura wavuma oku. UGayasura wasasaza intloko yakhe eGaya (Bihar) apho iNkosi uVishnu yayihleli khona, wabeka indawo yakhe yenkaba eNabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha) apho uBrahma Ji wayihleli khona, waza wabeka iinyawo zakhe ePithapuram (Andhra Pradesh) apho iNkosi uShiva yayihleli khona, kwaye ngaloo ndlela iYajna yaqala. ITrinity yenza iYajna ngokuqhubekayo iintsuku ezisixhenxe.

UGayasura waqhubeka ebala iintsuku ngokuva inkukhu ikhala kusasa. Ekwazi oku, ngosuku lokugqibela, iNkosi uShiva yathatha uhlobo lwenkukhu yaza yakhala ngaphambi kokuba ubusuku buphele. Akuva isandi senkukhu, uGayasura wacinga ukuba kusekuseni waza washukumisa umzimba wakhe ecinga ukuba iintsuku ezisixhenxe ziphelile. ITrinity yabonakala ngoko nangoko phambi kwakhe yaza yathi uGayasura waphule isithembiso sakhe sokugcina umzimba wakhe ungashukumi iintsuku ezisixhenxe. Bathi isohlwayo sokuphula isithembiso kukufa. UGayasura olilungisa wakwamkela oku ngaphandle kwesikhalazo. ITrinity yavuya ngobunqulo bakhe yaza yamcela ukuba afune isipho. Emva koko uGayasura wabacela ukuba igama lakhe likhunjulwe ecaleni kweTrinity. Wabacela ukuba bahombise umzimba wakhe ngokusisigxina ngeengubo zamaBrahmin. Wacela kwakhona isipho sokuba ukuba ookhokho bomntu banikwa iminikelo (iPinda Daan kunye neTarpan) kwezi ndawo zintathu, bangazalwa kwakhona.

Ekwamkeleni umnqweno kaGayasura, iNkosi uVishnu yathatha uhlobo lukaGadadhar eGaya, uBrahma Ji unqulwa ngendlela kaGirija Devi eNabhigaya, kwaye iNkosi uShiva inqulwa ePithapuram njengoKukkuteshwar (iNkosi eyathatha uhlobo lwenkukhu). Ngale ndlela, ezi ndawo zintathu zaduma ngePinda Daan kunye neTarpan:

  • IShirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • INabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • IPadagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Ubungqina bokuba indawo engcwele yaseNabhigaya ikuJajpur bukwafumaneka kwiincwadi zeMalika zePanchasakha Mahapurushas.

Kwincwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Intsingiselo:
"Yazi oku ngokuqinisekileyo, indawo engcwele yaseNabhigaya ikuJajpur."

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ngelixa eqikelela ukuzalwa kwakhe kweshumi malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo, wabhala kwincwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Intsingiselo:
"Ukuzalwa kwam kweshumi kuya kubonakala kwingingqi edumileyo yaseHarihara yaseNabhigaya, eJajpur."

Ngale vesi kwakhona, kucacile ukuba indawo engcwele yaseNabhigaya ikuJajpur.

UMahapurush ubhala ngalo mbandela mnye kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ingingqi yaseBiraja ikwindawo engangeekosha ezintlanu ukusuka eJajpur. INkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa endlwini yomzingeli kaVishnu ohlala kuloo ngingqi yaseBiraja, eNabhigaya.

Ngoko ke, kwingxoxo engasentla, kucacile ngokupheleleyo ukuba iSambal Nagar, indawo engcwele yaseGaya, kunye nengingqi yaseBiraja apho iiPanchasakhas zaqikelela ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki, ikuphakathi kwesithili saseJajpur sase-Odisha.

Ukuze sifumane ukucaca okungakumbi ngendawo yokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki, siza kuthatha ezinye iivesi ezikhankanywe kwiMalika.

UMahapurush Shishu Ananta Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iKalki Avatar iya kuzalwa kwiphondo lase-Odisha. UMahaprabhu Kalki uya kutshabalalisa abangendawo, ahlangule abazinikeleyo, aze aphinde amise iDharma eMhlabeni.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ecaphula iNkosi, ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa kubonwa ichaphaza okanye inkwenkwezi ngaphezu kwenyanga esibhakabhakeni, yazi ukuba ukubonakala kweNkosi ePhakamileyo kuya kwenzeka eJajpur.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, ubhala ngelixa ephinda amazwi eNkosi ngokuchanekileyo –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Intsingiselo:
"Xa ushiya iNilachala (Puri), uya kuya kuzalelwa endlwini yomBrahmin eJajpur. Lumka; musa ukuphulaphula nantoni na ethethwa ngumntu."

Kwincwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Tatwabodhini’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Intsingiselo:
"INkosi iya kuzalelwa eJajanagar; Iya kuzalwa njengoBrahmin."

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika egama lingu ‘Bigyanakalpa’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Intsingiselo:
"UMahaprabhu uya kuzalelwa eJajanagar; ethatha uGaruda kunye naye, uya kwenza umdlalo wakhe wobuthixo."

Kwiindinyana ezingentla, akusekho mathandabuzo okuba ukuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu Kalki kwakuqinisekile ukuba kuya kwenzeka eSambal Nagar, kwindawo engcwele yaseNabhigaya, kwingingqi yaseBiraja, kwisithili saseJajpur, eOdisha.

Ukuba sijonga iBhagavata Purana kunye neMahabharata ezabhalwa phambi kweBhavishya Malika, nazo zikhomba eJajpur, eOdisha malunga nendawo yokuzalwa yeNkosi uKalki.

Kumaxesha angaphambili, uMaharishi Vedavyas wachaza indawo yokuzalwa yeNkosi uKalki njenge 'Sambhal village' kwiShrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. Wayebhale wathi –

ISanskrit Sloka:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Intsingiselo:
"Endlwini yeBrahmin enomphefumlo omkhulu, inkosi yelali yaseSambhal, egama linguVishnuyasha, iNkosi uKalki iya kubonakala."

Kamva, kwi 'Vanaparva' yeMahabharata, uMaharishi Vyas ngokwakhe wayilungisa kancinci le nto waza wayibiza indawo yokuzalwa yeNkosi uKalki ngokuthi 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. Wabhalwa wathi –

ISanskrit Sloka:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Intsingiselo:
Ngeendinyana ezingentla, uMaharishi Vyas wachaza ukuba iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa e 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', okuthetha ilali yaseSambhal 'esekwe' okanye 'emisiweyo', ilali yaseSambhal eyayiza kusekwa njengekoloni yeeBrahmins zokwenza iiYajnas nokunqula. Wabhalwa ukuba iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa endlwini yeBrahmin enesidima eyayihlala kule lali yaseSambhal kwaye icula iingoma zokudumisa iNkosi uVishnu.

Ngoku, ukuba sihlola kwaye sikhangele kumaphepha embali, siya kufumanisa ukuba indawo yokuhlala yeeBrahmins ezasekwa ngeYajna nokunqula ikwisithili saseJajpur, eOdisha.

Enyanisweni, igama elithi Jajpur lanikwa ngenkulungwane ye-10 emva kwesihloko seKumkani yaseSomavamsi uJajati (Yayati). UKumkani uJajati waseka esi sixeko waza wenza iAbhinav Jajatinagar ikomkhulu lakhe. Kamva, etshintsha igama layo, yaqala yenziwa iJajanagar yaza yaba yiJajpur. UKumkani uJajati Keshari wayeze neBrahmins ezimalunga ne-10,000 ezivela eKannauj eUttar Pradesh waza wazihlala eJajpur, eOdisha, ukuze zenze iiAshwamedha Yajnas ezilishumi (Dashashwamedha). Nanamhlanje, iDashashwamedha Ghat ngaselunxwemeni loMlambo iBaitarani isikhumbuza ngesi siganeko. Ngapha koko, uMaharishi Vyas wayekhombe kule koloni yeeBrahmins ezihlala kwicala lasempuma letempile yaseBiraja eJajpur ngokuyibiza ngokuthi 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' kwi 'Vanaparva' yeMahabharata.

9 IKhandagiri – EBhubaneswar okanye kwenye indawo?

Nawuphi na umntu onomdla kwiBhavishya Malika akanalo naluphi na uthandabuzo malunga nokuba uMahaprabhu Kalki uya kuzalelwa phi. Bayazi kakuhle ukuba iKalki avatar iya kuzalelwa kwilali yaseSambal ekwiJajpur, eOdisha.

Abanalo naluphi na uthandabuzo lokuba emva kokuzalwa eJajpur, iNkosi iya kuhlala kwindawo egama linguKhandagiri.

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika egama lingu ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, eKaliyuga, iNkosi uHari iya kuhlala eKhandagiri kwiKalki incarnation yakhe.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwi ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ yakhe –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi iya kuhlala ngokufihlakeleyo eKhandagiri.

UMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala kwakhona kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika, i 'Patta Madana' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwindawo egama linguKhandagiri, kuya kubakho intlanganiso yabazinikeleyo. Apho, abazinikeleyo baya kuba ne-darshan (umbono) weNkosi uKalki kwaye baya kuqaphelana.

Ukuza kuthi ga ngoku, wonke umntu uyavuma, kodwa uthandabuzo lwabantu luvela malunga nokuba ikuphi le Khandagiri. Namhlanje, ngeevidiyo ezithile kunye neePDF, kubekwa amabango ahlukeneyo malunga neKhandagiri. Ngokwabanye abantu, le ndawo egama linguKhandagiri, apho iNkosi iya kuhlala khona, ikwiJajpur, eOdisha, ngelixa abanye besithi iKhandagiri ikwisithili saseDhenkanal, eOdisha.

9.1 Iphi iKhandagiri?

Ngalo eli nqaku, manditsho ukuba nawaphi na amagama akhankanywe ngentla malunga neKhandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), omabini ayilunganga ngokupheleleyo.

Echaza ukuba ikuphi iKhandagiri, omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yasovanta Das, wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika egama lingu ‘Basanta Manjari’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ubungangamsha beKhandagiri abuchazeki. Kukho iilakhs zabazinikeleyo apho. Izenzo zobuthixo ezifihlakeleyo (Leelas) iya kwenzeka eKhandagiri. Abaninzi abahlakaniphileyo nabamonki bazibandakanye ekuzileni ukutya apho. Umlambo ofihlakeleyo (Gupta Ganga) uqukuqela apho, oya kubangela intshabalalo eBhubaneswar ngexesha lomdlalo wokutshabalalisa kukaKalki. Ubungangamsha baloo Bhubaneswar nabo bukhulu kakhulu. Apho, uMama uBarabhuji kunye noMama uBhuasuni bakhona, abaya kwenza umsebenzi wokutshabalalisa ekupheleni kweKali Yuga.

Kwi-shloka engentla, uMahapurush ucacise ukuba iKhandagiri ikwiBhubaneswar.

Echaza indawo yeKhandagiri, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala nakwincwadi yakhe yeMalika egama lingu ‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho idabi eBhubaneswar, kwaye ihlathi laseEkamra likhona apho. Emva koko uMahapurush wachaza ukuba iKhandagiri nayo ikhona apho, apho abaninzi abahlakaniphileyo nabangcwele abagqibeleleyo baya kuhlala kwaye bahlale becula igama likaKalki kwaye bacamngce ngesimo sakhe. Kula maqhina aseKhandagiri, imini nobusuku kuya kwenzeka iibhajan kunye neekirtan. Apho, umlambo ofihlakeleyo (Kshira Nadi) nawo uya kuqukuqela. Loo Khandagiri iya kuba yi "Ndawo Yesiganeko" (Ghatana Sthana), apho idabi eliseleyo leMahabharata liya kwenzeka. Kufuphi yiUdayagiri, apho ii-ashram zabangcwele abaninzi abagqibeleleyo zikhona.

Nge-shloka engentla, uMahapurush ucacise ngokucacileyo ukuba iKhandagiri apho abangcwele abangenakubalwa baya kuhlala khona, apho ukucula okufihlakeleyo kwegama likaKalki kuya kuqhubeka emiqolombeni, kwaye apho idabi eliseleyo leMahabharata liya kwenzeka, ikwiBhubaneswar, apho ihlathi laseEkamra nalo likhona.

Apha ndingathanda ukutsalela ingqalelo yabafundi kwinto ebalulekileyo. Kwiincwadi zeMalika, iBhubaneswar ihlala ibizwa ngokuba yiEkamra Kshetra, iEkamra Van, okanye iEkamra Kanan. Enyanisweni, ngokwe-Ekamra Purana yenkulungwane ye-13, kwakukho umthi omnye kuphela wemango kufutshane nendawo kathixo olawulayo uLingaraj. Ngoko ke, indawo yaseLingaraj ikwabizwa ngokuba yiEkamra Kshetra (indawo enomthi wemango omnye).

Esi sisizathu sokuba iiMahapurushas zihlala zibiza iBhubaneswar ngokuba yiLingaraj Kshetra (indawo yetempile yaseLingaraj) okanye iEkamra Van Kshetra (indawo yomthi wemango omnye).

Ayisiyiyo le kuphela, ngenxa yendawo yeBindu Sarovar eBhubaneswar, iiPanchasakhas kwiincwadi zazo zeMalika ziye zabhekisa kwiBhubaneswar njengengingqi yeBindu Sarovar.

UMahapurush wabhala ecacisa kwi 'Garuda Gita' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Intsingiselo:
INkosi uKrishna ixelela umkhonzi wayo, uGaruda, ukuba iEkamra Kanan ekwiBhubaneswar yindawo efihlakeleyo negqibeleleyo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, abazinikeleyo beNkosi baya kuhlala kwingingqi yaseBhubaneswar Ekamra.

UMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iKhandagiri ikumda weBhubaneswar apho abazinikeleyo abangama-25 baya kuthatha inxaxheba kumdlalo wobuthixo.

UMahapurush ubhala ngakumbi kwi 'Chakada Madana' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho idabi kwindawo yaseKhandagiri eBhubaneswar.

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yasovanta Das, ubhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, yazi ukuba iBindu Sarovar ikwiBhubaneswar.

UMahapurush Yasovanta Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iBhubaneswar sisihlalo seLingaraj, oko kuthetha ukuba itempile yeLingaraj ikwiBhubaneswar.

Ukuba sisebenzisa uncedo lukaGoogle, nangona kunjalo sinokwazi ngokulula ukuba nokuba yiKhandagiri okanye iEkamra Kanan okanye iBindu Sarovar okanye itempile yeLingaraj, zonke zikwiBhubaneswar ngokwayo.

IKhandagiri, eya kuba likhaya leNkosi, apho abangcwele abaninzi baya kube becula igama likaKalki—ukucacisa ukuba le Khandagiri ikwiBhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra), uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona kwi ‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ezantsi kwentaba iKhandagiri kukho iBharata Paduka, ethandazelwa ngumngcwele ozifihlele. Kule Khandagiri inye, umlambo iGupta Ganga uqukuqela ngasese. Apho (eKhandagiri), abangcwele abaninzi abafezekileyo bahlala bezifihlele. Ihlathi laseEkamra likhona apho, eliyindawo yeYoga yeNkosi uShiva. Apho, intaka iShuka yakha yafumana ulwazi lweBrahma. Kufuphi kukho uThixokazi uBhuasuni, oya kwenza umsebenzi wokutshabalalisa eMhlabeni ekupheleni kweKali Yuga.

Ndingathanda ukuxelela abafundi apha ukuba kwenye yeendinyana ezingentla, uMahapurush wathi iKhandagiri ikwindawo yehlathi laseEkamra kwaye abangcwele abaninzi benza ukuzila ukutya eKhandagiri. Umlambo nawo uqukuqela apho ngasese. Kule ndinyana yangoku, uMahapurush uphinda into efanayo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona kwi ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ yakhe –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Apha uMahapurush waphinda wathi ikhaya leNkosi yiBhubaneswar. Kodwa akazange athabathe igama elithi Bhubaneswar okanye iKhandagiri. UMahapurush wayibiza indawo efanayo ngokuthi ‘Ekamra Van’. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba izenzo ezingcwele zeNkosi (Leelamaya) iya kubonakala kwindawo yaseEkamra Van. Kule ndawo inye yaseEkamra Van, iNkosi uAnanta Madhava, oko kukuthi, uKalki, uya kwenza izenzo ezingcwele, ukubona nokuyiva kuya kwenza uvuyo lwabakhonzi lungabi namida.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwakhona kwi ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ yakhe –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi iya kuphuma isiya ekutshabalaliseni ngehashe elithi “Kala-Dhala” okanye elimnyama namhlophe. Ngaphambi koko, iNkosi iya kuhlala eBhubaneswar. Bonke oosiyazi nabamonki abenza ukuzila ukutya kwindawo efezekileyo yaseKhandagiri baya kuphuma emiqolombeni ngelo xesha, bememezela igama leNkosi, kwaye baya kuhlanganisana eBindu Sarovar ekwiBhubaneswar. Emva koko, abapheki baya kudibana nabo bonke kwitempile ekufuphi yaseKapileshwar.

Kwindinyana engentla, iKhandagiri ikwabizwa ngokuba yindawo efezekileyo eya kuba likhaya loosiyazi nabamonki abaninzi. Kwaye kukwatshiwo ukuba nokuba yiKhandagiri okanye iBindu Sarovar okanye iKapileshwar Temple, zonke ezi zikwiBhubaneswar kwaye zikufuphi omnye komnye.

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, xa ebuzwa ngumfundi wakhe uBaranga Das ukuba iNkosi uKalki iya kuzalelwa phi kwaye iya kuhlala phi, ucacisa kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi iya kuzalelwa endlwini yomkhonzi ogama linguAnanta Mishra kwiShri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur). Emva kokuzalwa, iNkosi iya kushiya ikhaya layo iye kwindawo yabangcwele abafezekileyo ukuze yenze ukuzila ukutya. UMahapurush akazange ayeke nje ekutshweni “kwendawo efezekileyo,” wakwatsho ukuba igama lendawo leyo liya kuba yiKhandagiri, eya kuba kwiEkamra Kanan. Kule ndawo inye yaseKhandagiri yeEkamra Kanan, iNkosi iya kuhlala nabakhonzi bayo. UMahapurush wathi itempile yeShri Ananta Vasudev iya kuba khona, kwaye iLinga, oko kukuthi, itempile yeLingaraj, iya kuba khona. INkosi iya kuhlamba eBindu Sagar kwaye iya kutyelela itempile yeSiddha Kedara (Kedareshwar).

UMahapurush wakwatsho ukuba bonke abakhonzi baya kuhlamba kule Bindu Sarovar inye, emva koko ukukhanya okungcwele kuya kubonakala kubakhonzi, kwaye bonke baya kukhululeka kubumdaka beKali Yuga.

Ngendinyana engentla, uSishu Ananta Dasji wacacisa ukuba apho ikhona itempile yeShri Ananta Vasudev, apho ikhona itempile yeKedareshwar, apho ikhona iLingaraj, kule Bhubaneswar inye (indawo yaseEkamra Van), iKhandagiri ikhona. Le Khandagiri yindawo yabangcwele abafezekileyo, kwaye iNkosi iya kuhlala apha.

Ukuzalwa kwakhona kukaAchyutananda Dasji, uMngcwele uHadi Das, wabhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, iNkosi uMaha Vishnu iya kuzalelwa eMhlabeni ngokuzalwa kukaKalki. Imidlalo yakhe engcwele iya kuqala ukwenzeka kwindawo yomlambo iKshira (Vaitarani), iAmaravati, oko kukuthi, iJajpur. Emva koko imidlalo yakhe iya kwenzeka eKhandagiri apho abangcwele abafezekileyo baya kungenela kwi-bhajan-kirtan imini nobusuku emiqolombeni ngasese. UMahapurush wathi iNkosi iya kuhlala kwaye yenze imidlalo yayo kule Khandagiri okanye indawo yaseJhadakhandagiri.

UMngcwele uHadi Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush ubhala ukuba “Indawo Yesiganeko” iya kuba yindawo yeLingaraj okanye iBhubaneswar, kwaye idabi eliseleyo leMahabharata liya kwenzeka apho. Kuyaziwa ukuba ngaphambili sabona indinyana apho uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. UAchyutananda Dasji wabhala le ndinyana malunga neKhandagiri kwincwadi yakhe iMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ kwaye wathi iKhandagiri yi “Ghatana Sthana,” apho idabi eliseleyo leMahabharata liya kwenzeka.

Kamva, uHadi Das wabhala – iBhubaneswar okanye indawo yeLingaraj yi “Ghatana Sthana” kwaye imfazwe yeKali Mahabharata iya kwenzeka apho.

Oku kuthetha ukuba into ethethwa nguAchyutananda Das malunga neKhandagiri kwi ‘Chakada Madana’ yile kanye ethethwa kukuzalwa kwakhe kwakhona, uHadi Das, malunga nendawo yeLingaraj (Bhubaneswar) kwi ‘Kali Chautisha’. Oku kungqina ukuba zombini iindawo, oko kukuthi, iKhandagiri neBhubaneswar, zikhomba kwindawo enye. Ngamafutshane, iKhandagiri ikwiBhubaneswar.

Qaphela – Ukuza kuthi ga ngoku kwiindinyana ezininzi ezingentla, sifunde ukuba iKhandagiri eBhubaneswar yindawo yabangcwele abafezekileyo nabangcwele. Abo bangcwele bazinikele kwi-bhajan-kirtan yeNkosi uKalki imini nobusuku emiqolombeni yaseKhandagiri. Ngoku siza kufunda ukuba zeziphi iingcali eziphambili nabamonki abaya kube benza ukuzila ukutya ngenxa yeNkosi uKalki kule Khandagiri kwaye bangaphi abangcwele bebonke abaya kube benza i-bhajan-kirtan emiqolombeni.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwi ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ yakhe –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush wacaphula incoko kaKrishna-Garuda kwincwadi yakhe iMalika. UGaruda uthi indawo yaseKhandagiri ayikude neBhubaneswar (ikufuphi kakhulu). Baninzi abangcwele eKhandagiri. Apho, uNarada, uMarkandeya, uShambhu, uAgastya, uUpalasti, uDurvasa, uKashyapa, uChyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” oko kuthetha uDridhasyu, uParashara, uVa\-shish\-tha, uVishwamitra, njl., bebonke oosiyazi abalishumi elinambini kwaye kubandakanywa nabo bebonke oosiyazi abangama-60,000, iiDevarishis, kunye neeBrahmarishis bazinikele kwi-bhajan yegama leNkosi uKalki emiqolombeni. Oothixo babamba iintlanganiso kule miqolomba ebusuku kwaye bacula imantra yeNkosi uKalki imihla ngemihla.

Kule ndinyana, uGarudaji akazange axelele nje malunga noosiyazi abenza ukuzila ukutya emiqolombeni yaseKhandagiri kodwa wakwatsho ukuba umqolomba waseKhandagiri awuyi kuba kude neBhubaneswar, okanye, uya kuba kufuphi kakhulu.

Kwiindinyana ezili-15 ezingentla, kufuneka ukuba kucace kubafundi ukuba ngokweBhavishya Malika, iKhandagiri—apho uKalki aya kuhlala khona, apho abangcwele abaninzi abafezekileyo baya kuhlala khona, apho i-bhajan-kirtan yegama likaKalki iya kuqhubeka ngokuqhubekayo emiqolombeni, apho umlambo uya kuqukuqela ngasese, apho idabi eliseleyo leMahabharata liya kwenzeka—ikhona kuphela eBhubaneswar okanye kwindawo yeLingaraj okanye kwindawo yaseEkamra Van, kwaye akukho kwenye indawo.

10 Injongo Yokuzalwa Kwakhona KukaKalki

Njengoko sonke sisazi, iNkosi ithatha ukuzalwa kwakhona okulishumi kulo Mhlaba ukuseka ubulungisa (Dharma). Nokuba yiSatya Yuga, iTreta Yuga, okanye iDvapara Yuga, injongo yokuzalwa kwakhona kweNkosi eMhlabeni ihlala ifana—ukuseka iDharma.

KwiShrimad Bhagavad Gita, iNkosi uKrishna yathi kuArjuna –

ISanskrit Sloka:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Intsingiselo:
Ukuze kukhuselwe abalungileyo (abakhonzi), ukuze kutshatyalaliswe abangendawo, nokuze kusekwe ngokuqinileyo ubulungisa, ndizibonakalisa mna kwisizukulwana ngesizukulwana.

Kwiincwadi zeBhavishya Malika nazo, kukhankanyiwe ukuba ukuze kutshatyalaliswe abangendawo, kukhuselwe abakhonzi nabangcwele, nokuze kusekwe kwakhona iDharma, iNkosi uMaha Vishnu iya kuhla eMhlabeni ngokuzalwa kwayo kweshumi nokokugqibela, ukuzalwa kukaKalki.

Incoko phakathi kweNkosi uKrishna nomhlobo uArjuna kwiDvapara Yuga yacatshulwa malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo kwiKali Yuga nguMahapurush Achyutananda Das kwincwadi yakhe iMalika 'Chausathi Patala'. UMahapurush wabhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, uMhlaba, ungakwazi ukuthwala umthwalo wezono ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uya kukhala phambi kwam. Emva koko ndiya kuthatha ukuzalwa kukaKalki kwaye ndiya kubatshabalalisa abangendawo kwaye ndibakhulule abakhonzi. Ndihlala eIndiya, ndiya kulawula ihlabathi lonke kwaye ndiya kulawula abantu ngendlela elungileyo neyiyo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwenye incwadi iMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho isiphithiphithi esoyikekayo eMhlabeni. UKalki ngokwakhe uya kubatshabalalisa abangendawo kwaye akhulule uMhlaba kumthwalo wesono.

Ecaphula incoko kaKrishna-Arjuna, uMahapurush ubhala kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe iMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Intsingiselo:
UShri Krishna uthi ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, ndiya kuthatha ukuzalwa kukaKalki. Ndikhusela abangcwele, abantu abangcwele, nabantu abaneentliziyo ezicocekileyo, ndiya kubatshabalalisa bonke abangendawo naboni.

Ecaphula amazwi eNkosi kwakhona, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe iMalika 'Chausathi Patala' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Intsingiselo:
INkosi ithi kwiKali Yuga, xa isono sifikelele encotsheni yaso, ndiya kuvela ngendlela yam yeKalki. Ndiya kuvavanya abazinikeleyo kwiindawo ezahlukeneyo eOdisha, ndiya kugweba phakathi kokulungileyo nokungalunganga, ndize ndenze ngokufanelekileyo.

Ecaphula amazwi eNkosi, omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Balaram Das, wabhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Intsingiselo:
INkosi ithi ngexesha lokusekwa kweDharma ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMama uAshta Chandis kunye nezinye iiYoginis ziya kuqhuba intshabalalo enkulu. Kuya kuna igazi esibhakabhakeni. Kuya kubakho iziphazamiso ezininzi kwaye iintlekele ziya kuvela kwisithuba, oko kukuthi, esibhakabhakeni. Ngabo kuphela endibakhuselayo abaya kuhlala bekhuselekile. Abangendawo baya kutshatyalaliswa, kwaye abantu abangcwele nabahloniphekileyo baya kuhlangulwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, iNkosi iya kuthatha imo yayo yeKalki ihambe ihlabathi lonke kwaye iya kuhlangula uMama uMhlaba ngokutshabalalisa abangendawo.

Kwi 'Kali Kalpa Gita' ngokwayo, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhalwa kwakhona –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki ngokuqinisekileyo iya kuwuhlangula uMhlaba ngokutshabalalisa iiMlechhas (abantu abangamahlakani/abangendawo) nangokuphelisa abangendawo.

10.1 Ukuza kweNkosi uKalki

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMphefumlo Omkhulu uSishu Ananta Das, wabhalwa ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Chumbaka Malika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi Enkulu uKalki iya kuzalelwa eOdisha. Emva koko, iNkosi iya kutshabalalisa abangendawo kwaye ikhusele abazinikeleyo ukuze iphinde imise iDharma.

11 Izenzo zeNtshabalalo: Isiqalo Nesiphelo Esiqinisekileyo?

Njengoko sisazi kwiireferensi zeBhabishya Malika, kweli xesha lenguqu phakathi kweKali Yuga neSatya Yuga, iziganeko zentshabalalo ezingazange zabonwa ziya kwenzeka kwihlabathi lonke phantsi kobunkokeli beNkosi uKalki, ngazo zonke iiMlecchas (abantu abangamahlakani), aboni, abangendawo, nabangalunganga baya kutshatyalaliswa. IBhabishya Malika ikwabonelela ngesiqinisekiso sokuba bonke abazinikeleyo baya kuhlala bekhuselekile ngexesha lezi ziganeko kwaye akufuneki boyike.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chhayalisha Patala’, ucaphula amazwi eNkosi Enkulu apho iNkosi inika iinkcukacha zokupheliswa okuyinxalenye okuzayo (Khanda Pralaya).

UMahapurush ubhalwa:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, Amandla Aphezulu ngendlela yeXesha (Kala) aya kubangela ukupheliswa okuyinxalenye kwaye atshabalalise ihlabathi. Ngexesha lokupheliswa, zonke izidalwa eziphilayo ziya kuchaphazeleka. UChandi-Chamunda kunye nemikhosi etshabalalisayo iya kuzula imini nobusuku iphethe amakrele (Khanda) kunye neentloko (Khapar). Intlungu, ukubandezeleka, isifo, nosizi ziya kusasazeka kwihlabathi lonke. Zonke iiyantlukwano phakathi 'kwabakho' naba 'ngabantu abangaziwayo' ziya kunyamalala. Imimoya engamashumi amane anesithoba, ithatha imo ekhohlakeleyo kaRudra, iya kuncothula zonke iintaba kubandakanya iSumeru enkulu. Amafu amane, okanye iAvarta, iSamvarta, iPushkara, neDrona, aya kungqongqa iindawo ezine kwaye adale iintlekele ezoyikekayo. Ubumnyama obunzulu buya kusongamela uMhlaba; ilanga nenyanga aziyi kuphuma. Ngeli xesha, iimeteor ziya kuwa esibhakabhakeni kwaye zibe ngunobangela wentshabalalo. Kuloo bumnyama, uMama uBhairavi uya kugquma ngamandla kwaye atshabalalise aboni abangenakubalwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ecaphula amazwi eNkosi, ubhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki iya kubangela intshabalalo enkulu kweli hlabathi ngeendlela zakhe ezahlukeneyo. Kwezinye iindawo, uya kuthatha imo yamanzi kwaye atshonise yonke into; kwezinye, uya kuphephetha yonke into ngentshabalalo yomoya. Kwezinye iimeko, uya kubangela intshabalalo ngelixa egcina isifungo sokuthula, kwaye kwezinye, uya kubulala abangendawo ngendlela yeXesha. Kwezinye iindawo, uya kuthatha imo yakhe yeHlabathi, kwaye kwezinye, uya kubangela intshabalalo ngamandla akhe eYogamaya.

NgokweBhabishya Malika, iNkosi uKalki iya kubangela le ntshabalalo ngeenxa zonke zamanzi ezoyikekayo, iinyikima zomhlaba ezinkulu, izaqhwithi zomoya ezitshabalalisayo, iinxa zonke zomlilo, iintlekele zesithuba, izifo ezosulelayo eziyingozi, kunye nemfazwe yehlabathi yenyukliya.

11.1 Iinyikima zomhlaba

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha lezenzo zentshabalalo zikaKalki ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, iinyikima zomhlaba ezinamandla kangangokuba ne 'Himalayas' kunye neencopho zayo ezifana ne 'Everest' ziya kuqhekeka. Abazinikeleyo baya kuhlala bekhuselekile, ngelixa aboni baya kutshatyalaliswa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhalwa kwakhona kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, uMhlaba wonke uya kungcangcazela kwiinyikima zomhlaba, kwaye ngenxa yezo nyikima zomhlaba, izakhiwo ezikhulu ziya kungcwatywa phantsi komhlaba.

UMahapurush wabhalwa kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngenxa yomthwalo ogqithisileyo wezenzo zesono ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, inyoka uVasuki, ephethe uMhlaba entloko, iya kunikina intloko yayo ngokuphindaphindiweyo. Ngenxa yoku, iinyikima zomhlaba ezintathu ezinkulu ziya kwenzeka kulo Mhlaba.

Ingcwele enkulu eyazalelwa eOdisha ngenkulungwane ye-20, uThakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, wabhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwisigaba sokugqibela seKali Yuga, kuya kubakho iinyikima zomhlaba ezingenakubalwa apho amadoda nabafazi abangenakubalwa baya kutshabalala.

11.2 Isikhukula Samanzi (Jala Pralaya)

Ngelixa kuxoxwa ngeengozi zesikhukula samanzi, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngaphandle kweIndiya, phantse zonke izizwe ziya kutshona emanzini olwandle. Kuya kubakho isikhukula samanzi eMhlabeni, kwaye zonke iindawo ezine ziya kutshona. Ngenxa yokunyuka kobushushu belanga, imiqathane yomkhenkce ekhoyo kwiNorth Pole iya kuqala ukunyibilika, nto leyo eya kuchaphazela kakhulu uLwandle lweAtlantic. Umgangatho wamanzi ezilwandle uya kuqhubeka unyuka, ubangela ukuba izixeko eziselunxwemeni zitshone. Kwezinye iindawo, amaza etsunami aphezulu njengeentaba aya kunyuka. Yonke ijografi iya kutshintsha. Kodwa iNkosi Enkulu iya kuyikhusela iIndiya, kwaye impembelelo yesi sikhukula samanzi iya kuba ncinci eIndiya. Ngaphandle kweIndiya, onke amanye amazwekazi aya kuba ngamaxhoba esikhukula samanzi.

11.3 Isikhukula Somoya (Pavan Pralaya)

Omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, ubhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Patta Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, wonke umntu uya kuba ngongalunganga; akukho mntu uya kuba nalo naluphi na unqulo/umsebenzi osele. Esi siya kuba sisizathu sokuba iNkosi Enkulu uKalki imise iDharma. Imimoya engamashumi amane anesithoba iya kuvuthuza ngendlela yokuba uMhlaba uya kungcangcazela.

UMahapurush ubhalwa kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha lomdlalo otshabalalisayo weNkosi uKalki, imimoya engamashumi amane anesithoba iya kuvuthuza kwaye izise intshabalalo eMhlabeni.

Ingcwele eyimfama eyazalelwa eOdisha ngenkulungwane ye-19, uBhima Bhoi, wabhalwa ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika. UMahapurush ubhalwa:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuya kufika ixesha apho imimoya engamashumi amane anesithoba iya kuvuthuza kwaye neentaba neenduli ziya kungcangcazela. Amafu amane okanye iAvarta, iSamvarta, iPushkara, neDrona aya kuna kunye. Ilanga nenyanga aziyi kubonakala esibhakabhakeni, kwaye ubumnyama obunzulu buya kusongamela zonke iindawo ezine.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalwa kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha lokusekwa kweDharma ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuya kubakho isiphithiphithi kuwo wonke uMhlaba kubandakanya amazwekazi asixhenxe. Ivuthuza ngomsindo omkhulu kakhulu, imimoya engamashumi amane anesithoba iya kutshabalalisa zonke izakhiwo. Ngeli xesha, akukho mntu uya kukhuseleka. Amandla karhulumente nobulumko bakhe aziyi kuba luncedo. Ngabo kuphela abalandela indlela yeDharma kwaye bazinikele ngokupheleleyo kwiNkosi Enkulu abaya kufumana ukhuseleko.

IsiOdia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Intsingiselo:
Emva kokuba ebuzwe ngumfundi uBaranga malunga nomdlalo otshabalalisayo kaKalki owenzeka ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhalwa kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ukuba uluntu luya kufuneka lubone usuku apho umntu omnye kuphela, njengesithixo, uya kusinda phakathi kweelali ezili-10. Ngeli xesha, imimoya engamashumi amane anesithoba iya kuvuthuza ngesantya esikhulu kwaye itshabalalise zonke izakhiwo. Olo luya kuba lusuku lwenyanga epheleleyo lwenyanga kaBhadra.

11.4 Unogumbe woMlilo (Agni Pralaya)

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, umdaniso womlilo uya kubonwa kuzo zonke iindawo ezine. Umhlaba uya kutsha. Abantu abaninzi baya kufa. Ubutyebi, impahla, nemali kuya kutsha kube luthuthu. Ezi zenzo zokutshabalalisa ziya kuba zehlabathi lonke, zibonakale kancinci kwezinye iindawo kwaye zibonakale kakhulu kwezinye.

11.5 Intlekele yeSithuba/yoMoya

IsiOdia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Chakada Madana' ukuba ngexesha lokugqibela leKali Yuga, xa ukusekwa kweDharma kuqhubeka, kuya kubakho ukuphazamiseka okuninzi esithubeni. Kuya kubakho utshintsho olukhulu kwiiplanethi nakwiinkwenkwezi, kunye nakwiindawo zazo nakwisantya sazo. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ngaphandle kwabazinikeleyo, abantu abaqhelekileyo abayi kukwazi ukwazi ngale nto.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwisigaba sokugqibela seKali Yuga, kuya kubakho ukuphazamiseka okuninzi kunye neemfazwe ezinkulu eMhlabeni. Kuya kubakho ukuwa kweemeteor/iiasteroids kuzo zonke iindawo ezine kwaye kuya kuphuma izandi kwisithuba esingenanto.

11.6 Isifo kunye noBhubhane

OoMahapurush babhala kwiincwadi ezininzi zeMalika ukuba ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, izifo ezininzi kunye nezifo ezosulelayo ziya kunyanzela uluntu ukuba luguqe. IMalika yathi ngokucacileyo oogqirha baya kusilela ekuxilongeni nasekunyangekeni kwezo zifo nezifo ezosulelayo, kwaye amayeza abawanikayo aya kungasebenzi.

OoMahapurush bakwathe ukuba eneneni, ezi zifo nezifo ezosulelayo ziya kuziswa ngamandla omama anjengoYogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, njl. La mandla omama aya kuzula ihlabathi lonke ngeendlela ezifihlakeleyo njengezi zifo kwaye angene emizimbeni yabantu.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Bigyanakalpa’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha lokuseka iDharma ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuya kubakho indlala nembalela, okukhokelela ekufeni kwabantu abaninzi. Iintlobo ezingamashumi amathandathu anesine zezifo ziya kusasazeka kwihlabathi lonke.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona kwincwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, izifo ezininzi ziya kuba ngunobangela wokufa kwabantu abangenakubalwa.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona kwi-‘Jaiphula Malika’ yakhe nakwi-‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuya kuza izifo ezingaziwayo kangangokuba ubulumko boogqirha buya kuyeka ukusebenza. Ezi zifo aziyi kuchongwa, kwaye amayeza aya kungasebenzi ekunyangeni kwazo, ngenxa yoko abantu abangenakubalwa baya kufa.

UMahapurush ubhala kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMama Yogamaya uya kungena emizimbeni yabantu ngohlobo lwesifo.

UMahapurush ubhala ngakumbi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, xa kuza izifo ezosulelayo kangangokuba amayeza angasebenzi ekunyangeni kwazo, ngoko kufuneka kuqondwe ukuba ixesha lokuphela emhlabeni lisondele.

UMahapurush ubhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, amandla omama aya kungena ngokufihlakeleyo emizimbeni ngohlobo lwesifo kwaye atshabalalise abantu abaninzi. Kodwa kunye noku, uMahapurush wakwakhankanya kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika indlela yokuba uluntu lusinde kwezi zifo nezifo ezosulelayo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chausathi Patala’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, abo bantu bamkela iimpawu ezintle ezifana nenyaniso, uxolo, uvelwano, nokuxolela ekuziphatheni kwabo baya kuhlala bekhululekile kwezi zifo.

11.7 Imfazwe yeHlabathi

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho idabi elikhulu eMhlabeni. Kuya kubakho imfazwe enkulu phakathi kwezizwe, ekhokelela ekutshatyalalisweni komhlaba.

UAchyutananda Das ji wabhala kwakhona kwi-‘Jaiphula Malika’ ngokwayo:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwisigaba sokugqibela seKali Yuga, kuya kuqhambuka iimfazwe ezinkulu kakhulu phakathi kwamazwe ahlukeneyo. Ngamanye amazwi, xa kuqala imfazwe eyoyikisayo phakathi kwezizwe, kufuneka kuqondwe njengesiphelo seKali Yuga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho imfazwe yeathom-nyukliya eMhlabeni eya kuba yoyikeka kakhulu. Umhlaba wonke uya kungcangcazela ngokusetyenziswa kweebhombu zenyukliya ezingenakubalwa. UMama uMhlaba uya kulila.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uthi kwixesha langoku, kuya kubakho imfazwe enkulu phakathi kweIndiya nePakistan. Ngokutsho kukaMahapurush, le mfazwe phakathi kweIndiya nePakistan iya kuthi kamva iguquke ibe yimfazwe yehlabathi kuba amazwe amaninzi aya kuxhasa iPakistan kule mfazwe, ngelixa amanye amazwe aya kulwela iIndiya. Kule mfazwe, izizwe ezinkulu zehlabathi ziya kwahlulwa zibe yimikhosi emibini.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwi-‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kule mfazwe, amazwe afana neTshayina, iMelika, neIraq aya kuxhasa iPakistan, ngelixa amagunya afana neRashiya, iJamani, neJapan aya kulwela iIndiya.

UMahapurush ubhala kwincwadi yeMalika enesihloko esithi ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ilizwe elibizwa ngokuba yiPakistan liya kuzibandakanya kwimfazwe enkulu neIndiya. Kule mfazwe, amazwe afana neTshayina, iTurkey, iIran, iNgilani, neMelika aya kunceda iPakistan kwaye anike inkxaso yomkhosi. Ukubona konke oku, umhlobo omkhulu weIndiya, iRashiya, uya kuza kuqala kwaye yedwa ukunceda iIndiya. Iya kubonelela iIndiya ngazo zonke iintlobo zezixhobo, intsebenziswano yezoqoqosho, kunye nenkxaso yomkhosi kwaye iya kulwa imfazwe enkulu nxamnye nezizwe zotshaba egameni leIndiya.

UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ayisiyiyo iRashiya kuphela, kodwa neJapan neJamani ziya kulwela iIndiya kule mfazwe. Kwi-‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ ngokwayo, ubhala ngakumbi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iJapan ayiyi kukwazi ukulibala ukuwiswa kwebhombu yeathom kuyo yiMelika. Umsindo weJapan ngakuMelika awuyi kuthoba. Ilangatye lempindezelo liya kuhlala livutha entliziyweni yayo, ngenxa yoko iJapan iya kulwa neMelika kwicala leIndiya ngexesha lemfazwe yesithathu yehlabathi. Ngokwesicelo seJapan, iJamani nayo iya kulwa kwicala leIndiya.

12 Imfazwe Eseleyo ye-Mahabharata kwi-Kali Yuga

IiPanchasakhas zabhala kwiincwadi zeBhabishya Malika ukuba iMfazwe yeHlabathi eyenzeka ekupheleni kweKali Yuga iya kuba yimfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata. OoMahapurush bacacisa ukuba imfazwe yeentsuku ezili-18 yeMahabharata ekupheleni kweDwapara Yuga ayizange igqitywe ngokupheleleyo kuba, ngelo xesha, iNkosi uKrishna yagqiba ekubeni ingayiqhubi inxalenye yokugqibela yemfazwe ngelo xesha, kodwa iyigqibe ekupheleni kweKali Yuga.

Ayipheleli nje apha, iiMahapurusha ziye zabhala ngokweenkcukacha ngale mfazwe kwiincwadi zazo zeMalika zatyhila izinto ezininzi, njengokuba iya kwenzeka nini, bangaphi abantu abaya kufa ngexesha layo, iya kuhlala ixesha elingakanani, iya kuthatha hlobo luni, iya kwenzeka phantsi kobunkokeli bakabani, ngubani oya kuthatha inxaxheba kwicala laseIndiya, iya kuphelela phi, kwaye kuya kwenzeka ntoni emva kwale mfazwe, njl.njl.

12.1 Le mfazwe iya kwenzeka nini?

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kugqitywa ekupheleni kweKali Yuga. Kule mfazwe, "Yama Maa Sata Bhauni" okanye ooThixokazi abaSixhenxe abaNgumama (Saptamatruka) abakwiBiraja Khetra (uChamunda, uVarahi, uIndrani, uVaishnavi, uBrahmani, uKaumari, uMaheshwari, noNarasimhi) baya kubatshabalalisa aboni.

Echaza ukuba le mfazwe iya kwenzeka nini ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, umlo oseleyo waseIndiya (Mahabharata) uya kuqala ngexesha lokudibana kweMeena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn), apho abantu abangenakubalwa baya kufa.

12.2 Le mfazwe iya kwenzeka ngaluphi uhlobo kwaye iya kulwelwa phi kwinqanaba lokugqibela?

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, yazi ukuba inxalenye yemfazwe yeMahabharata isasele, eya kugqitywa njengeMfazwe yeHlabathi kwiKali Yuga.

UMahapurush uqhubeka ebhala kwi‘Jaiphula Malika’ yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kwenzeka eOdisha, kwaye ngelo xesha, amajoni amaninzi ezizwe ezilwayo aya kungena ngaphakathi eIndiya.

USanta Achyutananda Das ubhala kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kulwelwa kwiKali Yuga, eya kwenzeka kumhlaba weUtkala okanye iOdisha.

12.3 Iya kuhlala ixesha elingakanani le mfazwe?
