UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wakwaxela kwangaphambili kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ ukuba le mfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kuhlala iinyanga ezili-13 zizonke.

UMahapurush wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kuhlala iinyanga ezilishumi elinesithathu zizonke, kwaye ngokugqitywa kwale mfazwe, impembelelo yeKali Yuga kulo Mhlaba iya kuphela ngokupheleleyo.

12.4 Ngubani oya kuthatha inxaxheba kwicala laseIndiya kwinqanaba lokugqibela?

IiMahapurusha zabhala ukuba kwinxalenye yokugqibela yale mfazwe, ngelixa kwelinye icala kuya kubakho imikhosi emikhulu yezizwe ezilwayo, kwelinye icala, onke amajoni angamagorha exesha leMahabharata kunye namawaka abangcwele, izilumko, nabaprofeti baya kubakho phantsi kobunkokeli beNkosi enkulu uKalki ngokwakhe.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwi‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngexesha leMfazwe yeHlabathi yesiThathu, bebona uhlaselo oluvela kumacala amane eIndiya olwenziwa ziintshaba zangaphandle, abantu baseIndiya baya kothuka bakhale ngentlungu. Bengenakuzinceda, bonke baya kukhumbula iNkosi bacele ukhuseleko. Baya kuthi—O Nkosi, yiva isibongozo sethu, sisindise, shiya iVaikuntha uze eMhlabeni ukuze usikhusele.

Eshukunyiswa yile ntliziyo ebuhlungu yabazinikeleyo kwaye ebona uhlaselo lweIndiya olwenziwa ziintshaba zangaphandle, iNkosi ePhakamileyo uBrahman Narayana (Maha Vishnu) ngokwakhe, kwisimilo sakhe seKalki, uya kuvela eCuttack, eOdisha, aze anike i-darshan kubazinikeleyo.

UMahapurush wabhala ukuba kunye neNkosi uKalki ngelo xesha linye, uBhima, uArjuna, uBhishma, uDrona, uAshwatthama, uKarna, uShalya, uJayadratha, uBarbarika, uAbhimanyu, uBabhruvahana, uEkalavya, noGhatotkacha, ababesele bezelwe, baya kulwa kwicala laseIndiya baze boyise kakhulu izizwe ezilwayo. Amandla emantra angcwele ala majoni angamagorha aya kungqina ukuba angaphezulu kunamandla esayensi yanamhlanje ezintshaba.

Ngokuphathelele lo mba mnye, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe yeMahabharata iya kulwelwa kwiKali Yuga, apho amajoni angamagorha azo zonke izizukulwana ezintathu zangaphambili aya kuthatha inxaxheba.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uqhubeka ebhala kwi‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kwenzeka phantsi kobunkokeli beNkosi ePhakamileyo uBrahman Narayana (isimilo seKalki) ngokwakhe.

Ngokuphathelele lo mba, omnye weePanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yashovanta Das, wakwabala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Nidagha Nighanta’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata iya kugqitywa, kwaye uMadhava ngokwakhe, iNkosi enkulu uKalki, uya kunika umyalelo wokuqala le mfazwe.

Umngcwele omkhulu waseOdisha owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-20, uThakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, wakwabala ngalo mba kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho idabi elinzima phakathi kwamandla emipu yezizwe ezilwayo zaseIndiya kunye namandla okuzila (Tapasya) abangcwele abangenakubalwa baseIndiya kunye nezilumko, apho amandla emijukujelwa yanamhlanje ezintshaba aya kuba buthuntu phambi kwamandla okuzila aseIndiya, kwaye ekugqibeleni iIndiya iya kuphumelela kule mfazwe inkulu.

12.5 Le mfazwe iya kugqitywa phi eIndiya?

IiMahapurusha zabhala kwiincwadi zeMalika ukuba xa imikhosi yangaphandle ifika eOdisha, iya kulwa kwiindawo ezahlukeneyo eOdisha, njengaseBirat Gadi yaseCuttack, eChoudwar, eJobra, eBarabati, naseKaliaboda; ePuri; eJajpur; eGohira Tikiri yaseBhadrak; eParadip; eBalasore, njl.njl.

Nangona le mfazwe iya kwenzeka kuzo zonke ezi ndawo eOdisha, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wachaza ngokucacileyo ukuba iya kugqitywa ngokupheleleyo eKhandagiri eBhubaneswar xa iNkosi enkulu ngokwayo itshabalalisa onke amajoni ezizwe ezilwayo ngeSudarshana Chakra yakhe.

UMahapurush wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kalpatika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kuya kubakho imfazwe enzima kakhulu eKhandagiri eOdisha. Kweso sithuba sinye, iNkosi enkulu uKalki iya kutshabalalisa amajoni angenakubalwa ngeSudarshana Chakra yakhe.

12.6 Kuya kwenzeka ntoni ngale mfazwe kwaye kuya kwenzeka ntoni emva kwayo?

Kwi‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ivesi enxalenye yayo yokuqala imi ngolu hlobo:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, nje ukuba le mfazwe yeMahabharata igqitywe, impembelelo yeKali Yuga iya kuphela.

Emva koko uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wakwabala ivesi kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’, umgca wayo wokuqala uthi:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, ngokugqitywa kwemfazwe eseleyo yeMahabharata, uMhlaba uya kuba noxolo. Emva koko zonke iingxaki eMhlabeni ziya kuphela.

12.7 Inkcazo yemeko yamaMandla amakhulu afana neMelika neYurophu kwiMfazwe yeHlabathi

AbaPanchasakha Mahapurushas babhala kwimibhalo yabo yeMalika ukuba iMfazwe yeHlabathi yesiThathu iya kuba yoyikeka kangangokuba zonke izizwe ziya kuchaphazeleka yiyo. Onke amazwe athathwa namhlanje njengawona mandla anamandla ehlabathi, le mfazwe yokuphela kweKali Yuga iya kuwashiya echithakele.

Echaza iimeko zaseMelika kule mfazwe, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, isizwe esibizwa ngokuba yiMelika siya kuzithatha njengenkokeli yehlabathi. Sinxiliswe kukuzingca nokukratsha, siya kuzibiza ngokuba sesona silungileyo kwaye sisongele ukutshabalalisa amanye amazwe emfazweni. UMahapurush waqhubeka esithi iNkosi Enkulu ayikunyamezeli ukuzingca komntu kwaye ngoko ke iya kuwaphula ukuzingca kweMelika. Isixeko saseNew York saseMelika siya kutshatyalaliswa, kwaye eso sizwe siya kujongana nentshabalalo epheleleyo njengengqina lentshabalalo emandla.

Kwi-‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ ngokwayo, uNgcwele Achyutananda Das ubhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, unobangela weMfazwe yeHlabathi yesiThathu iya kuba sisizwe esibizwa ngokuba yi-‘Melika’. Eso sizwe siya kuseka intlonipho nokuzingca kwaso phezu koMhlaba wonke kwaye sifumane ubuchule ekulweni imfazwe. UMahapurush waqhubeka ebhala ukuba ngenxa yoku kuzingca, igama laso nomkhondo waso ziya kucinywa kwiMfazwe yeHlabathi yesiThathu. Emva kwemfazwe, imozulu apho iya kuba yityhefu, kwaye nangenxa yoko, abantu abaninzi abangenakubalwa baya kufa kumazwekazi aseMelika naseYurophu.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwi-‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yakhe:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kule mfazwe, izwekazi laseYurophu liya kutshatyalaliswa ngokupheleleyo. Ngaphandle kwabazinikeleyo beNkosi, akukho mntu uya kusinda kwezo zizwe.

Kwi-‘Jaiphula Malika’, uNgcwele Achyutananda Das ubhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, amazwe akwicala laseNtshona naseMntla aya kutshatyalaliswa kwiMfazwe yeHlabathi.

U-Achyutananda Das ji ubhala kwakhona kwi-‘Jaiphula Malika’ ngokwayo:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iJamani, iRashiya, iItali, iJapan, neTurkey, njl., izizwe ziya kutshatyalaliswa kakhulu yiMfazwe yeHlabathi.

Njengoko sele sitshilo, zonke iziganeko ezingentla ziya kwenzeka ikakhulu ngexesha leMeena-Shani yoga, oko kukuthi, phakathi kweminyaka ka-2025 no-2028.

NgoMatshi 29, 2025, iSaturn yangena kumqondiso weenkwenkwezi iPisces (Meena). Ngamanye amaxesha ihamba ngasemva (Vakri) okanye intshukumo ebuyela umva kwaye ngamanye amaxesha ihamba ngqo (Margi) okanye intshukumo ethe tye, iSaturn iya kuhlala kumqondiso weenkwenkwezi iPisces kude kube ngoFebruwari 23, 2028. Okwangoku, ukusuka ngoJuni 3, 2027, ukuya kuOktobha 20, 2027, kuya kubakho ukuhamba okufutshane kweSaturn kumqondiso weenkwenkwezi iAries (Mesha).

Ngoku umbuzo obalulekileyo uyavela apha. Sele kudlule malunga neenyanga ezili-10 ukusukela oko kwaqala iMeena-Shani yoga, kodwa ezi ziganeko zokutshabalalisa azikaqali. Kutheni kunjalo? Ewe, iintlekele zendalo ziyenzeka kwihlabathi lonke, iimfazwe nazo ziyaqhubeka phakathi kwamazwe aliqela, kodwa azikenzeki kwinqanaba elikhulu apho iziprofeto zazo zenziwe ngabaPanchasakhas kwimibhalo yeMalika! Umbuzo uphinda uphinde uvuke ezingqondweni zabaninzi kuthi ukuba inqanaba lezi ziganeko liya kuqina nini!

Iya kuqala nini kanye iNtshabalalo Enkulu? Ithini iBhabishya Malika ngalo mbandela? IMalika ngokuqinisekileyo ithi imidlalo yokutshabalalisa iya kwenzeka eMeena-Shani, kodwa iMalika ichaza ukuqala kwale midlalo yokutshabalalisa njengenxalenye yokugqibela yonyaka ka-2026 endaweni yonyaka ka-2025.

Omnye wabaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yashovanta Das, wabhala malunga neziphazamiso ezingazange zibonwe ngaphambili eziya kwenzeka kwihlabathi lonke ngexesha leMeena-Shani kwimibhalo yakhe yeMalika kwaye wanika ulwazi malunga nexesha lazo elichanekileyo. Kwenye yemibhalo yakhe yeMalika, wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwiminyaka emibini enesiqingatha yeMeena-Shani yoga, ngexesha lesigaba sesibini (iminyaka ka-2026-2027) nesigaba sesithathu (iminyaka ka-2027-2028), kuya kubakho ukufa okungafanelekanga ngamanani amakhulu, kwaye isiphithiphithi siya kulawula kuyo yonke indawo. Kuya kubakho iinyikima zomhlaba ezoyikekayo, uMhlaba wonke uya kungcangcazela. Kuya kubakho izikhalo zeBhairavi, oko kukuthi, uMama uBhairavi uya kwenza isikhalo esoyikekayo ngokukhawuleza esuka esithubeni. Izinto ezoyikekayo zokukhukula kwamanzi ziya kubonwa eMhlabeni. Ngenxa yomsindo kaMama uHingula, izindlu ezinkulu, izindlu ezinkulu, njl., ziya kutsha kakhulu. UMahapurush wabhala ukuba bonke abantu abangendawo baya kuba ngamaxhoba omsindo wooMama boothixokazi. Ngelo xesha, abantu baya kubona konke okungazange kubonwe ngamehlo abo kude kube ngoko, oko kukuthi, yonke into iya kwenzeka ngendlela engazange ibonwe ngaphambili. Bonke abantu baya kukhala becela inceba. Abo bazinikela ezinyaweni zeNkosi uShri Hari kwaye bathatha indawo yakhe yokukhusela, uMahapurush wathi bona bodwa baya kufumana usindiso kuko konke oku kwaye babe ziimbewu zeSatya Yuga.

Kwivesi engentla, uMahapurush Yashovanta Das wathi ezi midlalo yokutshabalalisa ziya kuqala ngokufanelekileyo ukusuka kwisigaba sesibini seMeena-Shani yoga, oko kukuthi, iminyaka ka-2026-2027.

Ngexesha leKali Yuga, abaninzi abaMahapurushas bazalelwa eOdisha amaxesha ngamaxesha. Ngenkulungwane ye-20, uMahapurush owazalelwa eOdisha watyhila iziprofeto ezaxelelwa yiNkosi Enkulu ngokwayo, uMama uYogamaya, noothixo noothixokazi ukuze kungenelwe abazinikeleyo. UMahapurush wabhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, emva kokuba kufike inxalenye yokugqibela yonyaka ka-2026 okanye uphele unyaka ka-2026, iNkosi uKalki iya kunika umyalelo kuwo onke amandla obuthixo (ooMama uAshta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, iikrore ezisithoba zeKatyayanis) ukuba aqalise intshabalalo.

UMahapurush emva koko waqhubeka ebhala:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, emva konyaka ka-2026, bonke oothixo noothixokazi baya kuvakalisa ubizo lwedabi lamaDeva-Asura. Ukuqala kwemfazwe (iMfazwe yeHlabathi) kuya kwenzeka ehlabathini.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba kuzo zombini iivesi ezingentla, uMahapurush wabhala ngokucacileyo “Unyaka ka-2026”.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, hlanganisa isithandathu (6) nesixhenxe (7) uze udibanise ishumi elinesithathu (13) kwisiphumo, oko kukuthi, 6+7+13=26, apho imfazwe emandla iya kuqala khona eIndiya. Emva kwelo xesha linye, iziganeko zokutshabalala okuyinxalenye (Khanda Pralaya) ziya kwenzeka kwaye uMhlaba uya kuqala ukutshatyalaliswa.

Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuba apha uMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ngokungafaniyo nevesi ezimbini ezingentla, akazange abhale “Unyaka ka-2026” kodwa wabhala kuphela u-26. Mhlawumbi apha, ngo-26, isalathiso sikaMahapurush Achyutananda Das sibhekisa kunyaka ka-2026 kuphela.

UMahapurush owazalelwa ngenkulungwane ye-20 ubhala kwakhona:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Intsingiselo:
Kwivesi engentla, uMama uYogamaya uthi emva konyaka ka-2026, wonke umntu uya kugwetywa. Bonke abo bangakholwayo, abangendawo, aboni, njl., bonke baya kutshatyalaliswa.

Ngoko ke, kufuneka kuqondwe ngokucacileyo ukuba imidlalo yokutshabalalisa kaKalki echazwe yiMalika iya kwenzeka ngexesha leMeena-Shani yoga ngokwayo, kodwa oku akuyi kwenzeka ngonyaka ka-2025 kodwa kuya kuqala ukusuka kwincam yonyaka ka-2026.

Ukuba le midlalo yokutshabalalisa iya kuhlala iminyaka emingaphi iyonke, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ngalo mbandela kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, imidlalo yokutshabalalisa iya kuqala kwinani lama-24 kwaye iya kuhlala kude kube kwinani lama-29. Yonke imidlalo yokutshabalalisa iya kuphela kwinani lama-29.

Qaphela ukuba kwivesi engentla, uMahapurush uxoxe ngenani lama-24, hayi unyaka ka-2024. Kodwa uMahapurush ukwenze kwacaca ukuba le midlalo yokutshabalalisa, eqala kwinani lama-24 kwaye ihlala kude kube kwinani lama-29, iya kuqhubeka iminyaka eyi-6 iyonke. UMahapurush akazange acacise ukuba iya kuqala ngaluphi unyaka kwaye iya kuphela ngaluphi unyaka, kodwa wathi ukusuka kwinani lama-24 ukuya kwinani lama-29, oko kukuthi, iminyaka eyi-6 iyonke, le midlalo yokutshabalalisa iya kuqhubeka.

Ekubeni kwiivesi ezingentla sele sisazi ukuba le midlalo yokutshabalalisa iya kuqala ukusuka kwincam yonyaka ka-2026, ngoku sinokufikelela ngokulula kwesi sigqibo sokuba inani lama-24 le-‘Kaliyuga Malika’ libonisa unyaka ka-2026-2027. Ngokunjalo, inani lama-29 lolu luhlu lufanayo lwale ncwadi yeMalika lusalathisa kunyaka ka-2031-2032.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ngencwadi yakhe yeMalika Pothi, wachaza kwakhona –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi uKalki iya kwenza umdlalo wayo (izenzo zakhe zokutshabalalisa). Ezi zenzo zokutshabalalisa ziya kuqhubeka kude kube ngunyaka ka-2033. Okwangoku, kuya kubakho izifo ezininzi ezosulelayo, eziya kubangela isiphithiphithi kuluntu. Kuya kufika ixesha apho kuya kubakho isiphithiphithi kuyo yonke indawo. Bonke abantu baya kuba kwimeko embi. Kwanolwazi lwezenzululwazi aluyi kuba luncedo.

Nangona kunjalo, u-Achyutananda Das ji wathi kwi-‘Kaliyuga Malika’ yakhe ukuba imisebenzi ephambili yokutshabalalisa iya kuphela kwinani lama-27.

UMahapurush wabhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, kwinani lama-27, amaza olwandle aya kuvela eMzantsi kwaye aya kudibana nomlambo iGanges ogcweleyo ovela eMntla. Kwangelo xesha, amaza amakhulu etsunami aya kuvela eNtshona kwaye enze umonakalo omkhulu. Koku kutshabalala okuyinxalenye, aboni abangenakubalwa baya kufa. Nabani na abangcwele nemiphefumlo elungileyo, uBaladev ngokwakhe uya kubasindisa kwaye abagcine bekhuselekile ngononophelo ezintabeni nasezindulini zaseIndiya.

Ngokutsho kukaMahapurush, le iya kuba yintlekele yokugqibela phakathi kweentlekele ezinkulu eziya kwenzeka kwinani lama-27, oko kukuthi, ngonyaka ka-2029-2030 ngokokubala okukhankanywe ngentla.

Ngoku masizame ukuqonda olu luhlu lwamaxesha ngezinye iivesi. UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iMfazwe Yehlabathi Yesithathu iya kuhlala iminyaka emihlanu. Kuya kubakho imfazwe ekrakra iminyaka emihlanu; Izulu, uMhlaba, kunye neLizwe Eliphantsi ziya kungcangcazela.

U-Achyutananda Das ji ubhala kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Intsingiselo:
Kule vesi ingentla, iNkosi uShri Krishna, ngelixa encokola no-Indra, ithi kuya kubakho imfazwe ekrakra ukusuka kwinani lama-24 ukuya kwinani lama-27.

Kucacile kuzo zombini iivesi ezibhalwe ngentla ukuba imfazwe ephambili okanye iMfazwe Yehlabathi Yesithathu iya kuhlala malunga neminyaka emi-4-5. Oko kukuthi, iqala ngo-2026-2027 ize iphele ngo-2029-2030.

Ngoko ke, ukuphela okukhulu okukhankanywe yiMahapurush ku ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ kwinani lama-27 okanye unyaka ka-2029-2030 kuhambelana ngokugqibeleleyo nazo zombini iivesi ezinikwe ngentla.

Kwiingxoxo ezingentla, ukuba isiphelo sishunqulwe:

Unyaka ka-2026 – 2027: Ixesha lentlekele liya kuqala.
Unyaka ka-2029 – 2030: Iziganeko ezoyikekayo nezintlekele kakhulu ziya kuphela.
Unyaka ka-2031 – 2032: Zonke iziganeko ezitshabalalisayo ziya kuphela.

Qaphela: Imisebenzi ephambili etshabalalisayo ephela ngonyaka ka-2029 ibandakanya yonke leyo ihlala ixoxwa, njenge – iinyikima zomhlaba ezinkulu, ukunyibilika kwamanzi okukrakra, iintlekele zomoya, iintlekele zomlilo, ukuwa kweemeteorite, izifo-ubhubhane, imfazwe yaseIndiya namazwe otshaba, iMfazwe Yehlabathi, imfazwe yeKali-Mahabharata, ukuqhekeka kweHirakud namanye amadama, ubumnyama beentsuku ezisixhenxe nobusuku obusixhenxe, iBhairavi Dak, njl.njl.

13 Ukuqala kweSatyayuga

Ukuze ungene kwiSatyayuga, kubalulekile ukuba umntu ahambe endleleni yeDharma. Kufuneka asoloko ethetha inyaniso kwaye alandele inyaniso kuphela; akufuneki athathe indlela yobudenge kodwa indlela yoxolo. Kufuneka abe neemvakalelo zothando, ububele, novelwano kuyo yonke into ephilayo. Akufuneki abangele nayiphi na intlungu kuyo nayiphi na into ephilayo ngengqondo yakhe, intetho, kunye nokuziphatha. Kufuneka abe ngumntu olungiselela zonke izidalwa ngaphandle kwesizathu, ukuziphatha kwakhe kufuneka kuxolele abanye, kufuneka abe nobuntu obulula nobuthe tye, akufuneki abe nintiyo kuye nabani na, kwaye kufuneka azi ukuba angazigcina njani iimvakalelo zakhe zilawuleka. Kufuneka abe ngumkhonzi kaThixo, asoloko egxininisa ingqondo yakhe kuThixo, achithe ixesha ekuculeni iingoma, ekubizeni amagama kaThixo, ajoyine iintlanganiso zokomoya (Satsang), enze imithandazo yemihla ngemihla kathathu (Trisandhya), alandele izibhalo, kwaye afunde iShrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (eyaziwa njengeMahapurana phakathi kwePuranas ezili-18) yonke imihla. Kufuneka abe ngumntu ofunda okanye oweva ngeentsomi zikaThixo. Kufuneka atye ukutya okumsulwa nokweSattvic (okunganyama). Kunye nokwenza imisebenzi yakhe yehlabathi, kufuneka akwenze neYoga, ukubiza amagama, njl.njl. Kufuneka asoloko ehlonipha abazali bakhe nabadala, abonise uthando kubaselula, kwaye aseke imvakalelo yobuhlobo noontanga.

Ngamnye wethu unqwenela ukungena kwiSatyayuga ngokubaleka imisebenzi etshabalalisayo ezayo, iintlekele zendalo, kunye neMfazwe Yehlabathi, nangokufumana ubabalo lweNkosi uKalki. Kodwa ingaba ukungena kwiSatyayuga kulula kangako? Hayi. Ukungena kwiSatyayuga akufani nokwenza isisombululo kwiglasi uze usisele ukuze nabani na osiselayo ngale ndlela ngokuqinisekileyo angene kwiSatyayuga!

Ukuze ungene kwiSatyayuga, wonke umntu kufuneka enze utshintsho olusisiseko ebomini bakhe. Kubalulekile ukuba kunye nokwenza imisebenzi yehlabathi kunye nemisebenzi, aqale ukuphila ubomi bokomoya.

Echaza ukuba ixesha elizayo liya kuba njani kubakhonzi, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, abakhonzi kwafuneka babandezeleke kakhulu eKaliyuga, kodwa emva kwexesha elifutshane xa iKaliyuga iphela kwaye kuvela ukukhanya kwexesha elingunaphakade (Ananta Yuga), abakhonzi baya kuvuya kakhulu. Emva koko zonke iintlungu zabo kunye neentlungu ziya kuhamba.

USanta Bhima Bhoi ubhala kwenye yeencwadi zakhe zeMalika ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, iNkosi ePhakamileyo iya kutshabalalisa abangendawo kwaye ikhusele abangcwele nabakhonzi, kwaye ikhathalele abantu emhlabeni. IKaliyuga iya kuphela kwaye iSatyayuga iya kufika, kwaye iNkosi uKalki ngokwaYo iya kuba ngumlawuli wehlabathi lonke. INkosi iya kuthatha iintlungu zabo bonke abakhonzi kwaye ibagcine bonwabile. Abakhonzi baya kuvuya, kwaye onke amazwe amathathu aya kuvakala ngengoma yegama elithi 'Hari'.

13.1 Iya kuqala nini iSatyayuga?

IBhabishya Malika ithi iAnanta Yuga okanye iAdi Satyayuga iya kuqala ngonyaka ka-2033. UMahapurush wabhala kwincwadi yakhe yeMalika –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuba ukusuka ngonyaka ka-2013, intshabalalo yeKaliyuga kunye nokukhanya kweNyaniso kuya kuqala ngokuthe ngcembe. Ukusuka ngelo xesha ukuya phambili, intshabalalo yabangendawo naboni iya kuqala. Amandla obuxoki, ubugwenxa, nokungalunganga aya kuncipha, kwaye impembelelo yeDharma iya kuqala ukwanda. UMahapurush wabhala ngakumbi ukuba ngokuqhubeka ngolu hlobo, ngonyaka ka-2033, kuya kuvela ukukhanya kweentsika ezine zeDharma, okuthetha ukuba ukusekwa ngokupheleleyo kweDharma kuya kwenzeka. Ukusuka ngelo xesha ukuya phambili, iimpawu zeNyaniso, uXolo, uBubele, noXolelo ziya kuqala ukuphuhla ngokupheleleyo. Kuya kubakho umanyano lwabo bonke abantu abanobuntu obuhle, obunobubele nobobungcwele, abangcwele, izilumko.

UMahapurush ubhala kwakhona –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Intsingiselo:
UMahapurush wathi lo ngumcimbi oyimfihlo kakhulu kwaye uya kwenzeka kwinani lama-33 (unyaka ka-2033). Abakhonzi kuphela abaya kukwazi oku. Baya kuvuya kakhulu besazi oku. Ihlabathi liya kukhululeka esonweni; akuyi kubakho uloyiko okanye ukudideka engqondweni yomntu. Wonke umntu uya konwaba, wonke umntu uya kuba nokutya okwaneleyo nempahla, wonke umntu uya kulandela indlela yeDharma. Emva koko kuya kubakho isivuno esihle kakhulu, kwaye oku kuya kuba ngenxa yobabalo lukaMama uLakshmi. Wonke umntu uya kuhamba endleleni yeNyaniso; ixesha elingunaphakade liya kufika. Wonke umntu uya kufunda iiPuranas neBhagavat kwaye enze iingoma nokucula. IKaliyuga iya kutshatyalaliswa ngokupheleleyo; le ngxelo ayiyi kungcangcazela konke konke. UMahapurush wathi ngakumbi ukuba eli liya kuba lixesha elingunaphakade kwaye oku kuya kuba lulungiselelo lwexesha elingunaphakade (Adya Satyayuga). Akuyi kubakho mntu ungalunganga, onobubi, okanye umlechha emhlabeni. Abakhonzi kuphela abaya kuhlala kwaye bonke baya kumanyana noThixo.

UMahapurush owazalwa ngenkulungwane ye-20 (esixoxe ngaye ngaphambili), ngelixa ecaphula iNkosi ePhakamileyo, uMama uYogamaya, kunye noothixo noothixokazi, wabhala –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Intsingiselo:
Apha uMahapurush ucela ukuba kubhalwe “3” kane ngokulandelelana. Oko kukuthi 3333. UMahapurush uthi ukusuka ngolu suku ukuya phambili, iSatyayuga iya kuqala emhlabeni. Apha ngo-3333, uMahapurush uthetha – umhla wesithathu, inyanga yesithathu, neminyaka engamashumi amathathu anesithathu, oko kukuthi, ngoMatshi 3, 2033. UMahapurush ubhala ngakumbi ukuba loo mini iya kuba nguLwesine kwaye iSatyayuga iya kuqala ngaloo mini.

UMahapurush wabhala kwakhona –

IsiOdia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Ukuguqulelwa koonobumba:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Intsingiselo:
Oko kukuthi, emva komhla wesithathu wenyanga yesithathu yenani lamashumi amathathu anesithathu lonyaka wamashumi amabini, oko kukuthi, ngoMatshi 3, 2033, iSatyayuga iya kufika.

Ngoko ke, akukho mathandabuzo ngoku okokuba ixesha elitsha (Ananta Yuga okanye Adi Satyayuga) liya kuqala ngonyaka ka-2033. Liya kuba lixesha lomkhonzi noThixo kuphela. Ngamanye amazwi, abakhonzi kuphela abaya kukwazi ukungena kweli xesha. INkosi iya kukhusela uMhlaba ngeNyaniso, iDharma, noBulungisa, kwaye bonke abakhonzi baya kuqhelisela iNyaniso, uXolo, uBubele, noXolelo kubomi babo.

14 Isishwankathelo

IBhabishya Malika yingqokelela yeencwadi ezininzi ezibhalwe ngamaPanchasakhas azalwa malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo kunye nabanye abangcwele baseOdia namadoda amakhulu azalwa ngenkulungwane ye-17, ye-18, ye-19, neye-20. Iincwadi zeMalika zabhalwa ikakhulu ngamaPanchasakhas eNkosi uShri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. La maPanchasakhas azibonakalisa njengabahlobo abasondeleyo beNkosi kuyo yonke iminyaka ngamagama ahlukeneyo, naseKaliyuga, malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo, la maPanchasakhas afumana ubukho bokuzibonakalisa kweNkosi, uChaitanya Mahaprabhu. Amagama ala maPanchasakhas azalwa eKaliyuga ngala – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, noJagannath Das.

AmaPanchasakhas athathwa njengokuzibonakalisa okuyinxalenye (Anshavatar) yeNkosi, kwaye malunga neminyaka engama-600 eyadlulayo, iincwadi zeMalika zaqulunqwa ngabo kuphela ngomyalelo weNkosi. Injongo ekuphela kwayo yokuqulunqwa kweencwadi zeMalika kukulungelwa kwabakhonzi – ukuvusa ingqondo elele yabakhonzi ekupheleni kweKaliyuga nokubahlangula ngokubakhokela. AmaPanchasakhas abhala ngokucacileyo kwiincwadi zawo ukuba ezi ncwadi akufuneki zityhilwe phambi kwanabani na ngaphandle kwabakhonzi. AmaPanchasakhas akwacacisa ukuba ngabo kuphela abaya kukwazi ukwamkela amazwi eMalika abaye baba ngabakhonzi beNkosi ukusuka ekuzalweni kwangaphambili nakumaxesha adlulileyo.

Ekubeni iMalika ililizwi likaMahaprabhu ngokwaKhe kwaye ibhalwe ngamaPanchasakhas kuphela ngomyalelo weNkosi, ngoko ke igama ngalinye leMalika lifana nomgca okrolwe elityeni ongenakuze ube yimpazamo kwaye ungenakuze utshintshwe. Akukho nto imangalisayo koku ukuba iziprofeto ezininzi zeMalika ezibhalelwe ixesha langoku ziye zabonakala ziyinyani nge-100%. Ngokomzekelo – isiprofeto senkululeko yaseIndiya, isiprofeto sokuzalwa kukaGandhiji, isiprofeto sokusekwa kwelizwe elibizwa ngokuba yiPakistan, isiprofeto sokwakhiwa kwedama laseHirakud eOdisha, njl.njl.

IBhabishya Malika ixoxa ngokweenkcukacha izihloko ezifana nokuphela kweKaliyuga, iKalki Avatar, intshabalalo yabangendawo naboni, ukukhuselwa kwabakhonzi, ukusekwa kweDharma, ukufika kweAnanta Yuga, njl.njl. IMalika ithi ubudala beKaliyuga, obabuyi-4,32,000 yeminyaka, buya kuncitshiswa bube yiminyaka engama-5,000 kuphela ngenxa yezenzo ezahlukeneyo zesono ezenziwe eKaliyuga. Emva kokuba kudlule iminyaka engama-5,000, iNkosi uJagannath iya kushiya iNilachala Dham yaKhe, iPuri, kwaye izalwe emzimbeni womntu eBiraja Kshetra, eJajpur yaseOdisha, kwaye emva koko ihlale eKhandagiri, eBhubaneswar. NgokweMalika, oku kuya kuba yiKalki Avatar kaThixo.

IMalika ithi ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, abakhonzi baya kufumana iimpawu ezininzi ezinjalo kwiPuri Jagannath temple nakwezinye iimonasteri neetempile zaseIndiya, xa bezibona baya kuqonda ukuba iKaliyuga iphelile kwaye iNkosi uKalki izelwe. Ngokomzekelo, ukuphaphazela kwebhanana ephambili kwiJagannath temple, ukuwa okuphindaphindwe kwamatye etempileni, iNilachakra ijika ibe ncam, ukuhlala rhoqo kwevulture phezu kwetempile, njl.njl. Kuyaziwa ukuba zonke ezi mpawu ziye zabonwa ngabakhonzi kwiminyaka embalwa edlulileyo.

IMalika ikhomba ekuzalweni kweNkosi uKalki ngonyaka othile phakathi kuka-2005-2008. NgokweMalika, injongo yeKalki Avatar kukutshabalalisa abangendawo, ukukhusela abakhonzi, nokuseka kwakhona iDharma emhlabeni. IMalika ithi le Yuga-parivartana iza kuqala ngo-2026-2027 ngexesha leMeena-Shani yoga yiNkosi uKalki kwaye iphele ngokupheleleyo ngo-2031-2032. Okwangoku, yonke imisebenzi emikhulu etshabalalisayo iza kwenzeka ngonyaka ka-2029.

IMalika ikwatsho ukuba ngexesha apho iNkosi iya kuseka iDharma emhlabeni, igama loKumkani wasePuri liya kuba nguShri Dibyasingha Deb, okuthetha ukuba zonke iintsomi ezitshabalalisayo zeNkosi ziya kwenzeka ngexesha lolawulo loKumkani wasePuri uDibyasingha Deb. NgokweMalika, ezi ntsomi zitshabalalisayo ziya kuba ikakhulu ngokuqhekeka kweZinto Ezintlanu (Panchabhuta) kunye neMfazwe Yehlabathi.

IMalika ithi emva kokuba yonke imisebenzi etshabalalisayo iphele ngokupheleleyo ngonyaka ka-2031-2032, iAdi-Satyayuga okanye iAnantayuga iya kuqala ngonyaka ka-2033.
